# TMNT (MAME set 'tmnt', World 4 Players, version X) - 68000 program symbols
#
# Addresses and names only: no game data lives in this file.
# Format:  ADDRESS  KIND  NAME  [LAYOUT]   [# comment]
#   KIND: func = function entry, code = code label, data = data label
# Addresses are the 68000's, 000000-05FFFF (the program has no mirrors).
#
# LAYOUT, for data tables that hold code addresses the analysis cannot see the code load
# (tools/core/m68000/analysis.py reads record=, code= and count=):
#   record=N        the table is an array of N-byte records
#   count=N         ... of N records
#   code=A          every record holds a 32-bit code address at offset A
# Sizes, offsets and counts are decimal.
#
# The analysis starts at the exception vectors (the reset at 001000, the VBLANK's IRQ5 at 003DC4) and finds the
# code without traces, following what the address registers hold into the tables of handlers; the lines here
# are what it cannot work out, each with how it was found (kref --jumps: where a jump through a register went).

# ======== tables of records with a code address ========

# four records of three work RAM addresses and a handler, which the loop at 004722 walks from the last one:
# 00475E calls the handler (kref --jumps: 00475E -> 0048B2, 0048E6, 00491A, 00494E)
0047C8  data  channel_records  record=16 code=12 count=4

# nozzle_row_run's routines by nozzle_status, 0-2 (0109F8 "lea $10a0e.l, A6", 010A06 "movea.l (A6,D7.w), A6"): three,
# the code of the first follows them (010A1A "bsr $1831e", whose 6100 7902 read as a fourth entry made 007902 a function)
010A0E  data  nozzle_row_routines  record=4 count=3 code=0

# ======== the C's files ========
# ADDRESS file PATH  # description: the functions from ADDRESS up to the next file line go to src/PATH.c (a file may
# take several stretches)
001000  file  system/boot         # the reset, the RAM and ROM tests, and the main loop that runs the jobs the frames queue

# ======== the boot ========
# the ROM test: sums every other byte of each area of the table at 0016CC into the work RAM from 060018, a word an
# area, writing the watchdog after each block; it keeps its return address in A6 (the boot has no stack yet: it
# tests the RAM). The board puts its own in its place (runtime/replaced.txt): the game has no ROM chips to sum
001850  func  sum_program_chips

# ======== the sprite engine ========
# An object (0x50 bytes in work RAM, 062000 on) shows an animation: +36 holds where its frame is, +33 how many
# more frames to show it. tools/data_walk.py walks the animations, their sprite lists and boxes from what the
# code hands set_animation (its A6), as these functions read them.

# A6 -> +36 of the object in A0, and the frame's count (+ the object's +43) -> +33; every animation starts here
047BF2  func  set_animation
# steps the object in A0 on to its animation's next frame when +33 runs out, without drawing it
0471AE  func  step_animation
# every object of the draw list (061400) into the K051960's sprite RAM (140400): its frame's sprites, its
# shadow sprite (+3A, when +1 has bit 7), and the animation stepped on
046F92  func  draw_sprites
# 8 sizes of 8 bytes, by the top 3 bits of a sprite's code: the bounds draw_sprites keeps a sprite of that
# size on screen by (046FDC "lea $4716e.l, A6", 046FF4/047004/04701C read them)
04716E  data  sprite_sizes
# 4-byte records (half the width, the height) at the offset a frame gives (+6): the collision code reads them
# (047268 "lea $53756.l, A3"; 047534 and 047662 the same)
053756  data  body_boxes

# ======== the layers' maps ========
# A layer (the K052109's tile layers) is drawn from a map: rows of 16-bit offsets into a table of blocks, a block
# 4 x 4 tiles (17 bytes: a colour byte, then the 16 tile codes; 33 with a floor: the colour, 16 bytes of the floor
# map at 061800, the tiles). tools/data_walk.py walks them from area_layers and from the maps the code stores.

# 10 records of 36 bytes, by the area (060121): layer A's x and y (32-bit), layer B's, A's map and blocks, B's
# map and blocks, A's map width in bytes and B's (01E258-01E2B6 copy them into 063340 and 063380)
01E5A0  data  area_layers  record=36 count=10 data=16,20,24,28

# ======== the palettes ========
# D7 = kind << 8 | the banks' mask, A6 = the data: queued at 060B00 (6 bytes a request), and done by 009272
# a frame later (the kinds' handlers, by 009310)
009242  func  post_palette_request
# 16 colours from each palette of the list in A6 (D7 + 1 of them) into the palette RAM at A5
0095E6  func  copy_palette_list
# the palette lists' counts less one, a byte a bank (0095E6's D7: 00938A "move.b (A5,D6.w), D7")
0095D2  data  palette_bank_counts
# where each bank starts in the palette RAM (0093A8 "movea.l (A5,D6.w), A5" after "movea.l #$95d6, A5")
0095D6  data  palette_bank_addresses  record=4 count=4 data=0
# a palette set (4 lists, a bank each) by the area: request kind 1 (00935A "movea.l #$9f56, A6", then the area)
009F56  data  area_palettes
# A5 = palette sets one after the other, D6's high byte = the banks: kept at 063414 and 063418 (02F8F4 "lea $a0ae.l,
# A5", D6 = $0C02); over the next frames (063411 the step) the palette RAM is read into 063800, moved a step toward
# the sets and written back: a fade, not a copy (tools/data_walk.py looks the function up by this name)
00961C  func  copy_palette_sets
comment copy_palette_sets the palettes faded toward a list of palette sets, over the next frames

047BAC  func  take_free_object

# ======== the tile blocks ========

04CBB4  data  object_tile_block_offsets

04CBC0  data  object_tile_blocks  record=10 count=6 data=4

04CD44  data  object_tile_block_pairs  record=12 count=5 data=4,8

# ======== the game's structures ========
# struct and field lines (tools/core/disassemble.py load_names): a structure's fields by their offset (hexadecimal)
# and type; a struct name after the type says the field points at one (u32 its address; u16 the low half of a work
# RAM address, which the code puts in the work RAM's page). The work RAM and the program's data run the other way
# round in the host's memory, so ram.h declares the fields from the structure's end (decompiler/pointers.py,
# REVERSED). A field nothing names here is unk_XX in the C, at the width the code uses it most.

# An object: 0x50 bytes, 50 of them from 062000 (the profile's STRUCT_ARRAYS: 046F08 walks 0x32 of them 0x50 apart);
# the first four are the turtles, the object loop (0477EA) runs the rest from 062140
struct object 50
# bit 7 drawn (046F22 "tst.b ($0,A0)" bmi: into the draw list), bit 6 its sprites flipped across (draw_sprites 046FEC
# "lsl.b #1" then bpl: a turtle faces left, an enemy right, 0485D6-0485E6), bit 4 it stands on another object (the
# collision check 0475F2 "ori.b #$10, ($0,A0)"), bit 3 its attack hit (0472D4 "bset #$3, ($0,A0)"), bit 2 attacking
# and bit 1 can be hit (047224, 04723E: the attack check), bit 0 in use (spawn_enemies 047ABA "move.w #$100, ($0,A2)")
field object 00 flags u8
# what it touches: bit 0 it stands on the floor or on an object, bits 1-4 a side blocked by the floor map (061800)
# or by a solid object (the collision checks 047632 "or.b D1, ($1,A0)"; move_object_blocked tests them), bit 7 in
# the air, which draws its shadow sprite (+3A: draw_sprites 04709E "tst.b ($1,A0)" bpl)
field object 01 contacts u8
# the kind: its low 7 bits pick the kind's routines in the object loop (0477EA: "(+2 & 0x7F) << 2" into the table
# at 053896); spawn_enemies sets it from the spawn record (+6)
field object 02 kind u8
# what the code that starts it gives it: spawn_enemies the spawn record's last byte (047AC0 "move.w ($6,A0),
# ($2,A2)": +6 the kind, +7 this); a foot soldier takes its way in from the high nibble (04DC56 on)
field object 03 parameter u8
# the routine the object loop runs, by the kind's table of routines (0477EA: "+4 << 2")
field object 04 routine u8
# the state the routine's dispatcher (048236) runs, by the table the routine hands it in A6
field object 05 state u8
# the attack it does: its kind in the low 7 bits, bit 7 set when it faces left (0485EC "ori.b #$80, D7" with
# flags bit 6); the attack check gives it to what the attack hits (0472E0 "move.b ($6,A0), ($7,A1)")
field object 06 attack u8
# the attack that hit it (the attacker's attack; the third loop of the attack check turns its bit 7 over,
# 0474FE "bchg #$7, ($7,A1)"): the enemies' hit reactions pick their way by it (048DBA)
field object 07 hit u8
# its health in bars: the attack check takes the attacker's damage off health and, when health runs out, leaves
# health_bars as it is; with none left the hit kills it (0472E6-047314: the player's score, 0472FE "move.w
# #$400, D7"); a knocked-down enemy with no bar left dies (04A1A2 "tst.b ($8,A0)")
field object 08 health_bars u8
field object 09 health u8
# what its attack takes off what it hits (the attack check 0472EA "sub.b ($b,A0), D2", 0472F6 "sub.w ($a,A0), D2")
field object 0A damage u16
# where it is on the screen: draw_sprites adds them to every sprite's x and y (046FF8, 047014)
field object 0C screen_x u16
field object 0E screen_y u16
# its height (8.8): draw_sprites adds its whole part to every sprite's y (04700C "move.b ($10,A0), D2");
# spawn_enemies sets it from the spawn record's height (+4); the jumps add a speed to it
field object 10 height u16
field object 10 height_whole u8
# where it is in the area, 24.8: spawn_enemies sets them to the spawn record's x and y shifted left 8
field object 12 x u32
field object 16 y u32
# its speeds in x, y and height (8.8): what stands on an object whose kind has bit 7 (a platform) moves with it
# (047616 "move.w ($1a,A3), D2", 047620 "move.w ($1c,A3), D2", 04762A "move.w ($1e,A3), D2"); the enemies'
# moves add them (048244 "move.w ($1e,A0), D0", 04824C "move.w ($1a,A0), D0")
field object 1A x_speed s16
field object 1C y_speed s16
field object 1E height_speed s16
# the height of what it stands on: the collision check sets it to the top of the object under it (047600
# "move.b D0, ($32,A0)", with flags bit 4) and clears it when it is off (04754A); 0 the ground
field object 32 floor_height u8
# half its depth in the screen's y: the attack check adds the victim's to the attack's reach in y (047258
# "add.w ($34,A1), D2"), the collision check both objects' (047576); the draw list sorts by screen_y plus it
# (046F30 "move.w ($34,A0), D0")
field object 34 depth u16
# the frames left on the animation's frame (+36): set_animation sets it to the frame's time plus +43, the sprite
# engine steps on when it runs out (0471B6 "subq.b #1, ($33,A0)")
field object 33 frame_time u8
# the animation's frame it shows (set_animation 047BFC "move.l A6, ($36,A0)"; draw_sprites draws its sprites)
field object 36 frame u32 animation_frame
# its shadow's sprite (draw_sprites 0470A6 "movea.l ($3a,A0), A4")
field object 3A shadow u32 sprite
# the next object drawn in the same row of the draw list (046F08 links them; draw_sprites 047154 "move.w ($3e,A0),
# D6" into the work RAM's page)
field object 3E draw_next u16 object
# the routine and state it was in when an attack hit it (the attack check 0472C8 "move.w ($4,A1), ($40,A1)",
# then routine 4); the enemies' hit reaction looks at it (048DD4 "cmpi.b #$3, ($40,A0)")
field object 40 hit_routine u16
# its state byte (Shredder's 052BE2 "cmpi.b #$4, ($41,A0)")
field object 41 hit_state u8
# the colour of its sprites (draw_sprites 047058: negative, each sprite's own; else this one with the sprite's bits
# 0x90); spawn_enemies sets it to FF
field object 42 colour u8
# frames added to every frame's time (set_animation "add.b ($43,A0), D1")
field object 43 animation_delay u8
# the object that hit it (the attack check "move.w A0, ($4c,A1)": the attacker's address, in the page)
field object 4C attacker u16 object
# whether a turtle's combo throws it, bit 7 (01BF7A "tst.b ($4b,A6)" on the object it grabs): every object the engine
# starts has it cleared (spawn_enemies 047AD4 "clr.b ($4b,A2)", start_object_from_record 047C50 "clr.b ($4b,A6)",
# start_shot 047C7E "clr.b ($4b,A6)"), the enemies' starts set it (048A16 "move.b #$84, ($4b,A0)"); the sewer's end
# counts the objects that have it (check_cleared_sewer 01A9AA "tst.b ($4b,A0)")
field object 4B throw u8
# the object its attack hit last (the attack check's second and third loops 0473E6, 0474DC "move.w A1, ($4e,A0)")
field object 4E hit_object u16 object

# An animation's frame (data_walk.py: animations): 10 bytes, 18 with an attack box (attack_frame). After the first
# frame, a frame whose time is 0 ends the animation: last_sprite 0 stays on the frame before, else sprites is the
# frame it goes on from (step_animation 0471CE-0471E0)
struct animation_frame 0A rom
# the frames it is shown (0 at the animation's end); its sprites less one (draw_sprites 046FCC: the dbra's count)
field animation_frame 00 time u8
field animation_frame 01 last_sprite u8
# its sprite list (draw_sprites 046FD0 "movea.l ($2,A3), A4")
field animation_frame 02 sprites u32 sprite
# its body box: the offset into body_boxes (the attack check 047264 "move.w ($6,A3), D2", then 04726E)
field animation_frame 06 body_box u16
# its attack box's depth: negative, none (the frame is 10 bytes); else the box's 8 more bytes follow (step_animation
# 0471C2 "tst.w (-$2,A3)" past the 10 bytes, 0471CA past the 8 more)
field animation_frame 08 attack_depth s16
# a frame with its attack box, as the attack check reads it (047204: the attacker's frame)
struct attack_frame 12 rom
field attack_frame 00 time u8
field attack_frame 01 last_sprite u8
field attack_frame 02 sprites u32 sprite
field attack_frame 06 body_box u16
# how far up or down from the attacker it reaches (047254 "add.w ($8,A2), D2" to the screen y's difference)
field attack_frame 08 attack_depth s16
# its x from the attacker (047270, negated when it faces left), half its width (04728C), its height above the
# attacker's (04729C) and its height (0472AA)
field attack_frame 0A attack_x s16
field attack_frame 0C attack_y s16
field attack_frame 0E attack_width s16
field attack_frame 10 attack_height s16

# A sprite of a frame's sprite list (draw_sprites 046FD4-047096, into the K051960's sprite RAM): its size (top 3
# bits) and the high bits of its code, the code's low byte, its colour, its flips (bit 7 across, bit 6 down), its x
# and y from the object's place on the screen
struct sprite 08 rom
field sprite 00 size_code u8
field sprite 01 code u8
field sprite 02 colour u8
field sprite 03 flips u8
field sprite 04 x s16
field sprite 06 y s16

# A sprite size's bounds (sprite_sizes, by the size: draw_sprites 046FDC): the x a sprite must be past to be drawn
# (047004), the y it must be under (04701C), its width (046FF4: a flipped sprite's x less it)
struct sprite_size 08 rom
field sprite_size 00 left s16
field sprite_size 02 bottom s16
field sprite_size 04 width s16

# A body box (body_boxes, at the offset a frame gives): half its width and its height (the attack check 04728C,
# 0472AA)
struct body_box 04 rom
field body_box 00 half_width s16
field body_box 02 height s16

# ======== the structures' pointers where the code works them out ========
# step_animation and draw_sprites walk an animation's frames: past an attack box (8 more bytes), and on from the end
# of an animation to the frame it goes on from, the pointer is a frame again
param step_animation a3 frame animation_frame
local step_animation 0471CA a3 frame animation_frame
local step_animation 0471DC a3 frame animation_frame
local draw_sprites 04712A a3 frame animation_frame
local draw_sprites 04713C a3 frame animation_frame
# draw_sprites: the bounds of the sprite's size (sprite_sizes plus the size times 8)
local draw_sprites 046FE2 a6 size sprite_size
# the attack check reads the attacker's frame with its attack box (it checks attack_depth first)
local check_attacks 04722E a2 attack attack_frame
local check_attacks 047336 a2 attack attack_frame
local check_attacks 047444 a2 attack attack_frame
# the body boxes the attack and the collision checks read: body_boxes plus the frame's offset
local check_attacks 04726E a3 box body_box
local check_attacks 04737A a3 box body_box
local check_attacks 047488 a3 box body_box
local turtle_collide_with_objects 04755C a4 box body_box
local turtle_collide_with_objects 047590 a5 other_box body_box
local enemy_collide_with_objects 04768C a4 box body_box
local enemy_collide_with_objects 0476C0 a5 other_box body_box

# ======== the game's variables in work RAM ========
# ADDRESS var NAME TYPE (tools/core/disassemble.py load_names): a variable of the work RAM, by what the code shows

# the game's states, one inside the other: the VBLANK's frame work runs by game_state (003F78, 0040B4, 004124 for 0,
# 1 and 2; with 3 it leaves out 003B5C and 003D1E), game_state 0's steps by game_step (003F7A, 003FD8 on to the
# next), the scenes by scene (00415E), each scene's steps by scene_step and scene_substep (the scenes' switches)
060001  var  game_state     u8
060003  var  game_step      u8
060005  var  scene          u8
060007  var  scene_step     u8
060009  var  scene_substep  u8
# what the program writes to 0A0001 (coin counters, the sound board's IRQ, IRQ5 on): the boot sets bit 5 (00138A)
# and writes it out (001392); the VBLANK handler clears bit 5 to lower the IRQ5
060058  var  board_control  u8
# the frames: the VBLANK adds one (003DF8); the attack check takes every other one by its bit 0 (04720A); a demo
# keeps it in 0600BE (004D1E) and puts it back (004CAE)
0600A4  var  frame_count    u16
# the demo the attract mode plays (start_demo: demo_settings by it)
0600B6  var  demo           u8
# the four players' recorded inputs in a demo (start_demo 004D70-004D88: from demo_inputs), which the demo's
# play reads a pair of bytes at a time
0600AA  var  demo_inputs_1  u32
0600AE  var  demo_inputs_2  u32
0600D2  var  demo_inputs_3  u32
0600D6  var  demo_inputs_4  u32
# the area the game plays (0-9: start_area_spawns reads area_spawn_lists by it; the demos set it from
# demo_settings), and the stage it is drawn as (0062EE: from the table at 00634D by the area), by which the
# areas' layers, tile banks, palettes, fire and scroll segments are read
060120  var  area           u8
060121  var  stage          u8
# the next record of the area's spawn list (start_area_spawns: from area_spawn_lists; spawn_enemies moves it 8 on
# as each enemy comes in)
061200  var  spawn_next     u32
# the draw list's first row with an object and how many objects it has (046F08 builds it, draw_sprites draws it)
061204  var  draw_first     u16
061206  var  draw_count     u8
# the draw list: a row a depth (the object's depth + screen_y + 0x38, even, below 0x180: 046F30-046F46), each the
# low half of the first object on that row (046F16 "lea $61400.l, A1", 046F80 "move.w A0, (A1,D0.w)"), the others
# chained through their draw_next (046F66 "move.w A0, ($3e,A2)"); draw_sprites walks it to its end (046FB2 "cmpa.l
# #$61580, A1") and clears it as it goes
061400  var  draw_list[192]  u16  object
# the scroll spawn_enemies follows: the address of the two speeds it adds (x, y) and of the two bytes whose bit 7
# says their direction (start_area_spawns: from area_spawn_scrolls), and where the scroll is, x and y in 24.8
# (from area_spawn_starts): an enemy comes in when spawn_x reaches its record's x
061210  var  spawn_speeds      u32
061214  var  spawn_directions  u32
061218  var  spawn_x        u32
06121C  var  spawn_y        u32

# ---- the game's steps and their timers ----
# a frame count for the steps of game_state 0 and of the attract mode: startup_wait sets 1 (00401E "move.b D0,
# $60002.l") and startup_end moves to the attract mode when it runs out (00402C "subq.b #1, $60002.l"); the profile's
# pauses (004F1A "move.b #$2, $60002.l") and its text's time (005142 "move.b #$64, $60002.l", 005170 "subq.b #1,
# $60002.l"), BEST 10 PLAYERS' (008FAC "move.b #$ff, $60002.l", 008FB6 "subq.b #1, $60002.l")
060002  var  step_timer  u8
# a frame count for steps that end by moving scene on: MASK ROM CHECK's (0019F0 "move.b #$80, $60004.l", 001A64 "subq.b
# #1, $60004.l", then 001A6E "addq.b #1, $60005.l"), the attract mode's end (005220 "move.b #$1, $60004.l", 005230
# "subq.b #1, $60004.l"), the coin's screen, a count every 64 frames (008A9E "move.b #$a, $60004.l", 008AFA "subq.b #1,
# $60004.l")
060004  var  scene_timer  u8
# the continue's count: 20 at its start (00789A "move.b #$14, $60006.l"), one less each time continue_count_time runs
# out (007922 "subq.b #1, $60006.l") and shown (007928 "move.b $60006.l, D7", 00792E "bsr $27b2"), GAME OVER at 0
# (007910 "tst.b $60006.l"); then GAME OVER's frames (007A3A "move.b #$f0, $60006.l", 007A94 "subq.b #1, $60006.l"),
# which a button cuts short after the first 0x40 (007AD2 "cmpi.b #-$50, $60006.l")
060006  var  continue_count  u8
# the frames to the continue's next count: 0x48 (00791A "move.b #$48, $60008.l", 007906 "subq.b #1, $60008.l"); a
# player without lives who presses a button makes it 1, every 8 frames (00796A "move.b (A6)+, D0", 007974 "tst.w
# ($0,A5)", 00798A "move.b #$1, $60008.l"): the count speeds up
060008  var  continue_count_time  u8
# the step under scene_substep: the staff roll's steps (006ED0 "lea $6ee6.l, A6", 006ED6 "move.b $6000b.l, D7") and the
# ending's GAME OVER's (0070A8 "lea $70be.l, A6", 0070AE "move.b $6000b.l, D7"), moved on by them (006F20 "addq.b #1,
# $6000b.l", 00707E "subq.b #3, $6000b.l" back to the next credit), cleared with the other steps (0069AA "clr.b
# $6000b.l")
06000B  var  scene_subsubstep  u8
# the frames the attract mode's title and opening wait: the banner's 20 (004C02 "move.b #$14, $600a8.l", 004C12
# "subi.b #$1, $600a8.l"), the title's 0xF0 (004C4A "move.b #$f0, $600a8.l"), the opening's 0xB2 (008DCE "move.b #$b2,
# $600a8.l", 008E12 "subi.b #$1, $600a8.l"); in a demo it is also the first player's demo hold (004D90 "move.b #$1,
# $600a8.l"; the table at 0047C8 gives it to update_game_inputs: 00472A "movea.l ($4,A6,D6.w), A4", 00473A "subq.b #1,
# ($0,A4)")
0600A8  var  attract_timer  u8
# frame_count kept while a demo plays: begin_demo keeps it and clears it (004D1E "move.w $600a4.l, $600be.l", 004D28
# "clr.w $600a4.l"), end_demo puts it back (004E16 "move.w $600be.l, $600a4.l")
0600BE  var  saved_frame_count  u16
# the frames a demo has played: 0 at its start (004DB0 "clr.w $600c0.l"), one more a frame (004DCE "addq.w #1,
# $600c0.l"), the demo over at 900 (004DD4 "cmpi.w #$384, $600c0.l", 004DE6 "move.b #$8, $60005.l")
0600C0  var  demo_frames  u16
# a frame count for the game's scenes: the area's title (006560 "move.b #$b4, $600c2.l", 006596 "subq.b #1, $600c2.l"),
# the ending (006CA6 "move.b #$28, $600c2.l"), the staff roll (007034 "move.b #$14, $600c2.l"), the ending's GAME OVER
# (00712E "move.b #$fa, $600c2.l", 00714C "subq.b #1, $600c2.l")
0600C2  var  game_scene_timer  u8

# ---- the job queue, the sound queue ----
# where queue_job puts the next job: written and moved on 2 bytes (004164 "movea.l $60010.l, A6", 00416A "move.w D7,
# (A6)+", 00417C "move.l A6, $60010.l"), back to the queue's start after 0603FF (00416C "cmpa.l #$603ff, A6", 004176
# "movea.l #$60380, A6")
060010  var  job_queue_write  u32
# the next job the main loop runs: it waits while the word there is negative (0013C8 "movea.l $60014.l, A0", 0013CE
# "move.w (A0), D1", 0013D0 "bmi $13c2"), marks it done and moves on (00139E "move.w #$ffff, (A0)+", 0013B0 "move.l A0,
# $60014.l")
060014  var  job_queue_read  u32
# the jobs, job << 8 | parameter (0013D4 "move.b D1, D7", 0013D6 "andi.w #$3f00, D1"), FFFF a free word: all FFFF at
# the boot (00120E "move.l #$ffffffff, D0", 001218 "move.l D0, (A0)+" from 0011FC "lea $60380.l, A0")
060380  var  job_queue[64]  u16
# the jobs the scene waits for: one more when it queues one (003FDE "move.b #$1, $60082.l", 004A14 "addq.b #1,
# $60082.l"), one less when the job is done (001E74 "subq.b #1, $60082.l" clear_layers_job, 001A90 sum_mask_roms), and
# the steps wait for 0 (004008 "tst.b $60082.l")
060082  var  jobs_pending  u8
# where queue_sound puts the next sound command (0091D4 "movea.l $60018.l, A6", 0091DA "move.b D7, (A6)+", 0091EC
# "move.l A6, $60018.l"), back to the start after 06043F (0091DC "cmpa.l #$6043f, A6", 0091E6 "lea $60400.l, A6")
060018  var  sound_queue_write  u32
# the next command the VBLANK sends to the sound board, one a frame, while it is behind sound_queue_write (009202
# "movea.l $6001c.l, A6", 009208 "cmpa.l $60018.l, A6", 009212 "move.b (A6)+, D7", 00922A "move.b D7, $a0009.l")
06001C  var  sound_queue_read  u32
# the sound commands queued (0091DA "move.b D7, (A6)+", 009212 "move.b (A6)+, D7"; 060400-06043F)
060400  var  sound_queue[64]  u8

# ---- the DIP switches and what the boot takes from them ----
# DSW1 (0A0011) as read at the boot, the bits turned on (001224 "move.b $a0011.l, D0", 00122A "not.b D0", 00122C
# "move.b D0, $60030.l"): the coinage, low nibble (00125A "andi.b #$f, D1") into 060303/060304, high nibble (001284
# "andi.b #$f0, D2") into 06030B/06030C
060030  var  dip_switches_1  u8
# DSW2 (001232 "move.b $a0013.l, D0", 00123A "move.b D0, $60031.l"): the lives a credit (0012A8 "andi.b #$3, D0"),
# the difficulty (001306 "andi.b #$60, D0"; again at a new game: 00622E "move.b $60031.l, D0"), the demo sounds
# (001318 "andi.b #$80, D0")
060031  var  dip_switches_2  u8
# DSW3 (001240 "move.b $a0019.l, D0", 001248 "move.b D0, $60032.l"): the screen's flip (004086 "btst #$0, $60032.l"),
# the test switch (003FCC "btst #$2, $60032.l"; queue_sound queues every sound with it: 0091A4 "btst #$2, $60032.l")
060032  var  dip_switches_3  u8
# the lives a credit gives: 1, 2, 3 or 5 from the table at 001426 by DSW2's low bits (0012AC "lea $1426.l, A0",
# 0012B2 "move.b (A0,D0.w), $60090.l"), added in BCD to the player's lives (003D40 "move.b $60090.l, D5", 003D4E "abcd
# D5, D4")
060090  var  lives_per_credit  u8
# free play: the boot would set it with DSW1's coinage at 0xF (00125E "cmpi.b #$f, D1"), but the instruction that
# would is a nop (001264 "nop"), so it stays 0 (001266 "move.b D0, $60091.l"); with it a coin switch starts the attract
# mode at once (00404A "tst.b $60091.l") and the coin's screen counts it as credits (0089B6 "or.b $60091.l, D0")
060091  var  free_play  u8
# the demo sounds (DSW2 bit 7, 0x80 when on: 001318 "andi.b #$80, D0", 00131C "move.b D0, $60092.l"): out of a game
# and the test, a sound is queued only with it (0091CA "tst.b $60092.l"), and the areas' music never (0091BA "cmpi.b
# #$60, D7", 0091C2 "cmpi.b #$6b, D7")
060092  var  demo_sounds  u8
# the difficulty of the DIP switches, 0 easy to 3 the hardest (001306 "andi.b #$60, D0", 00130A "lsr.b #5, D0",
# 00130C "move.b D0, $60094.l"; 0 in a demo: 004D12 "clr.b $60094.l"): the base of difficulty (006A58 "move.b $60094.l,
# $60130.l"), and the helicopters', drones' and Stockman's waits (013142 "move.b $60094.l, D7")
060094  var  difficulty_setting  u8
# the program's version: 0 from the boot (0012CE "move.b #$0, $6009f.l"); the title's logo animations by it (004AE0
# "move.b $6009f.l, D7", 004AE6 "lsl.w #3, D7"), bit 0 the copyright texts with HERO instead of NINJA (004C5E "btst
# #$0, $6009f.l", 004C6A "lea $4c7c.l, A5"), bit 1 the banner's place (004BE0 "btst #$1, $6009f.l")
06009F  var  version  u8

# ---- the inputs ----
# the four players' controls this frame, the bits turned on (003EBA "move.b $a0003.l, D0", 003EC2 "move.b D0,
# $60034.l"; P2 0A0005, P3 0A0007, P4 0A0015), read by player (check_starts: 0041AA "lea $60034.l, A5", 0041BA

# ---- the scroll ----
# the area's scroll segment, 4 a segment (an offset into the stage's list of area_scroll_segments): start_scroll_segment
# reads it (01A718 "move.w $633c0.l, D0", 01A71E "movea.l (A6,D0.w), A6"), the next when the layer passes the segment's
# end (01A6EE "cmp.l ($2,A0), D0", 01A6FE "addq.w #4, $633c0.l"); area 2's diagonal stretch is segment 1 (01A3DC
# "cmpi.w #$4, $633c0.l")
0633C0  var  scroll_segment  u16
# the scroll held on each side: the segment's four bytes (01A7C4 "move.l ($6,A6), $633c2.l"), all four when the area's
# end comes (01A8BE "moveq #-$1, D0", 01A8C0 "move.l D0, $633c2.l")
0633C2  var  scroll_held  u32
# held to the left: a turtle at the left edge does not push it (01ADFA "tst.b $633c2.l", 01AE00 "bne $1aef4")
0633C2  var  scroll_held_left  u8
# held to the right (01AFE0 "tst.b $633c3.l"): the drill's arrival (030438 "move.b #$ff, $633c3.l", 030870 "clr.b
# $633c3.l"), the lift (01F4C8 "move.b #$ff, $633c3.l"), too many enemies (02F6EC "move.b D0, $633c3.l")
0633C3  var  scroll_held_right  u8
# held up (01B1B6 "tst.b $633c4.l", 01B1BC "bne $1b2c6")
0633C4  var  scroll_held_up  u8
# held down (01B3A4 "tst.b $633c5.l"; the street's too many enemies: 02F6E6 "move.b D0, $633c5.l")
0633C5  var  scroll_held_down  u8
# the segment's kind, its first byte (01A722 "move.b ($0,A6), $633c9.l"): 0 the area's last, the end check from then on
# (01A72A "beq $1a876"); positive, the turtles push the scroll within the limits (01A72E "bpl $1a7c4"); negative, it
# moves by itself at the segment's speeds (auto_scroll: 01ACDA "tst.b $633c9.l", 01ACE0 "bmi $1ace6"); the skateboard
# ride and the lift set it so (01AA56 "move.b D0, $633c9.l", 01F502 "move.b #$ff, $633c9.l")
0633C9  var  scroll_mode  s8
# the tile RAM of the layer the working layer's new rows and columns go into, layer A's or B's (01F228 "move.l
# #$102000, $633cc.l", 01ED6E "move.l #$104000, $633cc.l"; 01E9F8 "add.l $633cc.l, D0")
0633CC  var  working_layer_tiles  u32 io
# the times the layer went round a stretch: the skateboard road's rounds, 20 a stretch (01FCB4 "cmpi.b #$14, $633d0.l",
# 01FCD6 "addq.b #1, $633d0.l"); the Technodrome's lift shaft, gone round once and then 80, the ride slowing (01F606
# "move.b $633d0.l, D0", 01F628 "addq.b #1, $633d0.l", 01F632 "move.b #$80, $633d0.l", 01F556 "move.b $633d0.l, D0",
# 01F55C "bmi $1f58c")
0633D0  var  layer_loops  s8
# the stage's layers' step: the Technodrome's (to the lift, the ride, off it: 01F422 "move.b $633d2.l, D0", 01F54E
# "addq.b #1, $633d2.l"), the highway's cars (01F814 "move.b $633d2.l, D0", 01F864 "addq.b #1, $633d2.l"), the
# skateboard road's stretches (01FC4C "move.b $633d2.l, D0")
0633D2  var  layers_step  u8
# the area's last segment reached: the stage's end check runs every frame (01A680 "tst.b $633ec.l", 01A68A "jsr
# $1a876.l"), which sets it (01A8C6 "move.b D0, $633ec.l"); the hurry count stops meanwhile (01EC0E "or.b $633ec.l, D0")
0633EC  var  last_segment  u8
# the burning building's scroll to the right held while it is set too (02F6D6 "tst.b $633f5.l", 02F6DC "beq $2f6ec",
# 02F6EC "move.b D0, $633c3.l"); nothing in the program writes it
0633F5  var  building_scroll_hold  u8
# the frames the layers stand still, not at the area's end, with fewer than 3 enemies (01EC18 "addq.w #1, $633f6.l",
# 01EC1E "cmpi.b #$3, $6120c.l"): from 0x100 the HURRY sign (01EC2A "tst.b $633f6.l", 01EC34 "lea $62f50.l, A0"); with
# enemies about it waits at 0x100 (01EC80 "move.w #$100, $633f6.l"), with none at 0x600 the missiles fall (01EC8A
# "cmpi.w #$600, $633f6.l", 01EC96 "jsr $13354.l"); cleared while the drill arrives (03042A "clr.w $633f6.l")
0633F6  var  hurry_time  u16
# the floor map walled off at the area's far end, once (01ED88 "tst.b $633fd.l", 01EDAC "moveq #-$2, D3", 01EDC4 "subq.b
# #1, $633fd.l"; the sewer and the lift's end too: 01F1C4 "subq.b #1, $633fd.l", 01F78A "subq.b #1, $633fd.l")
0633FD  var  floor_end_walled  u8

# ---- the area's end ----
# the stage's check has found the area beaten (01A900 "move.b #$ff, $633c8.l", 01A97C "move.b #$ff, $633c8.l"): the
# turtles start no attack or jump (01B952 "tst.b $633c8.l", 01B958 "bne $1b976"), and once none is busy area_cleared is
# set (01A690 "tst.b $633c8.l", 01A69A "tst.b $633ca.l", 01A6A4 "subq.b #1, $600bd.l"); the exits clear it at their end
# (02CD8E "clr.b $633c8.l")
0633C8  var  area_beaten  u8
# the turtles in a jump or an attack this frame, counted down from 0 (01A418 "move.b D0, $633ca.l", 01B7A0 "subq.b #1,
# $633ca.l", 01BD50 "subq.b #1, $633ca.l"): the area is not over while one is (01A69A "tst.b $633ca.l")
0633CA  var  turtles_busy  u8
# area 0's end: 0x60 frames once every player is ready and no enemy is left (01A8EC "move.b #$60, $633ed.l", 01A8F6
# "subq.b #1, $633ed.l"), sounds at 0x40 (01A90A "cmpi.b #$40, $633ed.l", 01A914 "move.b #$ba, D7"), then area_beaten
0633ED  var  building_clear_wait  u8

# ---- the floor map's cells around an object (read_floor_cells_at) ----
# The floor map (061800, 0x40 cells a row of 8 pixels) read at the object's x, at its floor level (y + floor_height:
# 01DE8C "add.b ($32,A0), D6") and at its height (y + height: 01DE92 "add.b D4, D5"). Codes: 01 floor, FF wall, FE and
# FD walls on one side, FA-FC stairs (01D9AE "cmpi.b #-$6, $633d3.l").
# the cell at its floor level (01DEC0 "move.b (A6), $633d3.l"; 0 when y is negative: 01E048 "clr.b $633d3.l"); the
# enemies follow its stairs (048914 "move.b $633d3.l, D0")
0633D3  var  floor_cell  u8
# the one above it (01DED6 "move.b (-$40,A6), $633d4.l"; row 0 takes row 31's: 01DEE2 "move.b ($7c0,A6), $633d4.l")
0633D4  var  floor_cell_up  u8
# right of it (01DEF8 "move.b ($1,A6), $633d5.l"); a wall there stops a turtle (01ABB8 "tst.b $633d5.l")
0633D5  var  floor_cell_right  u8
# left of it (01DF16 "move.b (-$1,A6), $633d6.l")
0633D6  var  floor_cell_left  u8
# above and right (01DF4C "move.b (-$3f,A4), $633d7.l"; the turtles' corners: 01DC64 "move.b $633d7.l, D1")
0633D7  var  floor_cell_up_right  u8
# above and left (01DF72 "move.b (-$41,A4), $633d8.l"; 01DC86 "move.b $633d8.l, D1")
0633D8  var  floor_cell_up_left  u8
# at its height: the one above (01DF9C "move.b (-$40,A5), $633d9.l"; 01D742 "move.b $633d9.l, D2")
0633D9  var  height_cell_up  u8
# right (01DFBE "move.b ($1,A5), $633da.l"; 01DAC2 "cmpi.b #-$5, $633da.l")
0633DA  var  height_cell_right  u8
# left (01DFDC "move.b (-$1,A5), $633db.l"; 01D98E "cmpi.b #-$6, $633db.l")
0633DB  var  height_cell_left  u8
# above and right (01E012 "move.b (-$3f,A4), $633dc.l"; 01DC5E "move.b $633dc.l, D2")
0633DC  var  height_cell_up_right  u8
# above and left (01E038 "move.b (-$41,A4), $633dd.l"; 01DC80 "move.b $633dd.l, D2")
0633DD  var  height_cell_up_left  u8
# the cell at its height itself (01DEC6 "move.b (A5), $633f4.l")
0633F4  var  height_cell  u8

0633C6  var  apartment_fire_spot  u8

0633F3  var  building_fire_frame  u8

# ---- the floor map ----
# the floor map: 32 rows of 0x40 cells (one an 8-pixel column) that the layers' blocks fill and the objects read
# (01DEB4 "lea $61800.l, A6"); the rows wrap, the first's row up is the last and the last's row down is the first
# (01DF2C "cmpa.l #$61840, A6", 01DF36 "adda.w #$800, A4", 01DF7C "cmpa.l #$61fc0, A6", 01DF86 "suba.w #$800, A4";
# 048966 "cmpa.l #$62000, A6", 048970 "suba.w #$800, A6"). The C writes its addresses as numbers today (0x61800u,
# 0x61840u, 0x61C30u...). 061830 is its cell 0x30 of row 0, where the Technodrome's lift walls the shaft, 16 columns
# down 0x20 rows (01F66A "lea $61830.l, A0", 01F67E "move.b D3, (A1)", 01F680 "adda.w #$40, A1"), and which the lift's
# end tests before walling it again (01F716 "lea $61830.l, A0", 01F71C "cmpi.b #-$1, (A0)"): floor_map(0x30)
061800  var  floor_map[2048]  u8
# the cell below the object's at its floor level (the row after: 01DF8A "move.b ($40,A4), $61118.l"; the last row's
# is the first's: 01DF7C "cmpa.l #$61fc0, A6", 01DF86 "suba.w #$800, A4"), the counterpart of floor_cell_up; a wall

# ---- the ranking ----
# a name was entered (007F78 "move.b #$1, $634a0.l"): then BEST 10 PLAYERS follows (007FDE "tst.b $634a0.l"); cleared
# at the ranking's start (007C00 "clr.b $634a0.l")
0634A0  var  name_entered  u8
# the ranking's 99 scores, BCD, and at the end the one being put in (007C16 "lea $63500.l, A6", 007C3C "move.w #$c4, D6",
# 007C4E "cmp.w D0, D1", 007C54 "move.w ($2,A6,D6.w), (A6,D6.w)"); its defaults at the reset (0082B8 "lea $63500.l,
# A6"); BEST 10 PLAYERS (0090C4 "lea $63500.l, A5"). The test mode's MASK ROM CHECK keeps its six sums in the first 12
# bytes (0019BC "lea $63500.l, A0", 001AC8 "move.w D7, ($0,A0)"); the next reset puts the defaults back.
063500  var  ranking_scores[100]  u16
# the place a player's score goes in, below the 99th (007C40 "move.w ($4,A3), ($2,A6,D6.w)" with D6 0xC4): named on its
# own because the C writes it by its address (ranking.c: unk_0635C6 = PLAYER(player)->score)
0635C6  var  ranking_new_score  u16
# the top ten's initials, 4 bytes each (three letters and 0), and the new one's after them (007C1C "lea $635c8.l, A5",
# 007C8C "move.l (A5,D2.w), ($4,A5,D2.w)"); the letter a player enters (007E62 "lea $635c8.l, A4", 007F00 "move.b
# (A4,D5.w), D4", 007F1A "move.b D4, (A4,D5.w)"); BEST 10 PLAYERS (009048 "lea $635c8.l, A5")
0635C8  var  ranking_initials[44]  u8
# the new score's initials, "AAA" to start (007C34 "move.l #$41414100, ($28,A5)"); named on its own for the same reason
0635F0  var  ranking_new_initials  u32
# each player's place, BCD, 0 none (007C22 "lea $635f4.l, A4", 007C66 "sbcd D1, D0", 007C68 "move.b D0, (A4,D7.w)"; 007BD0
# "move.l D0, $635f4.l"); a player in the top ten enters a name (007D4A "cmpi.b #$11, (A6,D7.w)")
0635F4  var  ranking_places[4]  u8
# the ranking's waits: 0x1E0 frames to look (007D7C "move.w #$1e0, $635f8.l"), 0x5DC for the names (007DE6 "move.w
# #$5dc, $635f8.l", 007E30 "subq.w #1, $635f8.l"), 0x78 and 0x3C after (007F80 "move.w #$78, $635f8.l", 007FB6 "move.w
# #$3c, $635f8.l"). Also the sound test's wait (005C00 "move.w #$12c, $635f8.l") and BEST 10 PLAYERS' sprite x
# (008FF0 "addi.w #$4, $635f8.l")
0635F8  var  ranking_time  u16
# before that its high byte: the turtles still to draw in black, a bit a player (007CEA "move.b #$4f, $635f8.l", 007D1A
# "bclr D0, $635f8.l", 007D72 "move.b $635f8.l, D7"); the unused coin counter test's pulse time (005A5E "subq.b #1,
# $635f8.l", 005B22 "move.b #$20, $635f8.l")
0635F8  var  turtles_in_black  u8
# the initials each player has entered, 3 done (007DB8 "clr.b (A5,D7.w)" with 007D9C "lea $635fa.l, A5"; 007ECE "addq.b
# #1, (A3,D7.w)" with 007E5C "lea $635fa.l, A3"); the title's banner's speed uses its first word (004BB0 "move.w #$7,
# $635fa.l", 004BCC "subq.w #1, $635fa.l") and the test mode its bytes (005A80 "addq.b #1, $635fa.l", 005BF4 "addq.b #1,
# $635fa.l")
0635FA  var  initials_entered[4]  u8
# the players entering a name, a bit each (007DCC "move.b D6, $635fe.l", 007E4A "btst D7, $635fe.l")
0635FE  var  players_entering_names  u8
# the players whose attack or jump was held the last time, a bit each: a new press takes the next initial (007EC2 "bset

0635FF  var  initials_buttons_held  u8

# ---- the test mode's MASK ROM CHECK (in ranking_scores' RAM; a name at its own address wins over the array) ----
# the ROM bank read: the sprites' through the K051960 (001B2E "move.b $63518.l, $6350e.l", 001B46 "move.b $6350e.l, D7",
# 001B4C "move.b D7, $140003.l"), the tiles' banks through the K052109 (001BEA "move.b $6350e.l, D7", 001BF6 "move.b
# $60064.l, $106b00.l", 001CB8 "addi.b #$44, $6350e.l")
06350E  var  mask_rom_bank  u8
# a byte of two bits from each of the four bytes read, added to a sum (001D0E "clr.b $6350f.l", 001D1E "ori.b #$1,
# $6350f.l", 001D8A "move.b $6350f.l, D3", 001D90 "add.w D3, (A6)+")
06350F  var  mask_rom_bits  u8
# the four running sums, two of them a chip's sum (001D08 "lea $63510.l, A6", 001D90 "add.w D3, (A6)+"; 001AC0 "move.w
# ($0,A6), D7", 001AC4 "add.w ($2,A6), D7", 001AC8 "move.w D7, ($0,A0)")
063510  var  mask_rom_part_sums[4]  u16
# the first and the last sprite ROM bank to read (001AA4 "move.b #$0, $63518.l", 001AAC "move.b #$3, $63519.l"; 001AE6
# "move.b #$40, $63518.l", 001BB4 "move.b $63519.l, D7", 001BBA "cmp.b $6350e.l, D7")
063518  var  mask_rom_first_bank  u8
063519  var  mask_rom_last_bank  u8

# ======== tables of the objects' addresses ========
# the objects the attack check takes one a frame, by frame_count & 3 (047204's last loop)
0471F4  table  attack_check_objects  u32 object
# where each group of objects new ones are taken from starts (take_free_object: by bits 4-5 of a spawn record's flags;
# take_free_object_in_group by D0), and how many objects each has, less one (the dbra's count)
047BE2  table  object_groups  u32 object
047BDE  table  object_group_counts  u8
# set_animation takes the animation's first frame in A6 (it goes into the object's frame)
param set_animation a6 animation animation_frame

# ======== the players' slots the enemies go after ========
# 062FA0: four records of 16 bytes, one a turtle (04776E sets their first long to the turtles' objects): an enemy
# keeps the one it goes after (enemy target) and counts itself in it
struct player_slot 10
# the turtle (04776E "move.l #$62000, $62fa0.l"; the enemies read it: 048B4E "movea.l (A1), A6")
field player_slot 00 turtle u32 object
# how many enemies go after it: an enemy adds itself when it takes the slot (048B00 "addq.b #1, ($4,A6)") and takes
# itself off when it leaves (048AB8 "subq.b #1, ($4,A1)"); the enemies pick the slot with fewer (048AC8)
field player_slot 04 enemies u8
# the sides of the turtle taken by an enemy that stands by it: bit 6 one, bit 7 the other (048BB6 "bset #$7,
# ($5,A1)", 048BCA "bset #$6, ($5,A1)"); both taken (C0 and up) and the others keep away (048B76 "cmpi.b #-$40,
# ($5,A1)")
field player_slot 05 sides u8
# frames an enemy holds its turtle: one grabbing it sets 0x60 and keeps it so while it holds (049CD0 "move.b #$60,
# ($8,A1)", 049D06), and no other grabs it meanwhile (049CA6 "tst.b ($8,A1)")
field player_slot 08 grab_time u8

# ---- the players' slots' fields ----
# the sides of the turtle taken by the Rodneys, kept apart from the other enemies' (sides): bit 7 one, bit 6 the other
# (051E64 "bset #$7, ($6,A1)", 051E78 "bset #$6, ($6,A1)"); both taken (C0 and up) and the next Rodney keeps away
# (051E3C "cmpi.b #-$40, ($6,A1)"); given back when it leaves the turtle (051FE8 "and.b D0, ($6,A1)")
field player_slot 06 rodney_sides u8
# the same for the Mousers (0511FC "bset #$7, ($7,A1)", 051210 "bset #$6, ($7,A1)", 0511CA "cmpi.b #-$40, ($7,A1)",
# 051306 "and.b D0, ($7,A1)")
field player_slot 07 mouser_sides u8
# the slot's last four bytes: nothing but the clears reaches them (start_player_slots' 04779C "movem.l D0-D2,
# $62fa4.l", which clears +04 to +0F)
field player_slot 0C unused u32
# +0C: no field. Nothing reads or writes a slot's +0C but the clears (04779C "movem.l D0-D2, $62fa4.l", which clears
# +04 to +0F; clear_objects). The C's PLAYER_SLOT(a1)->unk_0C (states.c 12104, 13414, 13519) is not a slot: there A1 is
# the turtle's object, and +0C/+0E are its screen_x and screen_y (04F972 "add.w ($c,A1), D1", 04F976 "move.w ($e,A1),
# D2"); see "seen on the way".


# Rocksteady's and Bebop's target (+20) is a turtle's object, not a slot: they take it from enemy_nearest_turtles
# (04F3DC "jsr $4835e.l", 04F3E2 "move.w A6, ($20,A0)") in the 062000 page, and enemy_boss_attack_move hands it to their
# moves in A1 (04F5EE "move.l #$62000, D0", 04F5F4 "move.w ($20,A0), D0", 04F5F8 "movea.l D0, A1"), which the dispatcher
# also runs with a slot in A1 for the other enemies' attacks (048D94 "movea.l D0, A1"); their moves read its screen
# place (04F972 "add.w ($c,A1), D1", 04F976 "move.w ($e,A1), D2"; 05072A, 050874) and height (04FDE2 "cmpi.b #$40,
# ($10,A1)")
scoped object enemy_rocksteady 20 target u16 object.turtle
scoped object enemy_bebop 20 target u16 object.turtle
local enemy_rocksteady_think 04F3F0 a1 turtle object.turtle
local enemy_bebop_think 050498 a1 turtle object.turtle
local enemy_boss_attack_move 04F5F8 a1 turtle object.turtle
param enemy_rocksteady_close_in_step a1 turtle object.turtle
param enemy_rocksteady_shoot_start a1 turtle object.turtle
param enemy_bebop_walk_to_front_step a1 turtle object.turtle
param enemy_bebop_close_in_step a1 turtle object.turtle

# ======== the players' records, the coin slots, the palette effects and requests (batch 4) ========
# the profile's STRUCT_ARRAYS lays each run out (the first, how many): PLAYER(PLAYER_ADDRESS(n)) for player n + 1

# the players' records: four of 8 bytes from 060100, one a player (004284 "lea $60100.l, A6", 004294 "lea $60108.l, A6",
# 003D78 "adda.l #$8, A1" in add_credit_lives' loop over the four)
struct player 08
# the lives, BCD, 999 at most: the credits turn into lives (003D46 "move.w ($0,A1), D4", 003D4E "abcd D5, D4", 003D58
# "cmpi.w #$999, D4", 003D64 "move.w D4, ($0,A1)"); draw_lives shows them (0023CC "move.w (A4,D4.w), D4")
field player 00 lives u16
# the lives' two low digits, which use_life takes one from (00430A "move.b ($1,A6), D1", 00430E "sbcd D0, D1"; a borrow
# takes one from the hundreds, 00431C "subq.b #1, ($0,A6)")
field player 01 lives_low u8
# a copy of unused_dip_setting made with the score (004342 "move.w $6009a.l, ($2,A6)"): nothing reads it
field player 02 unused_setting_copy u16
# the score, BCD: the enemies defeated (00433E "clr.w ($4,A6)"; add_point adds to it from its low byte: 001F4E "lea
# $60106.l, A4", 001FA0 "abcd -(A5), -(A4)"); draw_score_player_1 draws it (001FEA "lea $60104.l, A6")
field player 04 score u16
# the health bars the player's turtle has, kept between areas (004328 "move.b #$a, ($6,A6)"; 019C6A "move.b $60106.l,
# $62008.l" into the turtle's health_bars, 01A366 back; draw_health_bar: 002474 "move.b ($6,A4,D4.w), D4")
field player 06 health_bars u8
# the message under the player's box and its state (002692 "move.b #$1, ($7,A6)", 0026EE "move.b #$4, ($7,A6)", 002728
# "addi.b #$20, ($7,A6)")
field player 07 message u8

# the coin slots: four of 8 bytes from 060300, one a slot (003D1E "lea $60300.l, A0", 003D72 "adda.l #$8, A0")
struct coin_slot 08
# coins still to count on the slot's coin counter (003CE8 "addq.b #1, ($0,A0)", 003CD6 "subq.b #1, ($0,A0)")
field coin_slot 00 coins_to_count u8
# coins toward the next credit (003CEC "addq.b #1, ($1,A0)", 003D18 "clr.b ($1,A0)")
field coin_slot 01 coins u8
# the counter's pulse, 60 frames a coin (003CE0 "move.b #$3c, ($2,A0)")
field coin_slot 02 counter_pulse u8
# coins a credit and the credits they give, from the DIP switches (001274 "move.b (A0,D1.w), $60303.l", 00127C "move.b
# ($1,A0,D1.w), $60304.l"): only the first slot's are read, for every slot (003CF4 "cmp.b $60303.l, D7", 003D06 "move.b
# $60304.l, D7"); the second slot's get DSW1's high nibble (001290 "move.b (A0,D2.w), $6030b.l") and nothing reads them
field coin_slot 03 coins_per_credit u8
field coin_slot 04 credits_per_coins u8
# the counter on, into board_control's coin counter bit (003CDC "move.b D1, ($5,A0)", 003BC6 "tst.b ($5,A0)")
field coin_slot 05 counter_on u8
# credits not turned into lives yet (003D0C "add.w ($6,A0), D7", 003D68 "subq.w #1, ($6,A0)")
field coin_slot 06 credits u16

# the palette effects' slots: 64 of 10 bytes from 060480 (009888 "lea $60480.l, A5", 00989C "cmpa.l #$60700, A5")
struct palette_effect 0A
# bit 7 in use (00988E "btst #$7, (A5)"), bit 6 going backwards (0099A4 "bset #$6, ($0,A6)"), bits 0-1 the bank
field palette_effect 00 flags u8
# frames left of the step (00991C "subq.b #1, ($1,A6)")
field palette_effect 01 time u8
# the effect's step in the program's data (0098E0 "move.l A4, ($2,A5)", 009928 "movea.l ($2,A6), A5")
field palette_effect 02 step u32 rom
# the colour it writes in the palette RAM (0098E4 "move.l A3, ($6,A5)", 009950 "movea.l ($6,A6), A4")
field palette_effect 06 colour u32 io

# the palette requests: 40 of 6 bytes from 060B00, done a frame after they are queued (0011DE "lea $60b00.l, A0", 00924C
# "cmpa.l #$60bef, A5")
struct palette_request 06
# the kind << 8 | the banks' mask, 0 a free one (009248 "move.w D7, (A5)+", 0092BE "move.w (A0), D0")
field palette_request 00 kind u16
# its data (00924A "move.l A6, (A5)+", 0092C8 "movea.l ($2,A0), A6")
field palette_request 02 data u32 rom

# ---- struct layer (063300 WORKING_LAYER, 063340 LAYER_A, 063380 LAYER_B) ----
# The K052109's two scrolling layers keep 64 bytes each, layer A at 063340 and layer B at 063380; the code copies one
# into 063300, works on the copy and copies it back (01E708 "lea $63340.l, A3", 01E718 "lea $63300.l, A4", 01E720
# "move.l (A3)+, (A4)+"; 01E728 "lea $63340.l, A4"). Bytes 36-3F of each are never touched by name.
struct layer 40
# its place across, 24.8 (pixels << 8): the K052109's x scroll is it >> 8, 9 bits (01ECAA "move.l $63340.l, D0", 01ECB0
# "lsr.l #8, D0", 01ECB2 "andi.w #$1ff, D0", 01ECB6 "move.w D0, $6005c.l"); the area's start sets A's and B's from
# area_layers (01E26E "move.l (A6,D0.w), $63340.l", 01E27E "move.l ($8,A6,D0.w), $63380.l"); the stages compare layer
# B's with places in the area (01F258 "cmpi.l #$33000, $63380.l")
field layer 00 x u32
# x's 256-pixel page: 8 of the map's 32-pixel blocks, the map's column to draw from (01E122 "move.b $63301.l, D0",
# 01E128 "lsl.b #4, D0": 16 bytes, 8 map entries, a page); the skateboard road loops every 512 pixels (01FCC0 "btst #$1,
# $63301.l")
field layer 01 x_page u8
# its place up, 24.8 like x (01AD6A "add.l D0, $63304.l"; 02BD06 "move.l #$10000, $63344.l")
field layer 04 y u32
# y's 256-pixel page: 8 rows of the map's blocks (01E12A "move.b $63305.l, D1", 01E130 "lsl.w #3, D1", 01E138 "mulu.w
# D2, D1")
field layer 05 y_page u8
# y's pixel within its page: the K052109's y scroll is it negated (01ECCE "move.b $63346.l, D0", 01ECD4 "neg.b D0",
# 01ECD6 "move.b D0, $6005f.l"; layer B's shaken: 0116BC "add.b $63386.l, D7"); the floor map's row is read from it
# (01DE98 "sub.b $63306.l, D6")
field layer 06 y_pixel u8
# x's place within its 8-pixel tile column, times 32: the speed << 5 is added to it (01ACEC "move.w D0, D1", 01ACEE
# "lsl.w #5, D1", 01AD36 "add.w D1, $63308.l") and its carry brings a new column into view (01AD3C "bcc $1ad4c", 01AD40
# "move.l D0, $6332c.l"); set from x (01FCF0 "andi.w #$700, D0", 01FCF4 "lsl.w #5, D0", 01FCF6 "move.w D0, $63308.l")
field layer 08 column_phase u16
# the same for y and the rows (01AD76 "add.w D1, $6330a.l", 01AD7C "bcs $1b520"; 01B520 "move.l D0, $63330.l")
field layer 0A row_phase u16
# how far x moves a frame (no sign: x_direction says which way): auto_scroll adds or subtracts it (01ACE6 "move.w
# $6330c.l, D0", 01AD24 "add.l D0, $63300.l", 01AD04 "sub.l D0, $63300.l"); the segment's speed (01A732 "move.w ($6,A6),
# $6334c.l"), the lift's ride speeds up (01F560 "addq.w #4, $6330c.l", 01F56C "cmpi.w #$400, $6330c.l")
field layer 0C x_speed u16
# the same up and down (01AD4C "move.w $6330e.l, D0", 01AD6A "add.l D0, $63304.l"; 01A73A "move.w ($8,A6), $6334e.l";
# the opening's sweep: 02F9AA "addi.w #$40, $6330e.l")
field layer 0E y_speed u16
# the bytes of one of the map's rows: a row up is this less (01E132 "move.w $63310.l, D2", 01E13A "sub.w $63310.l, D1",
# 01E1AC "move.w $63310.l, D0", 01E1B2 "suba.l D0, A2"); 0x20, 16 blocks, for the pictures (02BE00 "move.w #$20,
# $63310.l"); area_layers gives A's and B's (01E2AE "move.w ($20,A6,D0.w), $63350.l")
field layer 10 map_width u16
# the map: words, each a block's offset in blocks (01E142 "movea.l $63312.l, A2", 01E152 "move.w (A3)+, D0", 01E154
# "movea.l $63316.l, A4", 01E15A "adda.l D0, A4"); the pictures' maps (02BDEC "move.l #$314c4, $63312.l")
field layer 12 map u32 rom
# the blocks: 4 by 4 tiles, the colour then the tile codes (01E15E "move.b ($0,A4), D1", 01E172 "move.b ($1,A4),
# ($1,A0)"; with the floor cells first in some: 01E0C2 "move.b ($11,A4), ($1,A0)"); 02BDF6 "move.l #$315c4, $63316.l"
field layer 16 blocks u32 rom
# which way y moves: 0 not at all, 7F up (y grows), 80 down (auto_scroll: 01AD5C "tst.b $6331a.l", 01AD66 "bmi $1ad82",
# 01AD82 "sub.l D0, $63304.l"); a turtle pushing the scroll up sets 7F (01B322 "move.b #$7f, $6331a.l"), down 80
# (01B536 "move.b #$80, $6331a.l"); the objects move the other way (01A5D8 "tst.b $6331a.l")
field layer 1A y_direction s8
# which way x moves: 0 not at all, 80 right (x grows), 7F left (01ACF6 "tst.b $6331b.l", 01AD00 "bmi $1ad24", 01AD24
# "add.l D0, $63300.l"; 01AF32 "move.b #$7f, $6331b.l", 01B11C "move.b #$80, $6331b.l"); the segment's (01A772
# "move.b ($f,A6), $6335b.l"); layer A's or B's moving stops the hurry count (01EC02 "move.b $6335b.l, D0", 01EC08
# "or.b $6339b.l, D0")
field layer 1B x_direction s8
# the segment's limits (01A7CC "move.l ($a,A6), $6335c.l"), copied to the working layer from the layer the stage
# scrolls (01A822 "move.l $6335c.l, $6331c.l", 01A84C "move.l $6339c.l, $6331c.l"): x no further right than this
# (01B146 "add.l D0, $63300.l", 01B158 "move.l $6331c.l, D2", 01B15E "cmp.l $63300.l, D2")
field layer 1C x_max u32
# y no further up (01B354 "add.l D0, $63304.l", 01B360 "move.l $63320.l, D2", 01B366 "cmp.l D0, D2")
field layer 20 y_max u32
# x no further left (01AF5C "sub.l D0, $63300.l", 01AF70 "cmp.l $63324.l, D2"; held there: 01AED2 "move.l $63324.l,
# $63300.l")
field layer 24 x_min u32
# y no further down (01B568 "sub.l D0, $63304.l", 01B57C "cmp.l $63328.l, D2"; 01B416 "move.l $63328.l, $63304.l")
field layer 28 y_min u32
# the x of the column that has come into view, the right edge's or the left's (01AD30 "addi.l #$15800, D0", 01AD10
# "subi.l #$1800, D0", 01AD40 "move.l D0, $6332c.l"), which draw_new_row_and_column draws (01EAB6 "move.l $6332c.l,
# D0"); layer A follows layer B's in the sewer (01F1F0 "move.l $633ac.l, $6336c.l")
field layer 2C new_column_x u32
# the y of the row that has come into view (01B520 "move.l D0, $63330.l", 01E9CC "move.l $63330.l, D0")
field layer 30 new_row_y u32
# not 0: a row to draw at new_row_y (01B526 "subq.b #1, $63334.l", 01E9C2 "tst.b $63334.l"); cleared every frame with
# new_column (01A406 "move.w D0, $63334.l")
field layer 34 new_row u8
# not 0: a column to draw at new_column_x (01AD46 "subq.b #1, $63335.l", 01EAAA "tst.b $63335.l"; layer A takes layer
# B's: 01F218 "move.b $633b5.l, $63375.l")
field layer 35 new_column u8


# the passes of a text typed several times over in colours (the staff roll's credit name, the profiles' and the
# ending's texts): 8 records of 10 bytes walked down from 063476 (007430 "lea $63476.l, A4", 007446 "suba.l #$a, A4";
# 0185B6 "lea $63476.l, A4", 0185C6 "suba.l #$a, A4"), the lowest at 063430
struct typed_pass 0A
# the tile RAM address of its next character (00750C "movea.l ($0,A4), A5", 00756E "addi.l #$4, ($0,A4)")
field typed_pass 00 place u32 io
# its next character, 0 the pass over (0074B2 "move.l A5, ($4,A4)", 0074F8 "tst.l ($4,A4)", 007508 "movea.l ($4,A4),
# A6")
field typed_pass 04 text u32 rom
# frames to its next character (007442 "move.b D6, ($8,A4)", 007500 "subq.b #1, ($8,A4)")
field typed_pass 08 wait u8
# the frames between characters (00749A "move.b #$4, ($9,A4)", 007576 "move.b ($9,A4), ($8,A4)")
field typed_pass 09 delay u8

# ======== the enemies ========
# The Foot soldiers and the other enemies share one engine (048236-0537xx): routine 0 starts them (by kind), 1 their
# way in (enter), 2 the thinking (think: goes after a turtle and picks a move from its definition), 3 an attack, 4
# the hit (the attack check sets it), 5 the hit's reaction, 6 a step over the floor's edge. Their functions are
# enemy_...; a kind's own enemy_KIND_..., a move's own enemy_MOVE_... when it keeps something else in a field.

# the slot of the turtle it goes after (062FA0 on): the start takes the nearest (0489F8 "move.w A6, ($20,A0)"), the
# thinking another one every 16 frames (048AFA)
scoped object enemy 20 target u16 player_slot
# frames left of its move; at 0 the thinking picks another (048D3E "tst.b ($22,A0)"); a change of approach clears it
# (048B86 "clr.b ($22,A0)")
scoped object enemy 22 move_time u8
# frames until it looks for a turtle again (048AA0 "subq.b #1, ($23,A0)", 048AA8 "move.b #$10, ($23,A0)")
scoped object enemy 23 retarget_time u8
# its kind's record in enemy_definitions (0489EE: 048A2A "move.l #$53efe, ($24,A0)"): the moves by approach (048D46
# "movea.l ($24,A0), A6" + approach * 8), the attacks (048CEA, +28), the reactions to a hit (+58)
scoped object enemy 24 definition u32 rom
# the move it makes, times 4: the thinking's moves (048D72 "move.w D6, ($28,A0)", run through 053C26 at 048D7A), an
# attack (048D2C, through 053D2A at 048DAA), a reaction to a hit, a way in, a step over an edge
scoped object enemy 28 move u16
# how it stands to its turtle: 0 away, 1 by its side (within 0x40: 048BA2 "move.b #$1, ($2a,A0)"), 2 its sides
# are taken (048B80), 3 keep off it while it is down (048C50), 4 out of the way of its attack (048CB2); the thinking
# picks a move from the definition's record of it (048D4C "move.b ($2a,A0), D0" + "lsl.w #3, D0")
scoped object enemy 2A approach u8
# where its turtle is (0482FA: 048336 "move.b D0, ($2b,A0)"): bit 6 to the left, bits 6-7 C0/80 below, bit 5 more
# across than up or down; how far across and up or down (04833A "move.w D1, ($2c,A0)", 04833E "move.w D2,
# ($2e,A0)")
scoped object enemy 2B target_direction u8
scoped object enemy 2C target_dx u16
scoped object enemy 2E target_dy u16
# the direction it last turned to (0486FC "move.b D0, ($30,A0)"; 0484E6 turns again only when it changes)
scoped object enemy 30 facing u8
# the chance in 100 it gets out of the way of its turtle's attack, when the turtle attacks it face to face (048C9E
# "jsr $48448.l" + 048CA4 "cmp.b ($31,A0), D7")
scoped object enemy 31 dodge_chance u8
# bit 7: its turtle faces it (0482FA: 048342 "lsl.b #1, D3", 048358 "move.b D3, ($44,A0)"; the thinking 048C94)
scoped object enemy 44 target_faces u8
# bit 7: the side of the turtle it took is bit 6 of the slot's sides, else bit 7 (048BD2 "bset #$7, ($45,A0)",
# 048BC0; 0486D6 gives it back)
scoped object enemy 45 side u8
# bit 7: a turtle can grab it and throw it, the low bits which throw (01BF7A "tst.b ($4b,A6)", 01C05E "move.b
# ($4b,A5), D3"); a Foot soldier's start sets it (048A16 "move.b #$84, ($4b,A0)")
scoped object enemy 4B throw u8

# #2, D0", "movea.l (A6,D0.w), A6", "jmp (A6)")

# ======== the turtles ========
# The four player objects (062000, 062050, 0620A0, 0620F0; kind 0-3, Leonardo first). The functions whose own object
# (A0) is a turtle are named turtle_...; their fields as the turtle code uses them. Evidence from build/listing/tmnt.s.

# ---- the turtle family's fields ----

# the turtle that pushes the scroll this frame (not carried back by it): cleared every frame (01ADA2 "move.b D0,
# ($3,A0)"), 0x7F when it pushes the scroll across (01AF46, 01B130 "move.b #$7f, ($3,A0)"), bit 7 when it pushes it
# up or down (01B32A, 01B53E "ori.b #$80, ($3,A0)"); turtle_run_all moves the others with the scroll: x unless the low
# bits (01A5AA "move.b ($3,A0), D2" + "andi.b #$7f, D2"), y unless bit 7 (01A5D0 "tst.b ($3,A0)" bmi). Nothing starts a
# turtle with a parameter (turtle_init clears it)
scoped object turtle 03 scroll_push u8
# how fast its height goes up or down in a jump, a fall or a knock (8.8, a size: falling says which way): the jump
# starts it at 0x500 (01B7B6 "move.l D0, ($1a,A0)" with 0x0500_0010; a jump attack: 01BA4C "move.w #$500,
# ($1a,A0)"), gravity takes 0x50 a frame going up (01BAC0 "subi.w #$50,
# ($1a,A0)"), it is added to the height going up (01BACA "add.w D0, ($10,A0)") and taken off it coming down (01BC66
# "sub.w D0, ($10,A0)", up to 0x500: 01BC52); the knocks' arc the same (01D49A-01D4BE). Not the x speed of the
# common field: the turtle moves across by its stick (turtle_move)
scoped object turtle 1A jump_speed u16
# 0 going up, else coming down: turned over at the top of the arc (01BBA8 "not.b ($1c,A0)", 01D4B4 "subq.b #1,
# ($1c,A0)"), tested to add or take off jump_speed (01BAFC "tst.b ($1c,A0)", 01BE22, 01D492)
scoped object turtle 1C falling u8
# frames counted down for the move it is in: how long holding jump keeps the jump rising (16 at the start: 01B7B6
# "move.l D0, ($1a,A0)" with 0x0500_0010; 01BAA8 "subq.b #1, ($1d,A0)" and 01BAB0 "btst #$4, (A1)": released, cleared
# at 01BAB8), the wait on a wall before kicking off it (01BB6C "move.b #$8, ($1d,A0)", 01BCE8-01BCF0), the wait
# after landing in an area's entry (01A080 "move.b #$8, ($1d,A0)", 01A0CC "subq.b #1, ($1d,A0)")
scoped object turtle 1D move_timer u8
# frames standing still: every 0x18 the idle animation goes on to its next state (turtle_stand 01B990 "addq.b #1,
# ($1e,A0)", 01B994 "cmpi.b #$18, ($1e,A0)"); a new state clears it (turtle_set_state 01B918 "clr.b ($1e,A0)")
scoped object turtle 1E idle_time u8
# a jump's turn: set when the somersault at the top starts (01BBC4 "subq.b #1, ($1f,A0)", and turtle_jump_top goes
# on falling when its animation ends: 01BAE2 "tst.b ($1f,A0)"); kicking off a wall, set when the wall is on the right
# (01BB42 "move.b #$ff, ($1f,A0)", cleared for a wall on the left: 01BB56), and the kick goes the other way (01BD0C
# "tst.b ($1f,A0)": 0x300 left or right); the jump clears it (01B7BA "clr.b ($1f,A0)")
scoped object turtle 1F jump_flip u8
# its speed across when it is knocked: from the knock's table (01C4C4 "move.w (-$10,A6,D2.w), ($20,A0)"), added to x
# or taken off it by the knock's side (01D462-01D466 "add.l D0, ($12,A0)", 01D4F2-01D4F6 "sub.l D0, ($12,A0)") and
# slowed down to 0x200 (01D47E "sub.w D7, ($20,A0)", 01D482); in an area's exit, the speed it drifts across by
# (02D298 "move.w ($6,A6,D1.w), ($20,A0)", 02D528 "move.w ($20,A0), D0" + "add.l D0, ($12,A0)")
scoped object turtle 20 drift_speed u16
# the side it is knocked to: bit 0 to the left, bit 7 hit from behind (01C30E-01C33C: from its facing, flags bit 6,
# and the attacker's, hit bit 7; 01C33C "move.b D0, ($22,A0)"); the knocks move it left with bit 0 (01C6DE "btst #$0,
# ($22,A0)" then "neg.w D0"; 01D44C), a knock-down from behind turns it round (01C482 "tst.b ($22,A0)" bpl, 01C492
# "eori.b #$40, (A0)"), and turtle_collide checks the walls on that side (01AB70 "btst #$0, ($22,A0)")
scoped object turtle 22 knockback_flags u8
# which reaction to a hit it is in: picks the reaction's handler (01C222 "move.b ($23,A0), D0", the table at 01C272)
# and is set with the reaction's state (01C44A "move.b D1, ($23,A0)", the state from the table at 01D3FC)
scoped object turtle 23 hit_reaction u8
# the step of the reaction (FF: over): the steps' handlers by it (01C666 "move.b ($24,A0), D0"), the next one (01C732
# "addq.b #1, ($24,A0)"), and turtle_hit ends the reaction with FF (01C234 "cmpi.b #-$1, ($24,A0)")
scoped object turtle 24 reaction_step u8
# which sequence of steps the reaction follows: frame_count's bit 0, +2 near the screen's edges (01C4D0-01C4DA "move.b
# D0, ($25,A0)", 01C518 "addq.b #2, ($25,A0)"), or by the bars left (01C53E); it picks the steps' table (01C738
# "move.b ($25,A0), D0") and the far ones slide faster (01C6CE "cmpi.b #$2, ($25,A0)")
scoped object turtle 25 reaction_variant u8
# frames to wait or slide in a reaction (01C688 "move.b #$8, ($26,A0)", 01C6C6 "subq.b #1, ($26,A0)", 01CA50
# "move.b #$60, ($26,A0)"), and in an area's exit (02BD72 "move.b #$20, ($26,A0)", 02BD94 "subq.b #1, ($26,A0)")
scoped object turtle 26 wait_timer u8
# the buttons pressed while an enemy holds it: one more for each press (01CECA "tst.b (A2)", 01CED0 "addq.b #1,
# ($27,A0)"), and at 8 it breaks free (01CED4 "cmpi.b #$8, ($27,A0)"; 6 at 01CFDA, 0x10 at 01D126)
scoped object turtle 27 escape_presses u8
# the enemy that hit it, or holds it: the attack check's second loop (0473DC "move.l A0, ($28,A1)"); the reaction reads
# its attack's sound by it (01C2DA "movea.l ($28,A0), A6"), and breaking free makes it let go (01CEEA "movea.l ($28,A0),
# A6" + "move.b #$3, ($5,A6)")
scoped object turtle 28 hit_by u32 object
# the step of its walk out in an area's exit: each step's handler by it (02BADA "move.b ($2c,A0), D0", 02BF54), the next
# one (02BB7C "addq.b #1, ($2c,A0)"); the exit's start clears it (02B304 "clr.b ($2c,A0)")
scoped object turtle 2C exit_step u8
# frames it cannot be hit for: 0x7F when it comes in (019CB8 "move.b #$7f, ($2d,A0)"); while it counts down
# (01B6AA "subq.b #1, ($2d,A0)") flags bit 1 (can be hit) stays clear, set only at 0 (01B6A2 "tst.b ($2d,A0)",
# 01B6D8 "ori.b #$2, (A0)"); with bit 7 (019B74 "ori.b #$80, ($2d,A0)") it also blinks (01B6D0 "eori.b #$80, (A0)")
# until 0x80 (01B6B2)
scoped object turtle 2D invulnerable_time u8
# which attack of its combo it is at: 0 the first, 1 the second (01B840 "move.b #$1, ($2e,A0)", 01BF56 "addq.b #1,
# ($2e,A0)"), 2 after breaking free (01CF02 "move.b #$2, ($2e,A0)"); turtle_attack_over picks the next attack by it
# (01BF40 "tst.b ($2e,A0)", 01BF48 "cmpi.b #$1, ($2e,A0)")
scoped object turtle 2E combo_step u8
# frames since the attack button was last pressed (cleared by a press: 01B6E8 "btst #$5, D2" + 01B6F0 "clr.b
# ($31,A0)", 01B6F4 "addq.b #1, ($31,A0)" held at 255); an attack pressed within 13 frames goes on with the combo
# (01BEF6 "cmpi.b #$d, ($31,A0)")
scoped object turtle 31 attack_press_time u8
# an enemy is close behind it: cleared every frame by the object loop (04783C, 047850 "clr.b ($44,A5)" through the
# list of the turtles at 062FA0) and set by an enemy within 0x40 across and 6 deep on its back (0482FA, 048352 "move.b
# #$ff, ($44,A6)", A6 the turtle from that list); the attack button then starts the combos from 12 on, with the attack
# that hits behind (01B838 "tst.b ($44,A0)", 01B846 "addi.w #$c, D7")
scoped object turtle 44 enemy_behind u8
# the combo it does: 3 bytes a combo (its three attacks) in its table of attacks (01D5F2, by the turtle): one of the
# first four at random (01B80C-01B820 "move.w D7, ($46,A0)" after "jsr $48448.l"), +12 with an enemy behind (01B84A),
# +24 for the throw (01BFDC "addi.w #$18, ($46,A0)"); its next attack read by it (01C102 "move.w ($46,A0), D7" +
# "move.b ($1,A4,D7.w), D0")
scoped object turtle 46 combo u16
# the blink of the "press the buttons" prompt drawn while an enemy holds it: 0x40 at the start (019CA2 "move.b #$40,
# ($48,A0)"), counted down every frame of a hold (01D28E "subq.b #1, ($48,A0)"), the picture changed at each 0x80
# (01D296-01D29A, 01D316 "move.b #$c0, ($48,A0)") and drawn again at 0x3F and 0xBF (01D29E, 01D2A8)
scoped object turtle 48 prompt_time u8
# which of the prompt's two pictures is drawn (01D27A-01D288: 01D35C or 01D3AC by 01D280 "tst.b ($49,A0)"; turned over at
# 01D310 "eori.b #$ff, ($49,A0)")
scoped object turtle 49 prompt_frame u8
# the enemy its attack hit last: the attack check's first loop (0472C4 "move.l A1, ($4a,A0)"); its combo throws it
# when it can be (01BF76 "movea.l ($4a,A0), A6" + "tst.b ($4b,A6)") and the throw sets it flying (01C35E, 01C36A
# "move.w #$500, ($4,A6)"). Its low half is where the common attacker is: no code writes a turtle's attacker (the
# attack check's second loop gives it hit_by instead)
scoped object turtle 4A hit_target u32 object
# where the prompt is in the fixed layer (an offset from 100000, by its place on the screen: 01D2C0-01D2EE "move.w D1,
# ($4e,A0)"), drawn there again (01D2B8 "adda.w ($4e,A0), A5") and erased (01D32C "adda.w ($4e,A0), A6"); a turtle
# is never an attacker of the attack check's second and third loops, so the common hit_object never lands here
scoped object turtle 4E prompt_place u16

# ---- the jump into area 4 (turtle_jump_in_...): +1E is a speed across, not idle_time and jump_flip ----
# its speed across while it jumps in: from the area's table of places (019DF4 "move.w ($4,A6,D1.w), ($1e,A0)"),
# added to x every frame (01A28C "move.w ($1e,A0), D1" + 01A292 "add.l D1, ($12,A0)")
scoped object turtle_jump_in 1E jump_in_speed s16

# ---- the functions whose own object is a turtle ----

# 004378 hands it the players who have just put a coin in (0600A6): for each bit of D7 (019B48 "lsr.b #1, D7") the
# turtle starts (019B50 "bsr $19c94"), full health (019B54 "move.w #$a03, ($8,A0)"), its marker (019B66), blinking
# (019B74 "ori.b #$80, ($2d,A0)")
019B3A  func  turtle_join

019B92  func  turtle_respawn
# 0067BC (the area's start) hands it the players in play: each turtle starts with the area's entry (019C0A "moveq
# #-$1, D6") and the bars the player had (019C6A "move.b $60106.l, $62008.l"; full and saved when 060122 bit 7 is
# clear: 019C22-019C28)
019BEA  func  turtle_start_area
# the object cleared (019C9A "move.l D1, (A0)+"), its kind the player (019CAE "move.b D0, ($2,A0)"), in use and drawn

019DD4  func  turtle_jump_in_start

01A160  func  turtle_jump_in_step

01A2D6  func  turtle_skateboard_shadow
# every turtle in play (01A434 "lea $62000.l, A0", 01A446 "btst #$0, (A0)"): its controls (01A44E "bsr $1b69e"),

01A3CA  func  turtle_run_all
# when it is being hit or thrown, the stick as if pushed the way it goes (01AB6E-01AB7C "move.b D0, (A1)"); the solid

01AB2C  func  turtle_collide

01AC84  func  turtle_check_walls

01B5D2  func  turtle_other_at_edge

# (01D718 "bne $1d7c0")
01D708  func  turtle_walls_up
# the walls from the cells around (0633D4-0633DB): up (01D760 "ori.b #$2, D0"), right (01D78C "ori.b #$8, D0"), left
# (01D7B8 "ori.b #$10, D0"), and the corners
01D742  func  turtle_wall_contacts
# its floor raised a level (01D7E4 "addi.b #$8, ($32,A0)") for each wall cell above it in the floor map (01D7EA "suba.w

# off its floor cell going right: at its floor height, unless the cell lets it through (01D9AE-01D9CE: cells -6, -4,

# right 0x1000, cells -6, -4 a step up)

01DC5E  func  turtle_corner_contacts

02C80E  func  turtle_exit_highway

02CDC0  func  turtle_exit_go_to_place

02D25E  func  turtle_exit_building_site_drop

02D48A  func  turtle_exit_building_site_rise

02D61E  func  turtle_exit_final_walk

# ---- comments ----
comment turtle_join a player who has just put a coin in comes into the game with full health, blinking, with its marker
comment turtle_respawn a player whose turtle was knocked out comes back at the start point with full health
comment turtle_start_area every player's turtle starts the area with the health bars it had
comment turtle_init a turtle's object set up for its player, invulnerable for a while, and placed by the area's entry
comment turtle_place_at_start the turtle at its start point, standing
comment turtle_start_fall_in the turtle starts the area falling in from above
comment turtle_jump_in_start the turtle starts area 4 jumping in, and the object that comes in with them
comment turtle_start_marker the player's marker object starts at the turtle's place
comment turtle_fall_in every frame of the entry: the turtles fall in across, land, wait and stand
comment turtle_jump_in_step every frame of area 4's entry: the object that came with them bounces away, the turtles jump in, land and stand
comment turtle_skateboard_shadow on the skateboard the turtles' shadows change every four frames
comment turtle_run_all every turtle in play a frame: controls, move, the screen's limits, the scroll, floor and walls
comment turtle_collide the turtle against solid objects, the floor map and its walls, and falling when nothing holds it
comment turtle_check_walls the walls around the turtle checked by the way its stick pushes
comment turtle_move the stick moves the turtle across and up or down, or pushes the scroll at the screen's edges
comment turtle_push_scroll_left the turtle at the left edge moves the scroll left at its walking speed
comment turtle_push_scroll_right the turtle at the right edge moves the scroll right at its walking speed
comment turtle_push_scroll_up the turtle at the top moves the scroll up at its walking speed
comment turtle_push_scroll_down the turtle at the bottom moves the scroll down at its walking speed
comment turtle_walk_speed the turtle's walking speeds, straight or on a diagonal
comment turtle_other_at_edge whether another turtle stands at the opposite edge, so the scroll cannot move away from it
comment turtle_control every frame: the time it cannot be hit, the blinking, and its routine (stand, walk, jump, attack, hit)
comment turtle_start_walk the turtle starts walking, across or up, or stands on the skateboard
comment turtle_start_jump the jump button starts a jump, or the special attack with the attack button too
comment turtle_start_combo the attack button starts a combo at random, or the attack behind when an enemy is there
comment turtle_start_attack an attack from the first step of the combo
comment turtle_set_attack the attack's kind, sound and damage, and the turtle attacking
comment turtle_set_state a new routine and state for the turtle with its animation
comment turtle_set_animation the turtle's animation for its routine and state
comment turtle_stand standing: jump, attack, walk, or the idle animation
comment turtle_walk walking: jump, attack, stand, or across and up by the stick
comment turtle_jump in the air: a jump attack by its height, or the jump's state
comment turtle_jump_rise the jump going up, higher while the button is held
comment turtle_jump_top the top of the jump: the somersault, or kicking off a wall it is pushed against
comment turtle_jump_fall the jump coming down until it lands
comment turtle_land_on_ground the turtle lands on the ground
comment turtle_land the turtle lands and stands, with the landing's sound
comment turtle_wall_jump the turtle on a wall for a moment, then away from it falling
comment turtle_attack attacking: the jump attacks' arcs, steps forward, the end of the attack
comment turtle_attack_over the attack ended: the combo's next attack, the throw, or back to standing
comment turtle_hit hit: the reaction by the attack that hit it, and out of play when its bars run out
comment turtle_hit_wait the reaction lasts its animation
comment turtle_refill_health the turtle's health full again
comment turtle_walk_off_screen the turtle walks off the screen
comment turtle_knocked_down knocked down: flies, slides, lies, gets up
comment turtle_drop_to_floor the turtle comes down to its floor
comment turtle_knocked_back knocked back: pushed, slides, gets up
comment turtle_knocked_flying knocked flying, or knocked out by the hit that takes its last bar
comment turtle_hit_countdown the reaction's last wait
comment turtle_held held by an enemy until it breaks free or its health runs out
comment turtle_held_struggle held: the prompt, the health draining, the presses to break free
comment turtle_held_dies the turtle's health ran out while held: the holder lets go and it dies
comment turtle_held_briefly held by an enemy for a moment, free after fewer presses
comment turtle_held_knock_off held by an enemy that is knocked away when the turtle breaks free
comment turtle_held_throw_off held by an enemy the turtle throws when it breaks free
comment turtle_hit_fly knocked into the air until it lands
comment turtle_show_prompt the prompt to press the buttons drawn blinking near the held turtle
comment turtle_erase_prompt the prompt erased
comment turtle_save_health the turtle's health bars kept for its player and shown again
comment turtle_knockback_arc the turtle on a knock's arc across and through the air, until it lands
comment turtle_floor_contact on another object: whether the turtle stands on it or is in the air
comment turtle_wall_contacts the walls around the turtle from the floor map's cells around it
comment turtle_raise_floor the turtle steps up a level of the floor map
comment turtle_lower_floor the turtle steps down a level of the floor map
comment turtle_start_area_exit the area is cleared: the turtles start walking out
comment turtle_exit_street area 2's exit: the turtles walk to one place and jump in there

# ======== the structures' pointers where the objects' code works them out ========
# enemy_look_at_target reads the turtle the enemy goes after (its slot's turtle) and tells it an enemy is behind it

# ======== the enemies, second half (04DC04-04F68A, 04F722-051492, 051750-051AB0, 051C44-05373A, 02FFFE-03015C) ========
# Kinds (enemy_definitions by the start's "move.l #..., ($24,A0)"; the frames rendered from the starts' and attacks'
# animations): 03 a Foot soldier with knives (stabs, kicks, throws knives: kind 04), 05 with a spear (thrusts it,
# throws it: kind 06), 07 with axes (throws three: kind 08), 09 with a gun (fires four shots: kind 0A, clubs with it),
# 0B the bare-handed one that throws a boomerang (kind 0C), 0D the bare-handed one that throws shuriken (kind 0E), 69
# the Foot soldiers on rocket skateboards of area 6 (a gun, axe or knife soldier by the parameter), 11 Rocksteady, 10
# Bebop, 4E a Mouser, 53 a Roadkill Rodney (one wheel: rushes and brakes; a whip arm and a laser), 0F Shredder (and
# his clones, parameter bit 7).

# ---- the sub-families' fields ----

# the spear soldier: hits with the spear left before it throws it; the start sets it by the players' setting
# (04DCE6 "lea $4dd2c.l, A6", 04DCEC "move.b (A6,D0.w), ($4a,A0)"); a thrust that hits takes one off and at 0 it
# throws the spear (04E312 "subq.b #1, ($4a,A0)", 04E31A "move.w #$4c, ($28,A0)")
scoped object enemy_spear 4A hits_left u8
# the throws of knives, shuriken and axes: frames until it lets go of the next one (04E526 "move.b #$14, ($46,A0)",
# 04E53C "subq.b #1, ($46,A0)"; 04F0AA, 04F0C0; 04EC3E "move.b #$10, ($46,A0)") and how many it still throws (the
# axes: 04EC0A "move.w #$1003, ($46,A0)", 04EC44 "subq.b #1, ($47,A0)")
scoped object enemy_throw 46 throw_time u8
scoped object enemy_throw 47 throws_left u8
# the gun soldier's shots: frames to the next and how many left (04ED0A "move.w #$804, ($46,A0)",
# 04ED20 "subq.b #1, ($46,A0)", 04ED44 "subq.b #1, ($47,A0)")
scoped object enemy_shoot 46 shot_time u8
scoped object enemy_shoot 47 shots_left u8
# the boomerang it threw (04F176 "move.l A6, ($46,A0)" after 047C5A spawns it; 04F186 "movea.l ($46,A0), A6" waits for
# its routine 4)
scoped object enemy_boomerang 46 boomerang u32 object
