# ======== the objects that are not enemies (proposal: kinds3) ========
# The kinds of the kind table (053896) whose objects are thrown or fired things, scenery, and the places Foot
# soldiers come out of; their routines (the table each kind's entry points at) are in objects/states.c. What each
# kind is comes from its code and from its animations, rendered from the addresses its set_animation calls take.
# Fields: only what a kind's own code uses that the common object fields do not name.
# "Gone off the screen" below is flags bit 5, which the objects' screen places set when an object is out of the
# bounds its kind has in spawn_offsets (047916 "bset #$5, ($0,A0)", after 0478DA "lea $53a5e.l, A4"); "struck" is
# routine 4, which the attack check sets on an object a turtle's attack hits (0472CE "move.w #$400, ($4,A1)").
#
# kind 04 knife: a Foot soldier's thrown knife (04E548 "lea $4e50a.l, A5": the record's kind is 04)
# kind 06 spear: the spear a Foot soldier throws after its thrusts (04E388 "lea $4e34e.l, A5": kind 06)
# kind 08 axe: a Foot soldier's thrown axe, spinning (04EC32 "lea $4ebee.l, A5": kind 08)
# kind 0A gun_shot: a Foot soldier's gun shot, which drops to the ground as it flies (04ED32 "lea $4ecee.l, A5")
# kind 0C boomerang: a Foot soldier's boomerang, which flies out and back (04F162 "lea $4f104.l, A5")
# kind 0E shuriken: a Foot soldier's thrown shuriken (04F0D6 "lea $4f08e.l, A5")
# kind 12 kicked_door: a door in area 0 a Foot soldier kicks down; the object becomes that soldier (spawn lists)
# kind 13 elevator: an elevator in area 0 whose doors open to let out one or two Foot soldiers (spawn lists)
# kind 14 manhole_soldier: a Foot soldier of area 2 that comes up out of a manhole and throws its cover (spawn lists)
# kind 15 window_soldier_drop: a Foot soldier that jumps out through a window of areas 2 and 7, breaking its glass
# kind 16 window_soldier_dive: a Foot soldier that dives out through a window of area 2, breaking its glass
# kind 17 sewer_ripple: a ripple on the sewer's water (area 3) a Foot soldier leaps out of in a splash
# kind 18 bomber: a Foot soldier (areas 0, 2, 7) that leans out from cover, throws dynamite, then walks in
# kind 19 rolling (manhole cover): the manhole cover kind 14 throws, rolling (04CAAA "lea $4c9b6.l, A5")
# kind 1A door_piece: a piece of the door kind 12 breaks, flying off (04C52C "lea $4c4c6.l, A5": four of them)
# kind 1B double_door: two doors of area 4 that swing open to let out two Foot soldiers (spawn lists)
# kind 1C boss_shutter: the roll-up door of area 4 that opens for Bebop and Rocksteady (spawn lists; 04A7C4 their
#   signal)
# kind 1F glass_shards: the shards of a window kinds 15 and 16 break (04CC58 "lea $4cbac.l, A5", 04CDEC)
# kind 3B foot_car: the hit box of the car full of Foot soldiers that drives across area 5; the car itself is the
#   picture draw_screen_picture puts in layer A (stage 5's layers: 01ECEC's sixth entry is 01F7EC), and the object
#   is never drawn: it starts in use only (04A91C "move.w #$100, ($0,A6)") and its start sets only bit 2 (04A99C
#   "bset #$2, ($0,A0)")
# kind 3C scenery (lamp): a floor lamp; no spawn list, record or code starts kind 3C, and no scenario runs it
# kind 3D scenery (chair): a toppled chair; no spawn list, record or code starts kind 3D, and no scenario runs it
# kind 3E scenery (fire): flames on the floor of area 0, three sizes by the parameter (spawn lists)
# kind 3F scenery (open manhole): the hole kind 14 leaves (04CA5A "lea $4c9be.l, A5"); a turtle that steps on it
#   falls in: its attack 0C is turtle_hit's reaction 12 (the table at 01C272), which takes a bar (01CDCE "subq.b #1,
#   ($8,A0)") and puts the turtle where the hole is (01CDF4 "move.l D0, ($12,A0)")
# kind 45 technodrome_door: a door of area 8 that opens and lets out Foot soldiers one after another (spawn lists)
# kind 47 falling_debris: a burning piece that falls from the ceiling of area 0 and burns on the floor (spawn lists)
# kind 4F mouser_hole: a wall of areas 3 and 7 that breaks and lets out Mousers (kind 4E) one after another
# kind 58 laser_bolt: a Roadkill Rodney's laser bolt (0523D4 "lea $5232a.l, A5": kind 58)
# kind 5D whip_arm: a Roadkill Rodney's whip arm, which can catch a turtle and drag it (0524EA "lea $5244a.l, A5")
# kind 5F rocksteady_bullet: Rocksteady's bullet, level or up at a turtle in the air (records 04FD9C, 04FDA2)
# kind 64 bebop_shot: Bebop's ring-shaped shot (050CC0 "lea $50c7c.l, A5": kind 64)
# kind 6A tire_soldier: a Foot soldier of area 5 that comes up out of a tire and throws it (spawn lists)
# kind 6B rolling (tire): the tire kind 6A throws, rolling (04C976 "lea $4c894.l, A5")
# kind 6C shredder_piece: a piece of Shredder that flies off and bounces when he loses a bar (05321E "lea
#   $531c2.l, A5")
# kind 6D shredder_ray: Shredder's ray (053708 "lea $53622.l, A5")
# kind 6F rocket: the rocket the Foot soldiers of area 5 (kind 56) carry and throw, which explodes where it lands
#   (04D606 "lea $4d558.l, A5", 04D60C "jsr $47c5a.l")
# kind 70 dynamite: the stick of dynamite kind 18 throws, which bounces and explodes (04D4D8 "lea $4d466.l, A5")
# kind 71 motorbike: a Foot soldier on a motorbike of area 5; hit, the soldier falls off and the bike rides on

# ---- the families' fields ----

# the sound a turtle it hits makes: a move's number times 4 like an enemy's move, which turtle_hit reads off the
# object that hit it (01C2DE "move.w ($28,A6), D0", 01C2E4 "lea $1d556.l, A6"); these objects set their own:
# 04A98A "move.w #$d4, ($28,A0)"
scoped object foot_car 28 hit_sound u16
# 04A830 "move.w #$dc, ($28,A0)" (the same as foot_car's hit_sound)
scoped object scenery_open_manhole 28 hit_sound u16
# 04AA98 "move.w #$70, ($28,A0)" (the same as foot_car's hit_sound)
scoped object falling_debris 28 hit_sound u16
# 04AB70 "move.w #$44, ($28,A0)" (the same as foot_car's hit_sound)
scoped object motorbike 28 hit_sound u16
# 04BA6E "move.w #$44, ($28,A0)", 04C132 "move.w #$44, ($28,A0)" (the same as foot_car's hit_sound)
scoped object rolling 28 hit_sound u16
# 04C27C "move.w #$70, ($28,A0)" (the same as foot_car's hit_sound)
scoped object rocket 28 hit_sound u16
# 04C348 "move.w #$70, ($28,A0)" (the same as foot_car's hit_sound)
scoped object dynamite 28 hit_sound u16

# the Foot soldier that threw it (04F172 "move.l A0, ($20,A6)" after 04F168 "jsr $47c5a.l"): while the soldier
# reacts to a hit the boomerang flies on and does not come back (04BE7C "movea.l ($20,A0), A6", 04BE80 "cmpi.b #$5,
# ($4,A6)")
scoped object boomerang 20 thrower u32 object
# frames it flies out and then back: 32 out (04BE44 "move.b #$20, ($46,A0)"), then it turns round (04BE5A "subq.b #1,
# ($46,A0)", 04BE62 "neg.w ($1a,A0)") and flies 32 more before it is gone (04BEA8 "subq.b #1, ($46,A0)", 04BEB0
# "clr.w ($0,A0)")
scoped object boomerang 46 flight_time u8

# the Roadkill Rodney whose arm it is (0524FE "move.l A0, ($24,A6)" after 0524F0 "jsr $47c5a.l"): the arm goes by
# his arm_flags and arm_length (04B828 "movea.l ($24,A0), A1", 04B82C "btst #$7, ($4a,A1)", 04B97E "move.b ($46,A1),

# ---- comments ----
comment knife_start a thrown knife flies the way its thrower faces; on the skateboards it is carried along with the ride
comment knife_fly the knife flies until it hits a turtle or leaves the screen
comment knife_vanish the knife's burst ends and it is gone
comment knife_struck a turtle's attack knocks the knife away
comment spear_start a thrown spear flies the way its thrower faces
comment spear_fly the spear flies through what it hits until it leaves the screen
comment spear_vanish the broken spear is gone
comment spear_struck a turtle's attack breaks the spear
comment axe_start a thrown axe flies spinning the way its thrower faces; it cannot be hit
comment axe_fly the axe flies through what it hits until it leaves the screen
comment gun_shot_start a gun shot flies the way the soldier faces, dropping as it goes
comment gun_shot_fly the shot flies until it hits a turtle, the ground, or leaves the screen
comment gun_shot_vanish the shot's burst ends and it is gone
comment gun_shot_struck the shot bursts; a turtle's attack can never strike it
comment boomerang_start a thrown boomerang flies out
comment boomerang_fly_out the boomerang flies out a while, then turns back
comment boomerang_fly_back the boomerang flies back a while and is gone
comment boomerang_fly_on the boomerang's thrower was hit: it flies on off the screen
comment boomerang_struck a turtle's attack breaks the boomerang
comment boomerang_vanish the broken boomerang is gone
comment shuriken_start a thrown shuriken flies the way its thrower faces
comment shuriken_fly the shuriken flies until it hits a turtle or leaves the screen
comment shuriken_vanish the shuriken's burst ends and it is gone
comment shuriken_struck a turtle's attack knocks the shuriken away
comment kicked_door_start a closed door
comment kicked_door_wait on the screen, a Foot soldier kicks the door down
comment kicked_door_break the door's pieces fly off
comment kicked_door_become_foot_soldier the soldier who kicked the door goes on as a Foot soldier
comment elevator_start an elevator with its doors closed
comment elevator_wait_on_screen the elevator waits until the scroll brings it to its place
comment elevator_open the elevator's doors open and a Foot soldier comes out, two with three turtles or more in play
comment elevator_scroll_off the open elevator is gone once it leaves the screen
comment manhole_soldier_start a manhole with a Foot soldier under its cover, counted as an enemy
comment manhole_soldier_wait the soldier comes up out of the manhole holding its cover
comment manhole_soldier_jump_out the soldier jumps out and leaves the manhole open
comment manhole_soldier_fly the soldier's jump comes down to the ground
comment manhole_soldier_throw the soldier throws the manhole cover
comment thrower_become_foot_soldier after its throw the soldier goes on as a Foot soldier
comment tire_soldier_start a tire with a Foot soldier behind it, counted as an enemy
comment tire_soldier_wait the soldier comes up out of the tire
comment tire_soldier_jump the soldier jumps
comment tire_soldier_fly the soldier's jump comes down until it lands
comment tire_soldier_throw the soldier throws the tire
comment rolling_manhole_cover_start a thrown manhole cover rolls at the turtles, knocking them flying
comment rolling_tire_start a thrown tire rolls at the turtles, knocking them flying
comment rolling_wait_hittable it rolls, and a turtle can hit it after a few frames
comment rolling_roll it rolls on until it leaves the screen
comment rolling_struck a turtle's attack sends it rolling back the other way
comment window_soldier_drop_start a Foot soldier behind a window, waiting for its place on the screen
comment window_soldier_drop_jump_out the soldier jumps out through the window and the glass shatters
comment window_soldier_drop_break_window the window is drawn broken
comment window_soldier_drop_fall the soldier comes down from the window until it lands
comment window_soldier_become_foot_soldier the soldier who came out of the window goes on as a Foot soldier
comment window_soldier_dive_start a Foot soldier behind a window, waiting for its place on the screen
comment window_soldier_dive_wait the window is drawn broken and the soldier dives out through the glass
comment window_soldier_dive_fall the soldier's dive comes down until it lands
comment glass_shards_start a window's glass shatters into shards
comment glass_shards_fall the glass shards fall until they are gone
comment door_piece_start a piece of the kicked door flies off
comment door_piece_fly the door's piece flies until it leaves the screen
comment sewer_ripple_start a ripple on the sewer's water, a Foot soldier under it, counted as an enemy
comment sewer_ripple_swim the ripple moves back and forth, then splashes
comment sewer_ripple_leap_out the soldier leaps out of the water
comment sewer_ripple_vanish the splash ends and the ripple is gone
comment sewer_ripple_swim_again no room for the soldier: the ripple swims on
comment bomber_start a Foot soldier behind cover with a stick of dynamite
comment bomber_wait on the screen, the soldier leans out
comment bomber_lean_out the soldier gets ready to throw
comment bomber_throw the soldier throws the dynamite, then steps out
comment bomber_step_out the soldier turns and walks out
comment bomber_walk_in the soldier walks in and goes on as a Foot soldier
comment dynamite_start a stick of dynamite with its fuse burning
comment dynamite_throw the dynamite is thrown at the nearest turtle
comment dynamite_bounce the dynamite bounces, flashing, and explodes on its last bounce
comment dynamite_explode the explosion ends and the dynamite is gone
comment double_door_start two closed doors
comment double_door_wait the doors swing open and two Foot soldiers come out
comment double_door_open the open doorway stays in the layer and the doors are gone
comment boss_shutter_start the closed roll-up door of area 4, with its colours
comment boss_shutter_wait the scroll reaches the door: the music stops and it rolls up
comment boss_shutter_open the door stays open, then Bebop and Rocksteady are told to come out, with their music
comment start_foot_car the car full of Foot soldiers starts behind the screen's left edge
comment foot_car_start the car drives across, knocking flying the turtles it runs into
comment foot_car_drive the car drives on until it is past the screen
comment scenery_lamp_start a floor lamp

comment falling_debris_burn the burning debris is gone once it leaves the screen
comment mouser_hole_start a wall with Mousers behind it
comment mouser_hole_wait on the screen, the wall breaks open
comment mouser_hole_open the broken wall is down

comment laser_bolt_start a laser bolt flies the way Rodney faces
comment laser_bolt_fly the bolt flies until it hits a turtle or leaves the screen
comment laser_bolt_vanish the bolt's burst ends and it is gone
comment whip_arm_start Rodney's whip arm, whose grab holds a turtle
comment whip_arm_follow the arm reaches out, is drawn in or put away as Rodney does it
comment whip_arm_let_go the turtle broke free of the arm
comment whip_arm_drag the arm drags the turtle it caught
comment whip_arm_struck a turtle's attack hits the arm, which lets go of a turtle it holds
comment whip_arm_place the arm at Rodney's side, as long as Rodney stretches it
comment rocksteady_bullet_start Rocksteady's bullet flies level, or up at a turtle in the air
comment rocksteady_bullet_fly the bullet flies until it hits a turtle or leaves the screen
comment rocksteady_bullet_vanish the bullet's burst ends and it is gone
comment bebop_shot_start Bebop's shot flies the way he faces
comment bebop_shot_fly the shot flies through what it hits until it leaves the screen
comment shredder_piece_start a piece of Shredder flies off
comment shredder_piece_bounce the piece bounces lower and lower until it leaves the screen
comment shredder_ray_start Shredder's ray
comment shredder_ray_shine the ray lasts a second, less if Shredder is hit
comment rocket_start a rocket is fired the way the soldier faces, or dropped when the soldier is hit
comment rocket_fly the rocket comes down and explodes
comment rocket_explode the explosion ends and the rocket is gone
comment motorbike_start a Foot soldier rides in on a motorbike

# ---- the pointers where these objects' code works them out ----
# the turtle a whip arm caught: its attack hit it last (hit_object, put in the work RAM's page): breaking it free sets
# its escape_presses (04B8B8 "move.b #$10, ($27,A2)", 04B916 "move.b #$10, ($27,A2)", 04B970 "move.b #$10, ($27,A2)")
local whip_arm_drag 04B8AC a2 turtle object.turtle
# the same turtle when the arm is struck (04B90A "move.l #$62000, D7", 04B910 "move.w ($4e,A0), D7")
local whip_arm_struck 04B914 a2 turtle object.turtle
# the same turtle when Rodney is hit (04B964 "move.l #$62000, D7", 04B96A "move.w ($4e,A0), D7")
local whip_arm_place 04B96E a2 turtle object.turtle

# the enemies' routines keep their turtle's slot in A1 (target, a link in the work RAM's page) for their moves
local enemy_think 048A9E a1 slot player_slot
local enemy_attack 048D94 a1 slot player_slot
local enemy_react 048EE4 a1 slot player_slot
local enemy_step_over_edge 048F0C a1 slot player_slot
local enemy_enter 048A6A a1 slot player_slot
# the second nearest slot the retargeting swaps in (048AD0 "exg A5, A6", 048AD2 "cmp.b ($4,A6), D0") may be no slot:
# enemy_nearest_slots leaves A5 as it found it when it finds one player (in the continue scenario, frame 4212, A5
# holds 048A94, a handler's address, and the byte read is its code's: data_assets.py's
# second_player_through_a_stale_pointer), so that read goes over the bus, as the original's, not into a slot; the
# same retargeting in the Mousers', Rodney's and Shredder's thinking (051130, 051DBC, 05287C "exg A5, A6")
local enemy_think 048AD0 a6 second_slot none
local enemy_mouser_think 051130 a6 second_slot none
local enemy_rodney_think 051DBC a6 second_slot none
local enemy_shredder_think 05287C a6 second_slot none


# ======== the objects' helpers (0180E0-01832A, 0184D2, 018574; 011DFA-011E6A) ========
# Small routines the objects of objects/scenery.c, objects/krang.c, objects/rock_soldiers.c, objects/air_attacks.c and
# objects/stockman.c share, each on the object in A0 (and the one in A6 when it starts another)
# the object's attack and hit, health_bars and health, depth and the sound a turtle it hits makes, from a 10-byte record
# (0180E0 "move.w ($0,A6), ($6,A0)", 0180E6 "move.l ($2,A6), ($8,A0)", 0180EC "move.w ($6,A6), ($34,A0)", 0180F2
# "move.w ($8,A6), ($28,A0)"): every kind of these files starts with one
0180E0  func  set_object_stats
# the sound a turtle these objects hit makes, which turtle_hit reads off the object that hit it (01C2DE "move.w
# ($28,A6), D0"): from the record (0180F2 "move.w ($8,A6), ($28,A0)"); the enemies keep their move there
scoped object set_object_stats 28 hit_sound u16

# the record set_object_stats reads, 10 bytes (0180E0 "move.w ($0,A6), ($6,A0)": attack and hit; 0180E6 "move.l ($2,A6),
# ($8,A0)": health_bars, health and damage; 0180EC "move.w ($6,A6), ($34,A0)": depth; 0180F2 "move.w ($8,A6), ($28,A0)":
# the hit sound), 50 of them from 0180FA to 0182ED, each kind's start loading its own into A6 ("lea $18104.l, A6" and
# the like, then "bsr $180e0")
struct object_stats 0A rom
field object_stats 00 attack u8
field object_stats 01 hit u8
field object_stats 02 health_bars u8
field object_stats 03 health u8
field object_stats 04 damage u16

# the bosses' target: Krang, the rock soldiers, Baxter Stockman and the flying drones go after one turtle, whose object
# they keep at +20 (011E5C "move.l A6, ($20,A0)")
scoped object boss 20 target u32 object
# the explosions of a defeated boss so far: boss_start_explosion places the next one by it (010EF0 "move.b ($2f,A0), D7")
scoped object boss 2F explosions u8
# one of the turtles in play (0600A1 their bits), not out of the game (0600A0) and not being hit (011E26 "cmpi.b #$4,
# ($4,A6)"), at random (011E46 "jsr $48448.l"): it goes to target, and the boss aims at it; D7 0 when there is none
011DFA  func  boss_pick_target
# x_speed and y_speed toward the target, from a table by boss_direction_to_target (011E70 "lea $11eb2.l, A6" for kind
# 1E, 011E80 "lea $11ef2.l, A6" for the others), and the boss turned the way it goes (011EAA "ori.b #$40, ($0,A0)")
011E6A  func  boss_aim_at_target
# the direction (0-15) from the boss to its target: by the signs of the differences across and up or down and by
# their ratio (0184D2 "movea.l ($20,A0), A5", 0184E2 "move.w ($c,A5), D0", 0184E6 "sub.w ($c,A0), D0", then "mulu.w
# #$3e8" and "divu.w" into the table at 018550)
0184D2  func  boss_direction_to_target

# ======== the sound queue (0091A4-009200) ========
# the sound or voice D7 into the queue the frames send to the sound board (0091D4 on): not while 060032 bit 2 or 0600A3
# is set (0091A4 "btst #$2, $60032.l", 0091B0 "tst.b $600a3.l"), never the musics 60-6B (0091BA "cmpi.b #$60, D7",
# 0091C2 "cmpi.b #$6b, D7"), and only when 060092 lets sounds play (0091CA "tst.b $60092.l")
0091A4  func  queue_sound
# D7 into the queue at 060400-06043F, the next place kept at 060018 (0091D4 "movea.l $60018.l, A6", 0091DA "move.b D7,
# (A6)+", 0091E6 "lea $60400.l, A6")
0091D4  func  queue_sound_command
# queue_sound with the sound in D0 (0091F8 "move.b D0, D7", 0091FA "bsr $91a4")
0091F4  func  queue_sound_d0

# ======== Krang (kind 65, area 9; and kinds 66, 67, 68) ========
# kind 65 krang: Krang's android body (area 9, one spawn record at 05F3F0): rendered (00EC9C on: a huge humanoid body
#   with Krang in its belly); it waits for the curtain (kind 68) to go, walks at the turtles, kicks a turtle close by,
#   punches with a fist that flies off its arm (kind 66), fires laser bolts (kind 67) at a turtle in front; with no hit
#   left it bursts in ten explosions and Krang flies off in his pod (00ECC0-00ECC8: his head alone, then flying),
#   saying say_im_invincible; it counts as an enemy (06120C) until he is gone
# kind 66 krang_fist: the fist that flies off Krang's arm across the screen at 0x680, knocking turtles flying
#   (00EB8C starts it: 00EBA4 "move.w #$6600, ($2,A6)"; its record 0182E4: attack 87)
# kind 67 krang_laser: a laser bolt from Krang, in a stream of five down to the floor at one of three angles (00EA20
#   starts them: 00EA26 "move.w #$6700, ($2,A6)"; rendered 00F760: a thin slanting bolt, the last one a spark)
# kind 68 krang_curtain: a striped curtain where Krang appears (area 9, one record at 05F3E8, at Krang's place): it
#   shimmers (015E02), breaks up with sound 16 (015E06), and when it is gone Krang starts (00DFF6 reads its flags
#   through 061314)

# the hits Krang takes: health_bars plus 2 a player in the game (00DFB0 "move.b ($8,A0), ($24,A0)", 00DFCC "add.b D7,
# ($24,A0)"), one less a hit (00E22C "subq.b #1, ($24,A0)"); under 30 he flashes and kicks with another animation
# (00E7EE "cmpi.b #$1e, ($24,A0)", 00E6BA)
scoped object krang 24 hits_left u8
# frames before he can be hit again after a hit (00E2C8 "move.b #$c, ($2a,A0)", 00E7E0 "subq.b #1, ($2a,A0)", 00E7E8
# "ori.b #$2, ($0,A0)")
scoped object krang 2A hit_delay u8
# 0x80 while he is hurt (00E220 "move.b #$80, ($2d,A0)"), cleared when he recovers (00E2C4 "clr.b ($2d,A0)"); nothing
# reads it
scoped object krang 2D hurt u8
# the turtle close to him: its bit and bit 7 when it is to his left (00E8B2 "ori.b #$80, D0", 00E8CC "bset D6, D0",
# 00E066 "move.b D0, ($2e,A0)"); his kick turns to it (00E640 "tst.b ($2e,A0)")
scoped object krang 2E close_turtle u8
# the angle of his lasers, 0 the shallowest, 2 the steepest (00E674 "move.b #$2, ($2f,A0)", 00E68C "move.b #$1,
# ($2f,A0)"; 00EA8C "move.b ($2f,A0), ($3,A6)": the lasers' parameter)
scoped object krang 2F laser_angle u8
# frames counted down: after his lasers (00E1F2 "move.b #$14, ($30,A0)", 00E200 "subq.b #1, ($30,A0)"), before he
# bursts (00E300 "move.b #$8c, ($30,A0)"), between his explosions (00E33C "move.b #$e, ($30,A0)")
scoped object krang 30 timer u8
# frames left of his walk (00E6F8 "move.b (A6,D7.w), ($31,A0)", 00E09C "subq.b #1, ($31,A0)")
scoped object krang 31 move_time u8
# his explosions so far, to ten (00E384 "addq.b #1, ($2f,A0)", 00E388 "cmpi.b #$a, ($2f,A0)")
scoped object krang_explode 2F explosions u8

# Krang who fired it (00EBE4 "move.l A0, ($20,A6)"); nothing reads it
scoped object krang_fist 20 shooter u32 object
# bit 7: it flies to the left (00EBCC "ori.b #$80, ($2f,A6)", 00EC22 "tst.b ($2f,A0)")
scoped object krang_fist 2F flipped u8
# Krang who fired it (00EA78 "move.l A0, ($20,A6)"); nothing reads it
scoped object krang_laser 20 shooter u32 object

scoped object krang_laser 2F flipped u8

scoped object krang_laser 30 delay u8

scoped object krang_laser 31 last u8

# "ori.b #$40, ($0,A0)")
00E724  func  krang_set_speeds
# hit_delay counted down to his being hittable (00E7E0 "subq.b #1, ($2a,A0)"); under 30 hits he flashes, faster under 20
# and 10 (00E7F8 "moveq #$3c, D7", 00E804 "moveq #$8, D7", 00E810 "moveq #$1, D7")
00E7D0  func  krang_count_down
# move_by_speeds, kept within 0x1000-0x8000 up and down the screen and pushed on past the left edge (00E838 "move.l
# #$8000, ($16,A0)", 00E84C "move.l #$1000, ($16,A0)", 00E85C "addi.l #$800, ($12,A0)")
00E826  func  krang_move
# a turtle in play within 0x0B up or down and 0x3A across of him (00E89E "cmpi.w #$b, D5", 00E8B8 "cmpi.w #$3a, D5")
00E866  func  krang_find_close_turtle
# a turtle in play within 0x0B up or down and 0x3E or more in front of him (00E910 "btst #$6, ($0,A0)", 00E940 "cmpi.w
# #$3e, D5")
00E8D0  func  krang_find_turtle_in_front
# the laser D0 of the stream: in use, kind 67 (00EA26 "move.w #$6700, ($2,A6)"), at Krang's place plus the offsets of
# the table at 00EA9E by the angle (00EA3C "lea $ea9e.l, A5"), flipped with him, the angle its parameter (00EA8C
# "move.b ($2f,A0), ($3,A6)"); one more laser on its way (00EA96 "addq.b #1, $6131a.l")
00EA20  func  krang_start_laser
# the fist from the free objects 24-31 (00EB8C "lea $62780.l, A6"), kind 66, 0x45 in front of him at height 0x32
# (00EBBA "move.w #$4500, D7", 00EBDE "move.w #$3200, ($10,A6)"); it sets his own flip, not the fist's (00EBF2 "ori.b
# #$40, ($0,A0)"), which he has already
00EB8C  func  krang_start_fist

# -- kind 66: Krang's fist --
# routine 0: drawn, its stats (00EC04 "lea $182e4.l, A6"), 0x680 across (00EC16 "move.w #$680, ($1a,A0)"), the other
# way when flipped (00EC36 "not.w ($1a,A0)")
00EBFE  func  krang_fist_start
# routine 1: when it has come off, flying (00EC54 "movea.l $f678.l, A6"), sound 06, hurting (00EC6A "ori.b #$4,
# ($0,A0)")
00EC4C  func  krang_fist_launch
# routine 2: across until it is off the screen (00EC76 "btst #$5, ($0,A0)"); then Krang's punch is over (06131B)
00EC76  func  krang_fist_fly

# -- kind 67: Krang's lasers --
# routine 0: drawn and hurting (00EAB6 "ori.b #$84, ($0,A0)"), its speeds and animation by the angle (00EACE "lea
# $eb1e.l, A6", 00EB00 "lea $f760.l, A6")
00EAB6  func  krang_laser_start
# routine 1: its delay (00EB2A "subq.b #1, ($30,A0)"); then on its way (00EB36 "subq.b #1, $6131a.l")
00EB2A  func  krang_laser_wait
# routine 2: down to the floor (00EB44 "tst.w ($10,A0)"); the last one sparks there (00EB64 "movea.l $f76c.l, A6"), the
# others are gone
00EB3E  func  krang_laser_fly
# routine 3: gone when the spark ends (00EB70 "tst.b ($33,A0)")
00EB70  func  krang_laser_spark

# -- kind 68: the curtain --
# routine 0: drawn, colour 8 (00E968 "move.w #$800, ($42,A0)"), shimmering (00E97A "movea.l $15e02.l, A6"); Krang
# watches it (00E986 "move.l A0, $61314.l")
00E962  func  krang_curtain_start
# routine 1: when that ends, sound 16 and it breaks up (00E99A "move.w #$16, D7", 00E9A4 "movea.l $15e06.l, A6")
00E992  func  krang_curtain_open
# routine 2: gone when that ends (00E9B6 "tst.b ($33,A0)", 00E9BE "jmp $1832a.l")
00E9B6  func  krang_curtain_end

# ======== the sewer's traps (area 3: kinds 50, 51, 52) ========
# kind 52 spiked_pole: six spiked poles hanging in a row across the sewer (area 3, the records at 05DDFC-05DE24, 0x0B
#   apart, height 0x80; rendered 01583C: a pole with spikes), which drop to just over the floor and rise again, all
#   together (061310 their phase), knocking turtles flying (its record 018258: attack 07); every 16 frames the ones
#   that hurt swap (011284 "bchg #$2, ($0,A0)"): those with an odd parameter start hurting (011126 "ori.b #$4,
#   ($0,A0)"); the first (parameter 80) starts them when it is on the screen, the ones with a parameter time them
# kind 51 sewer_launcher: something under the sewer's water (area 3, two records, not drawn: 01129C "bclr #$7,
#   ($0,A0)") that drifts up and down the screen; when a turtle is level with it and 0x30 or more across, ripples show
#   (0157A4) and a missile (kind 50) leaps out at it, then it hides 100 frames; twelve missiles and it is gone
# kind 50 sewer_missile: the missile: it rises out of the water to height 0x20 (011498-0114CC), then flies across at
#   0x480 at the turtle, knocking it flying (its record 01824E: attack 07); a turtle's hit bursts it (014C3A)

# frames counted down: the phases' times kept by the poles with a parameter (011188 "move.b #$a, ($30,A0)", 0111EE
# "move.b #$28, ($30,A0)", 011268 "move.b #$1e, ($30,A0)", 01119C "subq.b #1, ($30,A0)")
scoped object spiked_pole 30 timer u8
# bit 0: it drifts down the screen, else up; turned at y 0x800 and 0x4000 (011380 "move.b ($2f,A0), D7", 0113B6 "bchg
# #$0, ($2f,A0)")
scoped object sewer_launcher 2F drift u8
# frames before it fires: 40 at first, 100 after each missile (0112B8 "move.b #$28, ($30,A0)", 011354 "move.b #$64,
# ($30,A0)", 011330 "subq.b #1, ($30,A0)")
scoped object sewer_launcher 30 delay u8
# the missiles still to fire, less one (0112BE "move.b #$c, ($31,A0)", 01135E "subq.b #1, ($31,A0)")
scoped object sewer_launcher 31 missiles_left u8
# 1 when its turtle is to the right (011470 "move.b #$1, ($2f,A6)"): it flies right (0114DE "tst.b ($2f,A0)")
scoped object sewer_missile 2F goes_right u8

# -- kind 52: the spiked poles --
# routine 0: drawn, hurting by the parameter, its stats (01112C "lea $18258.l, A6"), the phase 0 (011142 "clr.b
# $61310.l")
011116  func  spiked_pole_start
# routine 1: the first starts the drops when it is under 0x160 on the screen (011166 "cmpi.w #$160, ($c,A0)", 011170
# "move.b #$1, $61310.l"); then all of them wait 10 frames
01115A  func  spiked_pole_wait
# routine 2: up; the timers' frames, then phase 2: the drop at 0x600 (0111A4 "move.b #$2, $61310.l", 0111B8 "move.w
# #$600, ($1e,A0)")
011190  func  spiked_pole_hold_up
# routine 3: down to height 0x18 (0111CE "sub.w D7, ($10,A0)", 0111D2 "cmpi.w #$1800, ($10,A0)"); sound 24 and 40 frames
# down (0111FC "move.b #$24, D7")
0111C6  func  spiked_pole_drop
# routine 4: down; then phase 4: up at 0x300 (01121C "move.b #$4, $61310.l", 011230 "move.w #$300, ($1e,A0)")
011208  func  spiked_pole_hold_down
# routine 5: up to height 0x80 (01124A "cmpi.w #-$8000, ($10,A0)"), routine 2 again with 30 frames (01125A "move.b #$2,
# ($4,A0)", 011268 "move.b #$1e, ($30,A0)")
01123E  func  spiked_pole_rise
# every 16 frames it hurts or stops hurting (011276 "move.b $600a5.l, D7", 011284 "bchg #$2, ($0,A0)")
011276  func  spiked_pole_swap_hurting

# -- kind 51: the launcher under the water --
# routine 0: not drawn, its stats (0112A2 "lea $181a4.l, A6"), 40 frames, 12 missiles (0112BE "move.b #$c, ($31,A0)")
01129C  func  sewer_launcher_start
# routine 1: drifting; after its delay, a turtle in line (0112F2 "bsr $113c6"): a missile from the free objects 32-39
# (0112FE "lea $62a00.l, A6", 011316 "bsr $1144a"), and the ripples drawn (01131E "ori.b #$80, ($0,A0)", 011324
# "movea.l $157a4.l, A6")
0112DA  func  sewer_launcher_wait
# routine 2: when the ripples end, hidden 100 frames (01134E "bclr #$7, ($0,A0)", 011354 "move.b #$64, ($30,A0)");
# with no missile left, gone (01135E "subq.b #1, ($31,A0)")
01133A  func  sewer_launcher_fired
# routine 3, and gone off the screen or out of missiles (011374 "bra $1832a")
011374  func  sewer_launcher_remove
# up or down the screen by drift (011378 "lea $113be.l, A6", 01139A "add.l D7, ($16,A0)"), turned at its edges
011378  func  sewer_launcher_drift
# a turtle in play within 4 up or down and 0x30 or more across (0113FE "cmpi.w #$4, D5", 01141A "cmpi.w #$30, D5"): its
# bit, bit 7 when it is to the left
0113C6  func  sewer_launcher_find_turtle
# the missile: kind 50 (011450 "move.w #$5000, ($2,A6)") at the launcher's place, turned to the turtle (011476 "ori.b
# #$40, ($0,A6)")
01144A  func  sewer_launcher_start_missile

# -- kind 50: the missile --
# routine 0: drawn, hurting, can be hit (01147E "ori.b #$86, ($0,A0)"), its stats (011484 "lea $1824e.l, A6"), rising
# at 0x200 (01149E "move.w #$200, ($1e,A0)"), sound 0E
01147E  func  sewer_missile_start
# routine 1: up to height 0x20, then across at 0x480 (0114CC "cmpi.w #$2000, ($10,A0)", 0114DA "move.w #$fb80, D7")
0114C6  func  sewer_missile_rise
# routine 2: across until it is off the screen (0114F6 "bsr $182ee", 0114FA "bra $1831e")
0114F6  func  sewer_missile_fly
# routine 3: gone (0114FE "bra $1832a")
0114FE  func  sewer_missile_remove
# routine 4, struck: it neither hurts nor can be hit (011502 "bclr #$2, ($0,A0)", 011508 "bclr #$1, ($0,A0)"), sound
# 0F and its burst (01151C "movea.l $14c3a.l, A6")
011502  func  sewer_missile_struck
# routine 5: gone when the burst ends (011528 "tst.b ($33,A0)")
011528  func  sewer_missile_vanish

# ---- comments ----
comment set_object_stats an object's attack, health, depth and the sound a turtle it hits makes, from its record
comment move_by_speeds moves the object by its speeds across, up or down the screen, and in height
comment find_free_object finds an object not in use among the next ones
comment remove_if_off_screen the object is gone once it is off the screen
comment remove_object the object is gone: out of use
comment place_at_object puts the other object where this one is
comment boss_pick_target picks one of the turtles in play at random as the boss's target and aims at it; none, none picked
comment boss_aim_at_target sets the boss's speeds toward its target and turns it that way
comment boss_direction_to_target the direction, one of sixteen, from the boss to its target
comment queue_sound queues a sound or a voice for the sound board, when the game lets sounds play; never a music
comment queue_sound_command puts a command into the sound board's queue
comment queue_sound_d0 queue_sound with the sound in another register
comment krang_appear Krang's appearance, step by step
comment krang_appear_setup Krang stands ready, with his hits by the number of players
comment krang_appear_wait Krang waits for the curtain to go, then raises his arms
comment krang_appear_end Krang can be hit and starts his first move
comment krang_walk Krang walks; with a turtle close by, he may kick it, fire steep lasers or pose instead
comment krang_walk_at_target Krang walks at his target a while
comment krang_walk_on Krang walks on at his target a while
comment krang_charge Krang runs at his target a while
comment krang_pose_end Krang's pose ends and he picks his next move
comment krang_walk_step every 16 frames Krang aims again, and he moves every other frame
comment krang_next_move with a turtle in front of him Krang fires lasers, punches or poses; else he picks another walk
comment krang_melee Krang's kick or punch, step by step
comment krang_kick_end Krang's kick ends and he walks again
comment krang_punch_throw Krang throws his arm out and his fist flies off it
comment krang_punch_wait Krang waits for his fist to fly off the screen
comment krang_fire_lasers Krang's lasers, step by step
comment krang_lasers_fire Krang fires a stream of five lasers
comment krang_lasers_wait Krang waits for all his lasers to be on their way
comment krang_lasers_end after his lasers Krang walks again
comment krang_struck a turtle's attack takes one of Krang's hits; with none left he starts to burst
comment krang_recoil Krang recovers from a hit and goes on
comment krang_explode Krang's end, step by step
comment krang_explode_start Krang stops, with his shadow, before he bursts
comment krang_explode_wait Krang's body falls apart
comment krang_explode_burst an explosion bursts on Krang's body as it floats
comment krang_explode_next the next of Krang's ten explosions
comment krang_escape Krang's escape, step by step
comment krang_escape_start Krang's head comes out in his pod, saying he is invincible
comment krang_escape_wait the pod floats a while, then takes off
comment krang_escape_dip the pod dips, slower and slower
comment krang_escape_fly the pod flies up off the screen
comment krang_gone Krang is gone: one enemy less
comment krang_hover Krang floats up and down
comment krang_pick_move picks Krang's next walk, charge or pose by its chances
comment krang_start_walk Krang picks a turtle and walks at it; with none, he poses
comment krang_start_walk_on Krang walks on at his target
comment krang_start_charge Krang runs at his target
comment krang_start_pose Krang poses, with his cry the first time in a row
comment krang_start_kick Krang kicks the turtle close to him
comment krang_start_lasers_steep Krang aims his lasers steeply, at a turtle close by
comment krang_start_lasers Krang aims his lasers at the middle angle
comment krang_start_lasers_far Krang aims his lasers at the shallowest angle, far in front of him
comment krang_start_punch Krang draws back his arm to punch
comment krang_set_move_time how long Krang's walk lasts, at random
comment krang_aim_charge aims Krang's run at his target
comment krang_aim_walk aims Krang's walk at his target
comment krang_set_speeds Krang's speeds in the direction picked, and he turns that way
comment krang_count_down Krang can be hit again after a while; weak, he flashes faster and faster
comment krang_move Krang moves, kept on the floor and on the screen
comment krang_find_close_turtle which turtle is close to Krang, and on which side
comment krang_find_turtle_in_front which turtle is level with Krang and in front of him, far enough
comment krang_start_laser one laser of Krang's stream, from his body at the angle he aims
comment krang_start_fist Krang's fist flies off his arm
comment krang_fist_start Krang's fist sets off across the screen
comment krang_fist_launch the fist comes off and flies, hurting
comment krang_fist_fly the fist flies across; off the screen, Krang's punch is over
comment krang_laser_start a laser of Krang's stream, at its angle
comment krang_laser_wait the laser waits its turn in the stream
comment krang_laser_fly the laser goes down to the floor; the stream's last one sparks there
comment krang_laser_spark the laser's spark ends and it is gone
comment krang_curtain_start the curtain where Krang appears shimmers
comment krang_curtain_open the curtain breaks up
comment krang_curtain_end the curtain is gone, and Krang starts
comment spiked_pole_start a spiked pole hangs over the sewer
comment spiked_pole_wait the first pole on the screen starts the row dropping
comment spiked_pole_hold_up the poles wait up a while, then drop
comment spiked_pole_drop the pole drops to just over the floor
comment spiked_pole_hold_down the poles wait down a while, then rise
comment spiked_pole_rise the pole rises back up
comment spiked_pole_swap_hurting the poles that hurt and the ones that do not swap now and then
comment sewer_launcher_start something lies hidden under the sewer's water
comment sewer_launcher_wait it drifts under the water; with a turtle in line, a missile leaps out at it
comment sewer_launcher_fired its ripples end and it hides a while; out of missiles, it is gone
comment sewer_launcher_remove the launcher is gone
comment sewer_launcher_drift it drifts up and down the screen under the water
comment sewer_launcher_find_turtle which turtle is in line with it across the water, far enough
comment sewer_launcher_start_missile a missile leaps out at the turtle
comment sewer_missile_start the missile rises out of the water
comment sewer_missile_rise the missile rises, then flies at the turtle
comment sewer_missile_fly the missile flies across until it leaves the screen
comment sewer_missile_remove the missile is gone
comment sewer_missile_struck a turtle's attack bursts the missile
comment sewer_missile_vanish the missile's burst ends and it is gone

# ---- the pointers where these objects' code works them out ----
# the lasers krang_lasers_fire starts (find_free_object from the objects 32-39) and krang_start_laser fills
local krang_lasers_fire 00E9D8,00E9E4 a6 laser object.krang_laser
param krang_start_laser a6 laser object.krang_laser
# the fist krang_start_fist starts (find_free_object from the objects 24-31)
local krang_start_fist 00EB8C,00EB98 a6 fist object.krang_fist
# the missile the launcher starts
param sewer_launcher_start_missile a6 missile object.sewer_missile


# ======== the scenery, the hazards and the explosions (00F8DA-011115) ========
# The kinds of the kind table (053896) whose routines are at 00F8DA-011115: scenery of the areas a turtle can break or
# knock away, hazards that hit the turtles (and the enemies: the attack check's third loop), and the explosions. What
# each kind is comes from its code, its spawn records (area_spawn_lists: +6 the kind, +7 the parameter) and its
# animations, rendered from the addresses its set_animation calls take. Their stats come from set_object_stats'
# 10-byte records (+0 attack and hit, +2 health_bars, health and damage, +6 depth, +8 the hit sound). "Struck" is
# routine 4, which the attack check sets on an object a turtle's attack hits (0472CE "move.w #$400, ($4,A1)"); "gone
# off the screen" is remove_if_off_screen (flags bit 5). A turtle's reaction to an attack is turtle_hit's table at
# 01C272: attack 04 knocked down, 07 knocked flying, 09 frozen in a block of ice (the turtle's state 1C, then 1D
# breaking out: rendered), 0A electrocuted (state 1E, the skeleton flashing: rendered), 11-13 turtle_refill_health.
#
# kind 63 ball_dropper: an unseen spot high up in area 0 (one spawn record, height C0) that drops four balls (kind
#   60) as the screen reaches it: screen x 0x140, 0x100, 0xD0, 0xA0 (the table at 00F938 by balls_left)
# kind 60 ball: a dark ball that drops from the dropper's height and bounces away, back and to the right, knocking
#   flying the turtles it hits (attack 07); it cannot be hit (00F94C starts it: 00F952 "move.w #$6000, ($2,A6)")
# kind 61 road_barrier: a striped barrier lying across the highway (area 5, six records): parameter 0 the barrier,
#   drawn and harmless; parameter 1 one of its hit boxes, not drawn, knocking turtles down (attack 04)
# kind 62 tire_stack: tires lying on the highway (area 5, four records): two stacked by parameter 0, one by 1;
#   harmless, it cannot be hit
# kind 6E technodrome_ball: the ball of area 8 (ten records along a diagonal, at heights in a wave): it moves along
#   the diagonal every frame, drops in and bounces off to the right, knocking turtles flying (attack 87)
# kind 20 hydrant: a fire hydrant of area 2 (two records): when its four bars are gone it breaks and sprays four jets
#   of water (kind 21) the way the last hit sent it
# kind 21 hydrant_water: a jet of water from a broken hydrant, weaker each time (three animations, 0144E8 by its
#   jet), flying across at 0x580 (00FCD0 starts it: 00FCD6 "move.w #$2100, ($2,A6)")
# kinds 22, 59 and 5A roadside: things by the road a turtle's hit sends flying, knocking down what they hit (attack
#   84): kind 22 a parking meter (area 2, five records; its record 01452A), kind 59 a "20 MPH" speed limit sign on a
#   pole (area 4, one record; 01456A), kind 5A a traffic cone (area 4, six records; 015AF6); each record holds the
#   standing animation and then the one knocked flying
# kind 23 rat: a sewer rat (area 3, five records) that drops in, then runs, stops with a squeak, runs again; harmless
# kind 24 alligator: an alligator swimming slowly left in the sewer's water (area 3, one record), which snaps its jaws
#   with sound 12 when it is under 0xC0 on the screen; harmless
# kind 25 skateboarder: a woman on a skateboard gliding across the street (area 2, one record; the board under her
#   feet in both frames): a turtle's hit makes her duck with a cry (speech BA); no scenario runs it
# kind 26 pizza: a pizza box (areas 2-5 one each, areas 7 and 8 three each, all parameter 02): taken, it opens with
#   its name and refills the turtle's health: its attack 11-13 by the parameter (the records at 010148) is turtle_hit's
#   turtle_refill_health; the opened boxes say SPEEDY, WHIRLY and ENERGY (parameters 0, 1, 2)
# kind 27 billboard: a "Stunner" billboard high up in area 2 (one record, height 0x40): at one of four places on the
#   screen two Foot soldiers (kind 2A) jump down from it and it topples onto the turtles (attack 07), then lies drawn
#   in the layer; no scenario runs it
# kind 2A billboard_soldier: a Foot soldier who jumps down from the billboard and then is a boomerang soldier (kind
#   0B) (010306 starts it: 01030C "move.w #$2a00, ($2,A6)")
# kind 28 oil_drum: a "DANGER" oil drum (area 4 two, area 7 three): when its three bars are gone its fuse is lit, it
#   blows up (an explosion of kind 33, knocking flying) and leaves its wreck (kind 2D)
# kind 2D oil_drum (wreck): the burnt drum left after the explosion (0104B6 "move.w #$2d00, ($2,A0)"), until it leaves
#   the screen
# kinds 29, 2B and 37 turret: a gun on top of a tall lattice column (area 7 two, area 8 three, all kind 2B; 29 and 37
#   have the same table, 01053C, and nothing starts them), which slides back and forth in depth and fires bursts of
#   four shots (kind 2C); when its four bars are gone it is destroyed and seven explosions (kind 31) climb its column
# kind 2C turret_shot: the turret's shot, four beads in a row, flying at 0x800 and knocking turtles down (attack 04),
#   a burst when it hits (0107C0 starts it: 0107C6 "move.w #$2c00, ($2,A6)")
# kind 4B waiting_soldier: a Foot soldier of area 7 (one record) standing with his back turned, blade raised, who is a
#   knife soldier (kind 03) once he is under 0x140 on the screen or a turtle hits him; no scenario runs it
# kind 5B one_way_sign: a ONE WAY sign on a pole in area 4 (one record): a turtle's hit spins it, and while it spins it
#   knocks flying what it hits (attack 07); it cannot be broken (its bars back to 16 at each hit)
# kind 2F nozzle_row: two little triangles on the Technodrome's floor (area 8, five records 0x40 apart), each the
#   head of a row of five nozzle places going into the screen (the lists at 010AB2 by the parameter): a turtle within
#   0x40 to its left and level with one of them makes a nozzle (kind 2E) come up there
# kind 2E freeze_nozzle: a nozzle that comes up out of the floor and sprays a cloud of gas to the left that freezes
#   turtles in ice (attack 09), then sinks back; it can be broken (010AF0 starts it: 010AF6 "move.w #$2e00, ($2,A6)")
# kind 30 electric_beam (electric): a tall vertical bolt of lightning in the Technodrome (area 8, two records), which
#   comes for 60 frames after a random wait and electrocutes turtles (attack 0A)
# kind 39 electric_vent (electric): an unseen spot on the floor of area 7 (three records) that now and then shoots an
#   electric column (kind 3A) up
# kind 3A electric_column (electric): a column that shoots up out of the floor, blinks, and goes on up out of sight,
#   electrocuting turtles (attack 0A, the electric beam's record values) (010D7A starts it: 010D80 "move.w #$3a00,
#   ($2,A6)")
# kind 40 grille: a slanted grille in two pieces (its frames); no spawn list, record or code starts kind 40, and the
#   address it leaves in 061302 (010E64 "move.l A0, $61302.l") nothing reads
# kind 31 explosion (small): a small explosion (up to 32 pixels); started harmless by the turret's wreck and the
#   broken nozzle (010ED6 "move.w #$3100, ($2,A6)"), attacking only by code no path reaches (011006)
# kind 32 explosion (big): a big explosion (up to 128 pixels), for the bosses' defeats (010F86 "move.w #$3200,
#   ($2,A6)"; also 010FD4)
# kind 33 explosion (drum): a big explosion, attacking (010F96 "move.w #$500, ($0,A6)", 010F9C "move.w #$3300,
#   ($2,A6)"): only the oil drum starts it
# kind 42 explosion (rocket): the rocket's explosion's animation (015258, which the rocket sets itself, 04C2EE
#   "movea.l $15258.l, A6", and so does code at 012824 and 013334); nothing starts kind 42
# kind 57 explosion (medium): an explosion up to 64 pixels (01101C "move.w #$5700, ($2,A6)"), started only from
#   func_013656 (0136B4 "bne $11016"), which no scenario runs
# All five explosions knock flying what they hit when they are started attacking (their record 018168: attack 07,
# damage 0x510).
#
# Three functions start in the middle of an instruction (00FA00, 00FFC0, 00FFE0) and one at the return of a call
# (00FC00): only run_object_state's switch holds them (src/objects/states.c), the analysis taking those values for
# state handlers; nothing in the game jumps there. They are named by what running them would do.
# 010FEE (explode_small) is a routine no path reaches: the analysis takes it from its func line here.

# ---- the families' fields ----

# ---- comments ----
comment ball_dropper_start an unseen spot high up that will drop four balls
comment ball_dropper_wait a ball drops each time the screen reaches the next place; the dropper is gone after the last
comment ball_dropper_drop_ball a ball at the dropper's place
comment ball_start a dark ball, high up
comment ball_drop the ball drops and starts bouncing away, knocking turtles flying
comment ball_bounce the ball bounces on until it is gone at the area's back
comment ball_land Unused as an entry: only the dispatcher's switch has it, in the middle of an instruction; the ball's landing sound without a new bounce
comment road_barrier_start a barrier across the highway, or one of its unseen parts that knock turtles down
comment road_barrier_stay the barrier stays until it leaves the screen
comment tire_stack_start tires lying on the highway, two or one by the parameter
comment tire_stack_stay the tires stay until they leave the screen
comment technodrome_ball_start a ball high up in the Technodrome
comment technodrome_ball_drop the ball drops and starts bouncing off to the right, knocking turtles flying
comment technodrome_ball_bounce the ball bounces on until it is past the screen's right edge
comment technodrome_ball_drift the ball moves along the area's diagonal
comment hydrant_start a fire hydrant with four bars
comment hydrant_stay the hydrant stays until it leaves the screen
comment hydrant_spray the broken hydrant sprays a jet of water every few frames until jets_left runs out
comment hydrant_spray_jet a jet of water if there is room for it, else the spray ends
comment hydrant_struck a turtle's hit sparks on the hydrant, or breaks it and starts the spray the way the hit sent it
comment hydrant_recover the hydrant can be hit again
comment hydrant_start_water a jet of water from the hydrant, weaker as the jets go
comment hydrant_water_start a jet of water flies across, hitting what it meets
comment hydrant_water_fly the water flies on until it leaves the screen
comment roadside_cone_start a traffic cone
comment roadside_pole_start a parking meter or a speed limit sign
comment roadside_stand the thing stands by the road, and a turtle can hit it
comment roadside_stay it stays until it leaves the screen
comment roadside_fly it flies on until it leaves the screen
comment roadside_speed_sign_struck a turtle's hit sends the speed limit sign flying
comment roadside_cone_struck a turtle's hit sends the traffic cone flying
comment roadside_parking_meter_struck a turtle's hit sends the parking meter flying
comment roadside_knock_away it flies off the way the hit sent it, knocking down what it hits
comment rat_start a sewer rat, which drops in or runs
comment rat_drop the rat drops in and sits
comment rat_sit the rat sits a while, then runs
comment rat_run the rat runs, and is gone once it leaves the screen
comment rat_run_on Unused as an entry: only the dispatcher's switch has it, in the middle of an instruction; the rat's run
comment rat_run_step the rat runs a while, then stops with a squeak
comment rat_squeak Unused as an entry: only the dispatcher's switch has it, in the middle of an instruction; the rat squeaks and sits
comment rat_random_run_time how long the rat runs, at random
comment alligator_start an alligator swims slowly in the sewer's water
comment alligator_swim the alligator swims, and snaps its jaws as it comes on
comment alligator_snap the alligator swims on
comment move_across_screen it moves across until it leaves the screen
comment skateboarder_start a woman on a skateboard glides across the street
comment skateboarder_idle nothing
comment skateboarder_glide she glides on until she leaves the screen
comment skateboarder_struck a turtle's hit makes her duck with a cry
comment pizza_start a pizza box, whose taking refills a turtle's health
comment pizza_wait as the pizza comes on the screen it is announced; a turtle that takes it gets its health back
comment pizza_vanish the opened box is gone
comment billboard_start a billboard high up, with Foot soldiers behind it
comment billboard_wait two Foot soldiers jump down and the billboard topples onto the turtles
comment billboard_fall the billboard falls to the ground with a crash
comment billboard_land the fallen billboard stays in the background
comment billboard_start_soldier a Foot soldier on the billboard, counted as an enemy
comment billboard_soldier_start the soldier crouches on the billboard
comment billboard_soldier_wait the soldier jumps
comment billboard_soldier_jump_down the soldier comes down to the ground and lands
comment billboard_soldier_become_boomerang_soldier the soldier goes on as a Foot soldier with a boomerang
comment oil_drum_start an oil drum with three bars
comment oil_drum_stay the drum, or its wreck, stays until it leaves the screen
comment oil_drum_blow_up the drum blows up, knocking flying what is near
comment oil_drum_become_wreck the burnt wreck of the drum is left
comment oil_drum_struck a turtle's hit makes the drum flash, or lights its fuse
comment oil_drum_recover the drum can be hit again
comment turret_start a gun on a tall column, with four bars
comment turret_move the turret slides, waiting to fire
comment turret_fire the turret slides and fires a burst of four shots
comment turret_wreck the destroyed turret's explosions
comment turret_wreck_explode an explosion on the destroyed turret's column
comment turret_wreck_next the next explosion, higher up, until seven
comment turret_wreck_stay the wreck stays until it leaves the screen
comment turret_struck a turtle's hit makes the turret flash, or destroys it
comment turret_recover the turret can be hit again
comment turret_slide the turret slides back and forth in depth
comment turret_random_wait how long the turret waits before its next burst, at random
comment turret_start_shot a shot from the turret's gun
comment turret_shot_start the turret's shot flies the way the turret faces, knocking turtles down
comment turret_shot_fly the shot flies until it hits or leaves the screen
comment turret_shot_vanish the shot's burst ends and it is gone
comment waiting_soldier_start a Foot soldier standing with his back turned
comment waiting_soldier_wait the soldier waits until he is well on the screen
comment waiting_soldier_become_knife_soldier the soldier goes on as a Foot soldier with knives
comment one_way_sign_start a ONE WAY sign on a pole
comment one_way_sign_stay the sign stays until it leaves the screen
comment one_way_sign_spin the sign spins, knocking flying what it hits, until it stops
comment one_way_sign_idle nothing
comment one_way_sign_struck a turtle's hit sets the sign spinning
comment nozzle_row_start the marks of a row of nozzles in the Technodrome's floor
comment nozzle_row_run the row watches for turtles, waits for its nozzle, or is done
comment nozzle_row_watch a turtle near one of the row's places makes a nozzle come up there
comment nozzle_row_wait a nozzle is up
comment nozzle_row_done the row's nozzle was broken: no more come up
comment nozzle_row_start_nozzle a nozzle at one of the row's places
comment freeze_nozzle_start a nozzle comes up out of the floor
comment freeze_nozzle_rise the nozzle is up and can be hit
comment freeze_nozzle_spray the nozzle gets ready, then sprays
comment freeze_nozzle_spray_start the nozzle sprays a cloud that freezes turtles in ice
comment freeze_nozzle_spray_stop the spray stops and the nozzle sinks
comment freeze_nozzle_sink the nozzle is gone and its row watches again
comment freeze_nozzle_struck a turtle's hit breaks the nozzle in a small explosion
comment freeze_nozzle_broken the broken nozzle stays until it leaves the screen
comment electric_beam_start an electric beam, off for a while
comment electric_beam_wait the beam comes on and electrocutes turtles
comment electric_beam_shine the beam stays on a second, then goes off again
comment electric_vent_start a spot in the floor that shoots electric columns
comment electric_vent_wait now and then the vent shoots an electric column
comment electric_vent_wait_column the vent waits for its column to be gone
comment electric_random_wait how long the electric hazard waits, at random
comment electric_vent_start_column an electric column at the vent
comment electric_column_start an electric column shoots up out of the floor, electrocuting turtles
comment electric_column_rise the column is up
comment electric_column_flicker the column blinks a while, then goes
comment electric_column_leave the column goes on up out of sight and tells its vent
comment electric_column_blink the column shows on every other frame
comment grille_start Unused: a slanted grille
comment grille_idle Unused: nothing
comment start_small_explosion a small explosion, harmless
comment boss_start_explosion one of the explosions of a defeated boss, with its sound
comment start_big_explosion a big explosion, harmless
comment explode_small Unused: an attacking small explosion where the object is, with its sound
comment start_small_blast Unused: a small explosion that knocks flying what it hits
comment place_explosion the explosion where the object is, moved across and up
comment explosion_small_start a small explosion
comment explosion_big_start a big explosion
comment explosion_drum_start the oil drum's explosion
comment explosion_rocket_start Unused: an explosion like the rocket's
comment explosion_medium_start a medium explosion
comment explosion_end the explosion ends and it is gone

# ---- the pointers where these objects' code works them out ----
# the objects these functions start, in A6 from their callers (find_free_object): their fields by the new kind's
# family (the writes 00FD2E "ori.b #$80, ($2f,A6)", 010342 "ori.b #$80, ($2f,A6)", 0107FA "move.b #$1, ($2f,A6)",
# 010B14 "move.l A5, ($20,A6)", 010D9A "move.l A0, ($20,A6)")
param hydrant_start_water a6 water object.hydrant_water
param billboard_start_soldier a6 soldier object.billboard_soldier
param turret_start_shot a6 shot object.turret_shot
param nozzle_row_start_nozzle a6 nozzle object.freeze_nozzle
param electric_vent_start_column a6 column object.electric
# the vent the column tells it is gone (010E1E "movea.l ($20,A0), A6", 010E22 "move.b #$1, ($2f,A6)")
local electric_column_leave 010E1E a6 vent object.electric


# ======== the rock soldiers and their shots (01154E-012A2D) ========
# The two bosses made of rock (kinds 1E and 1D) and the objects their code starts. What each kind is comes from its
# code, its spawn records (area_spawn_lists: +6 the kind, +7 the parameter) and its animations, rendered from the
# addresses its set_animation calls take; nothing in the program names the two bosses, so they go by their areas. Their
# stats come from set_object_stats' 10-byte records (+0 attack and hit, +2 health_bars, health and damage, +6 depth, +8
# the hit sound). "Struck" is routine 4, which the attack check sets on an object a turtle's attack hits
# (0472CE "move.w #$400, ($4,A1)"); "gone off the screen" is flags bit 5 (remove_if_off_screen). The turtles they look
# for are the first four objects whose player is in play and not out (0600A1 and not 0600A0) and that are not being hit
# (routine 4: 011AE4 "btst D6, $600a1.l", 011AEE "btst D6, $600a0.l", 011AF8 "cmpi.b #$4, ($4,A6)").
#
# kind 1E rock_soldier_site: the boss of area 7, the building site (one spawn record, 05F10C, at height 0x80): a big
#   humanoid of rock with a gun (rendered: 014E52 aims it). He drops in from above once the scroll reaches 0x6E000,
#   lands shaking the screen to the boss music and "Crash the turtles!", then walks at a turtle, stands or jumps
#   (landing with the screen shaking), strikes a turtle close by, and fires bursts of gun blasts (kind 34) at a turtle
#   in front within 0x8C. He takes 40 hits (his stats' bars) and 2 more for each player in play
#   (011588 "move.b ($8,A0), ($24,A0)", 0115A6 "lsl.w #1, D7"); every hit takes one. No scenario reaches him (index.txt:
#   0 ran).
# kind 1D rock_soldier_technodrome: the boss of area 8, the Technodrome (one spawn record, 05F3DE): a rock soldier with
#   a missile launcher (rendered: 01548A, a missile fired from it, the launcher's smoke ring). Unseen until the scroll
#   reaches 0xD1000, he appears with the boss music, a wall slab (kind 48) flying off and the shock bars (kinds 4C and
#   4D); then walks at a turtle, stands or taunts (speech A6, or B1 "Crash the turtles!"), strikes a turtle close by,
#   and fires a missile (kind 49) at a turtle in front at least 0x46 away. He takes 40 hits and one more for each player
#   (0121E6 "add.b D7, ($24,A0)"). The scenario area8 beats him.
# Both: struck, a flinch (or, with no hits left, the defeat: 10 explosions, then four pieces of rubble, kind 41, fall
#   off him and he stays as a heap of rocks: rendered 014F9E and 015672); his colour flashes more as he weakens. Kind 1E
#   runs his own moves (011C0E) and 1D his (012452) from the shared code by the kind (0119CE "cmpi.b #$1e, ($2,A0)"), so
#   the static analysis sees both kinds reach both.
# kind 34 gun_blast: kind 1E's gun blast (01186C starts it: 011FD2 "move.w #$3400, ($2,A6)"): 0x48 in front of him at
#   height 0x30, it flies at 0x400 for its animation's 22 frames (rendered 014FC2: a small blast that swells into a
#   burst), attack 0B (turtle_hit's table at 01C272 has the same reaction for 0A and 0B); it tells the soldier when it
#   hits. No scenario runs it.
# kind 41 rubble: a piece of rock that falls off a defeated rock soldier (011A78 starts four:
#   0120BA "move.w #$4100, ($2,A6)") and stays on the ground (rendered 0151BA, 0151C4, 0151CE: lumps of rock, by the
#   parameter)
# kind 48 wall_slab: a big slanted slab (rendered 0156DE) that flies off left at 0x800 as kind 1D appears, knocking
#   turtles flying (attack 07); as it starts a block of 6 by 17 tiles is drawn into a layer (0126DA "lea $191a4.l, A6",
#   0126E4 "jsr $183fa.l") (012668 starts it: 01266E "move.w #$4800, ($2,A6)")
# kinds 49 and 4A missile: kind 1D's missile (rendered 01569A: a missile with its flame; 012716 starts kind 49:
#   01271C "move.w #$4900, ($2,A6)"), which flies at 0x300 knocking turtles flying (attack 87) and explodes against a
#   wall or outside 0x60-0x17F across on the screen; a turtle can hit it out of the air. Kind 4A has the same routines
#   (the table at 0126FE) and no spawn list, record or code starts it.
# kind 4C shock_bars: three tall posts in a row (rendered 015752: white bars) that appear with kind 1D, 0x8A nearer the
#   front than him (012894: 01289A "move.w #$4c00, ($2,A6)", 0128AC "addi.l #$8a00, ($16,A6)"), blinking faster and
#   faster until they flicker on, until he is defeated, then blinking slower and slower until they are gone; they do not
#   attack
# kind 4D shock_bars (hit box): not drawn, the bars' picture with its attack box (0129B4 "movea.l $1574e.l, A6"), at
#   kind 1D's place as he appears (012984: 01298A "move.w #$4d00, ($2,A6)"): while the bars flicker on it attacks one
#   frame in 16 with attack 0A, which electrocutes a turtle (the electric beam's attack, kind 30)
#
# Two routines no path reaches are named here (0116A2, 0129C0): the analysis takes them from their func lines.
# 06130B and 06130C, which 0123F8 and 012830 test, are never set by any code
# (the only writes clear 06130B: 01221C, 012856), so the missile's limit they would make never applies.

# ---- the families' fields ----

# the hits it still takes: its bars from its stats plus 2 for each player in play (kind 1D: plus 1) at its start
# (011588 "move.b ($8,A0), ($24,A0)", 0115A8 "add.b D7, ($24,A0)", 0121E6 "add.b D7, ($24,A0)"); each hit one less, the
# last one defeats it (01190E "subq.b #1, ($24,A0)", 011912 "beq $1195a"); its flashing goes by it
# (011F5C "cmpi.b #$1c, ($24,A0)", 011F68 "cmpi.b #$14, ($24,A0)", 011F74 "cmpi.b #$a, ($24,A0)")
scoped object rock_soldier 24 hits_left u8
# the blows it struck since its last hit, up to 3 (011D04 "addq.b #1, ($26,A0)", 011D12 "move.b #$3, ($26,A0)",
# 011936 "clr.b ($26,A0)"): with the turtles in play they pick the blow's animation, slower as they go
# (011CEC "add.b ($26,A0), D7", 011CF0 "move.b (A6,D7.w), D7") and the chance of the next one
# (0116F6 "move.b ($26,A0), D1")
scoped object rock_soldier 26 blows u8
# frames after it recovers before it can be hit again (0119C0 "move.b #$8, ($2a,A0)", 011F4E "subq.b #1, ($2a,A0)",
# 011F56 "ori.b #$2, ($0,A0)")
scoped object rock_soldier 2A hit_delay u8
# the players in play at its start (0115A2 "move.b D7, ($2b,A0)", 0121E2 "move.b D7, ($2b,A0)"); nothing reads it
scoped object rock_soldier 2B players u8
# bit 7 when one of its gun blasts hit a turtle (0120A0 "move.b #$80, ($2c,A6)"): after the burst's least time that ends
# the burst (011884 "tst.b ($2c,A0)", 011888 "bmi $118b2"); cleared as a burst or a missile starts
# (011C78 "clr.b ($2c,A0)", 0124BC "clr.b ($2c,A0)")
scoped object rock_soldier 2C shot_hit u8
# 0x80 when it is struck (0118EC "move.b #$80, ($2d,A0)"), cleared when it recovers or its attack ends
# (0119BC "clr.b ($2d,A0)", 0118E2 "clr.b ($2d,A0)"); nothing reads it
scoped object rock_soldier 2D hurt u8
# the turtle close by it strikes (find_close_turtle's bits, bit 7 when the turtle is on its left:
# 01173E "move.b D0, ($2e,A0)"), kept while it turns to it (011CBA "tst.b ($2e,A0)", 011CC2 "ori.b #$40, ($0,A0)",
# 011CCE "clr.b ($2e,A0)")
scoped object rock_soldier 2E close_turtle u8
# the explosions so far when it is defeated, 10 in all (011A5C "addq.b #1, ($2f,A0)", 011A60 "cmpi.b #$a, ($2f,A0)"):
# boss_start_explosion places each one by it (010EF0 "move.b ($2f,A0), D7"); cleared as each move starts
# (011D40 "clr.b ($2f,A0)")
scoped object rock_soldier 2F explosions u8
# frames counted down: the waits of its way in (01160E "move.b #$50, ($30,A0)", 01167C "move.b #$28, ($30,A0)"), the
# burst's length (011C8C "move.b (A6,D7.w), ($30,A0)", 011870 "subq.b #1, ($30,A0)"), the flinch
# (01193A "move.b #$1c, ($30,A0)", 0119B0 "subq.b #1, ($30,A0)"), the defeat's steps (011A0A "move.b #$64, ($30,A0)",
# 011A24 "move.b #$c, ($30,A0)", 011A70 "move.b #$96, ($30,A0)"), when the rubble falls (011A80 "move.b ($30,A0), D7")
scoped object rock_soldier 30 timer u8
# frames its move lasts: a walk or a stand by chance (011DE6 "move.b (A6,D7.w), ($31,A0)",
# 0117BC "subq.b #1, ($31,A0)"), the jump and its landing (011D8E "move.b #$28, ($31,A0)",
# 011848 "subq.b #1, ($31,A0)"), the least a burst fires (011C92 "move.b #$20, ($31,A0)", 011878 "subq.b #1, ($31,A0)")
scoped object rock_soldier 31 move_time u8

# the rock soldier that fired it (01200A "move.l A0, ($20,A6)"): told when the blast hits
# (01209C "movea.l ($20,A0), A6", 0120A0 "move.b #$80, ($2c,A6)")
scoped object gun_blast 20 shooter u32 object.rock_soldier
# bit 7 when it flies left, the way its rock soldier faces (011FFA "ori.b #$80, ($2f,A6)", 012042 "tst.b ($2f,A0)",
# 012056 "not.w ($1a,A0)")
scoped object gun_blast 2F flies_left u8

# the rock soldier that fired it (012754 "move.l A0, ($20,A6)"); nothing reads it
scoped object missile 20 shooter u32 object.rock_soldier
# bit 7 when it flies left (012744 "ori.b #$80, ($2f,A6)", 0127A2 "tst.b ($2f,A0)", 0127B6 "not.w ($1a,A0)")
scoped object missile 2F flies_left u8

# what it goes by: the bars' (kind 4C) the rock soldier, whose defeat (routine 6) makes them go
# (0128B4 "move.l A0, ($20,A6)", 012928 "movea.l ($20,A0), A6", 01292C "cmpi.b #$6, ($4,A6)"); the hit box's (kind 4D)
# the bars, by whose routine it attacks or goes (01299C "move.l A5, ($20,A6)", 0129E2 "movea.l ($20,A0), A6",
# 0129E6 "move.b ($4,A6), D7")
scoped object shock_bars 20 leader u32 object
# the frames from one frame drawn to the next, less one: 0x3D at first, 4 less every 16 frames down to 1
# (0128E2 "move.b #$3d, ($2e,A0)", 012910 "subq.b #4, ($2e,A0)", 012914 "cmpi.b #$1, ($2e,A0)"), then one more every 8
# frames up to 0x28 when they go (01294E "addq.b #1, ($2e,A0)", 012952 "cmpi.b #$28, ($2e,A0)")
scoped object shock_bars 2E blink_period u8
# frames to the next frame drawn (012966 "subq.b #1, ($30,A0)", 01296E "move.b ($2e,A0), ($30,A0)",
# 012974 "bset #$7, ($0,A0)")
scoped object shock_bars 30 blink_timer u8

# ---- the functions ----

# -- kind 1E: the rock soldier of the building site --
# routine 0, his way in, by the state (011554 "moveq #$0, D7", 01155C "movea.l (A6,D7.w), A6": the table at 011562)
01154E  func  rock_soldier_site_enter
# state 0: drawn, his stats, the hits he takes (011576 "ori.b #$80, ($0,A0)", 01157C "lea $181cc.l, A6",
# 0115A8 "add.b D7, ($24,A0)"), standing (0115AC "movea.l $14da0.l, A6"), counted as an enemy, the boss not down and the
# area not cleared (0115D8 "addq.b #1, $6120c.l", 0115DE "clr.b $61313.l", 0115E4 "clr.b $6131d.l")
011576  func  rock_soldier_site_start
# state 1: once the scroll reaches 0x6E000 the music fades (0115EC "cmpi.l #$6e000, $63380.l",
# 0115FA "move.w #$fe, D7"), 80 frames (01160E "move.b #$50, ($30,A0)")
0115EC  func  rock_soldier_site_enter_wait_for_scroll
# state 2: after the 80 frames, his jump's animation and down at 0x800 (01161E "movea.l $14da4.l, A6",
# 01162A "move.w #$f800, ($1e,A0)")
011616  func  rock_soldier_site_enter_wait
# state 3: drops (01163C "bsr $11f94") until he is on the ground (011640 "tst.w ($10,A0)"): sound 3E, the boss music 64
# and the balloon "Crash the turtles!" beside him (011648 "move.w #$3e, D7", 011652 "move.w #$64, D7",
# 011676 "jsr $3001c.l"), 40 frames (01167C "move.b #$28, ($30,A0)")
01163C  func  rock_soldier_site_enter_drop
# state 4: the screen shakes for the 40 frames (011690 "bsr $116b0"), then he can be hit and attacks
# (011696 "ori.b #$6, ($0,A0)") and his first move (01169C "moveq #$0, D0", 01169E "bra $11c0e")
011688  func  rock_soldier_site_enter_land
# no path reaches it: the timers cleared, the next routine at its first state (0116A2 "clr.w ($30,A0)",
# 0116A6 "clr.b ($5,A0)", 0116AA "addq.b #1, ($4,A0)")
0116A2  func  rock_soldier_site_enter_end
# a layer's scroll byte (060063) from its place (063386) less 0 or 4 by the frame count (0116B0 "move.b $600a5.l, D7",
# 0116B6 "andi.b #$2, D7", 0116BC "add.b $63386.l, D7", 0116C4 "move.b D7, $60063.l"): the screen shakes
0116B0  func  rock_soldier_site_shake_screen
# routine 1: his flashing (0116CC "bsr $11f3e"); in the air or landing, the state (0116D0 "cmpi.b #$3, ($5,A0)",
# 0116DA "cmpi.b #$4, ($5,A0)"); else a turtle close by (0116E4 "bsr $11ad8") is struck at a chance from 01175A by
# blows, whether the turtle is in front and the turtles in play (0116EE "lea $1175a.l, A6",
# 011720 "move.b $61207.l, D7", 011732 "jsr $48448.l"): turned to it, sound 08, the blow's animation by the turtles in
# play and blows (011CA8 "move.w #$300, ($4,A0)", 011CD2 "move.b #$8, D7", 011CE6 "lea $11d1a.l, A6",
# 011CF4 "lea $14da0.l, A6"); else the state (011746 "lea $11782.l, A6")
0116CC  func  rock_soldier_site_think
# state 0: walks at his target (move_by_speeds within y 0x10-0x78 every other frame: 0117B8 "bsr $11f94"), aiming at it
# every 16 frames (0117A4 "bsr $11e6a"); move_time over, the move's end (0117C4 "moveq #$1, D0")
011796  func  rock_soldier_site_walk
# state 1: the same, moving two frames in four (0117DE "btst #$1, $600a5.l", 0117EA "bsr $11f94",
# 0117F6 "moveq #$2, D0")
0117CC  func  rock_soldier_site_walk_on
# state 2: stands until move_time is over (0117FE "subq.b #1, ($31,A0)", 011806 "moveq #$3, D0")
0117FE  func  rock_soldier_site_stand
# state 3: in the air, falling 0x80 faster each frame (011812 "add.w D7, ($10,A0)", 01181E "subi.w #$80, ($1e,A0)");
# down, sound 3F, on the ground with no shadow (011826 "move.w #$3f, D7", 011838 "bclr #$7, ($1,A0)")
01180E  func  rock_soldier_site_jump
# state 4: the screen shakes until move_time is over (011844 "bsr $116b0"), then he can be hit again and the move's end
# (011850 "ori.b #$2, ($0,A0)", 011856 "moveq #$4, D0")
011844  func  rock_soldier_site_jump_land
# a move over: a turtle in front within 0x8C (01185E "bsr $11b42"): the burst, routine 2, of 32 to 80 frames by chance,
# 32 at the least, the gun aimed (011C72 "move.w #$200, ($4,A0)", 011C8C "move.b (A6,D7.w), ($30,A0)",
# 011C92 "move.b #$20, ($31,A0)", 011C98 "movea.l $14db0.l, A6"); else the next move by D0 (011868 "bra $11c0e")
01185E  func  rock_soldier_site_move_done
# routine 2: his flashing; every 4 frames a gun blast from a free object of 24-31 (01188C "move.b ($30,A0), D7",
# 011890 "andi.b #$3, D7", 011898 "lea $62780.l, A6", 0118A4 "jsr $1830c.l"): 0x48 in front at height 0x30, flying his
# way, with him as its shooter (011FE8 "move.w #$4800, D7", 012004 "move.w #$3000, ($10,A6)",
# 01200A "move.l A0, ($20,A6)"); when timer runs out, or once move_time is over and a blast hit, routine 3 with the gun
