# ---- comments ----
comment rock_soldier_site_enter the building site's rock soldier comes in, by the state of his way in
comment rock_soldier_site_start the rock soldier waits high up, his hits set by the players in play; the area is not cleared
comment rock_soldier_site_enter_wait_for_scroll once the scroll reaches him the music fades
comment rock_soldier_site_enter_wait after a while he drops
comment rock_soldier_site_enter_drop he drops to the ground, then the boss music and his balloon "Crash the turtles!"
comment rock_soldier_site_enter_land the screen shakes from his landing, then he fights
comment rock_soldier_site_enter_end Unused: ends the way in, the next routine
comment rock_soldier_site_shake_screen the screen shakes
comment rock_soldier_site_think he strikes a turtle close by at a chance, else goes on with his move
comment rock_soldier_site_walk he walks at his target, aiming at it now and then
comment rock_soldier_site_walk_on he walks on at his target
comment rock_soldier_site_stand he stands a while
comment rock_soldier_site_jump he is in the air until he lands
comment rock_soldier_site_jump_land the screen shakes from his landing, then he can be hit again
comment rock_soldier_site_move_done a move over: a burst at a turtle in front within reach, else the next move
comment rock_soldier_site_fire he fires gun blasts until the burst is over
comment rock_soldier_site_finish_attack the blow, or the gun held up after the burst, plays out; then the next move
comment rock_soldier_site_next_move the next move by chance, by what he did before
comment rock_soldier_site_walk_start he picks a turtle and walks at it, or stands if there is none
comment rock_soldier_site_walk_on_start he walks on as he was
comment rock_soldier_site_stand_start he stands a while
comment rock_soldier_site_jump_start he jumps where he stands, now and then with "Crash the turtles!"
comment rock_soldier_site_random_move_time how long the move lasts, at random
comment rock_soldier_site_weak_flash his flashing as he weakens, in his colours
comment rock_soldier_site_move he moves by his speeds within the area's depth
comment rock_soldier_technodrome_enter the Technodrome's rock soldier comes in, by the state of his way in
comment rock_soldier_technodrome_start the rock soldier waits unseen, his hits set by the players in play; the area is not cleared
comment rock_soldier_technodrome_enter_wait_for_scroll once the scroll reaches him the music fades
comment rock_soldier_technodrome_enter_wait the boss music starts
comment rock_soldier_technodrome_enter_appear he appears and fights, with the wall slab flying off and the shock bars
comment rock_soldier_technodrome_think he strikes a turtle close by at a chance, more often as he weakens, else goes on with his move
comment rock_soldier_technodrome_walk he walks at his target, aiming at it now and then
comment rock_soldier_technodrome_walk_on he walks on at his target
comment rock_soldier_technodrome_stand he stands a while
comment rock_soldier_technodrome_taunt he taunts until the animation ends
comment rock_soldier_technodrome_move_done a move over: now and then a missile at a turtle far enough in front, else the next move
comment rock_soldier_technodrome_fire the missile's firing plays out, then the next move
comment rock_soldier_technodrome_finish_blow the blow plays out, then the next move
comment rock_soldier_technodrome_next_move the next move by chance, by what he did before
comment rock_soldier_technodrome_walk_start he picks a turtle and walks at it, or taunts if there is none
comment rock_soldier_technodrome_walk_on_start he walks on as he was
comment rock_soldier_technodrome_stand_start he stands a while, crouched on his way in
comment rock_soldier_technodrome_taunt_start he taunts with a speech
comment rock_soldier_technodrome_random_move_time how long the move lasts, at random
comment rock_soldier_technodrome_move he moves by his speeds within the area's depth
comment rock_soldier_technodrome_start_wall_slab the wall slab flies off where he appears
comment rock_soldier_technodrome_start_missile a missile from his launcher, the way he faces
comment rock_soldier_technodrome_weak_flash his flashing as he weakens, in his colours
comment rock_soldier_struck a hit makes the rock soldier flinch, or the last one defeats him
comment rock_soldier_recover the rock soldier gets over the hit and moves on
comment rock_soldier_defeated the defeated rock soldier, by the state of his defeat
comment rock_soldier_defeated_start the defeated rock soldier stands a while
comment rock_soldier_defeated_wait he stands until the explosions
comment rock_soldier_defeated_explode an explosion on him
comment rock_soldier_defeated_next_explosion the next explosion, until ten
comment rock_soldier_defeated_crumble rubble falls off him; then the boss is down and the area cleared
comment rock_soldier_defeated_stay he stays a heap of rocks
comment rock_soldier_find_close_turtle a turtle close enough to strike, and on which side
comment rock_soldier_find_turtle_ahead a turtle in front of him at the range of his weapon
comment rock_soldier_weak_flash he can be hit again after a while, and flashes more as he weakens
comment gun_blast_start a gun blast flies the way the rock soldier faces
comment gun_blast_fly the blast swells as it flies, and tells its rock soldier when it hits a turtle
comment rubble_start a piece of rock falls off the defeated rock soldier
comment rubble_fall the piece falls to the ground
comment rubble_stay the piece stays on the ground
comment wall_slab_start a slab of the wall flies off to the left, knocking turtles flying
comment wall_slab_fly the slab flies until it leaves the screen
comment missile_start a missile flies the way the rock soldier faces, knocking turtles flying
comment missile_fly the missile flies until it meets a wall or reaches the screen's sides, and explodes
comment missile_explode the explosion ends and the missile is gone
comment missile_idle nothing
comment missile_struck a turtle's attack bursts the missile
comment missile_vanish the burst ends and the missile is gone
comment shock_bars_start the shock bars appear, blinking
comment shock_bars_blink_in the bars blink faster until they flicker on
comment shock_bars_on the bars flicker until their rock soldier is defeated
comment shock_bars_blink_out the bars blink slower until they are gone
comment shock_bars_blink the bars show now and then
comment shock_bars_hit_box_start the unseen part of the bars that electrocutes turtles
comment shock_bars_hit_box_blink_in Unused: counts down as the bars blink in
comment shock_bars_hit_box_zap while the bars flicker on it electrocutes turtles now and then; it goes with them

# ---- the pointers where these objects' code works them out ----
# the gun blast rock_soldier_site_fire starts, a free object of 24-31 (011898 "lea $62780.l, A6",
# 0118A4 "jsr $1830c.l"): its fields by its family (011FFA "ori.b #$80, ($2f,A6)", 01200A "move.l A0, ($20,A6)")
local rock_soldier_site_fire 011898,0118A4 a6 blast object.gun_blast
# the rock soldier a gun blast tells it hit (01209C "movea.l ($20,A0), A6", 0120A0 "move.b #$80, ($2c,A6)")
local gun_blast_fly 01209C a6 shooter object.rock_soldier
# the rubble rock_soldier_defeated_crumble starts (011A9A "lea $62a00.l, A6", 011AA6 "jsr $1830c.l")
local rock_soldier_defeated_crumble 011A9A,011AA6 a6 rubble object
# the shock bars and their hit box rock_soldier_technodrome_enter_appear starts (0122AE "lea $62780.l, A6",
# 0122BA "jsr $1830c.l"; 0128BE "lea $62780.l, A6", 0128CA "jsr $1830c.l"): their leader (0128B4 "move.l A0, ($20,A6)",
# 01299C "move.l A5, ($20,A6)")
local rock_soldier_technodrome_enter_appear 0122AE,0122BA a6 bars object.shock_bars
local rock_soldier_technodrome_enter_appear 0128BE,0128CA a6 hit_box object.shock_bars
# the objects these functions start, in A6 from their callers (01229E "jsr $1830c.l", 0124CC "jsr $1830c.l")
param rock_soldier_technodrome_start_wall_slab a6 slab object
param rock_soldier_technodrome_start_missile a6 missile object.missile


# ======== Baxter Stockman, the helicopters, the drones and the car (012A2E-0143BF) ========
# The kinds of the kind table (053896) whose routines are at 012A2E-0143BF. What each kind is comes from its code, its
# spawn records (area_spawn_lists: +6 the kind, +7 the parameter) and its animations, rendered from the addresses its
# set_animation calls take (the car: from the tile block it draws, with area 4's tile banks). Their stats come from
# set_object_stats' 10-byte records (+0 attack and hit, +2 health_bars, health and damage, +6 depth, +8 the hit sound).
# "Struck" is routine 4, which the attack check sets on an object a turtle's attack hits
# (0472CE "move.w #$400, ($4,A1)"); "gone off the screen" is remove_if_off_screen (flags bit 5). "With the ride" is
# carry_with_ride: in area 6 (stage 6) these objects move 0x600 a frame across with the scroll, as the knife does
# (04BB32). A smaller y is nearer the front (down the screen: Rocksteady "walks down" with
# 04F354 "addi.l #-$100, ($16,A0)"). The free objects they take are find_free_object's from object 4, 12, 24 or 32
# (062140, 0623C0, 062780, 062A00), among the next 8 or 12.
#
# kind 35 parked_car: a big car of area 4, the street of cars (one spawn record, 05E0CE). The car is a picture of 22 by
#   14 tiles the object draws into a layer (012A72 "lea $18c28.l, A6", 012A7C "jsr $183fa.l"; rendered: a sedan facing
#   the front right) and keeps where the object is by that layer's scroll (012B8C "move.w ($24,A0), $6005c.l",
#   012B94 "move.w ($26,A0), $6005e.l"); the object itself shows a piece of wall at its place (rendered 015052: a window
#   with 555-4321 under it). Once the object is under 0xE0 on the screen (012A8C "cmpi.w #$e0, ($c,A0)") the car drives
#   off to the front right for 56 frames, 4 pixels a frame (012AFE "subi.w #$4, ($24,A0)",
#   012B0A "subi.b #$4, ($27,A0)"), with three unseen hit boxes (kind 36) going the same way, the layer's rows blanked
#   one every other frame from its last row up (012BA8 "move.b #$0, (A6,D7.w)", 012BAE "move.b #$10, ($1,A6,D7.w)": the
#   high byte the colour, the low the code, tile 0x10 colour 0 being empty); then three Foot soldiers, four when 061207
#   (the turtles in play) is 2 or more, leap out of where it stood (kind 38). Harmless itself (its stats 0181A4: no
#   attack, no bars). No scenario runs the drive (index.txt: 012AA6 0 ran).
# kind 36 car_hit_box: one of the car's three unseen hit boxes (012BCC starts them: 012BD2 "move.w #$3600, ($2,A6)"), at
#   the car's place plus an offset from 012C10 by its number; not drawn (012C1C "move.b #$5, ($0,A0)": in use and
#   attacking), it moves with the car, 0x400 across and 0x400 toward the front (012C2C "move.w #$400, ($1a,A0)",
#   012C70 "sub.l D7, ($16,A0)"), knocking flying what it hits (its stats 0181E0: attack 07, damage 0x310), sound 3A as
#   each starts; its frame (01505C, rendered: a puff) only gives its attack box
# kind 38 car_soldier: a Foot soldier who leaps out of where the car stood (012C7E starts it:
#   012C84 "move.w #$3800, ($2,A6)"; rendered 015092: a Foot soldier in mid leap) to the front right until his screen y
#   is under the one of his number, then is a gun soldier (kind 09: 012D18 "move.l #$81000900, ($0,A0)",
#   enemy_gun_soldier_start)
#
# kind 43 helicopter: a small helicopter of area 6, the highway on skateboards (87 spawn records: parameter 00 16 times,
#   01 32, 80 39; with kind 69 the area's only enemies): rendered 0152F6 and 015320, the same helicopter in two colours
#   (palettes 0E and 0A by the parameter's bit 0): a rotor over a small cabin on skids. In the air with its shadow, 0x40
#   high and bobbing, it keeps in front of the turtles for 900 frames, drifting back and forth in depth, then falls
#   behind and is gone; by the parameter, bit 0 clear: bursts of four bullets (kind 44) fired down at a slant; set:
#   three missiles (kind 46) dropped; bit 7: it faces right. Four bars, attack 07 (it knocks flying the turtles it
#   touches); destroyed, a big explosion (kind 32). Sound 0A plays while any helicopter is about
#   (006AF6 "tst.b $6130a.l", 006B00 "move.w #$a, D7"), 09 when the last one is gone; 06130A counts them.
# kind 44 copter_bullet: the helicopter's bullet (013168 starts it: 01316E "move.w #$4400, ($2,A6)"; rendered 015370: a
#   small bullet with a trail, slanting down), 0x500 across and 0x500 down from in front of the helicopter's cabin,
#   knocking turtles down (attack 04); on the road a spark (rendered 015396), harmless, then gone
# kind 46 falling_missile: a missile that drops straight down and explodes on the ground (rendered 0153CE and 01534E: a
#   missile; the explosion is the rocket's, 01525C): parameter 0 the helicopter's (01327A starts it), knocking down
#   (attack 04), 0x300 a frame; parameter 1 one at each turtle in play when no enemy has been about for a while: the
#   layers' code of every area counts the frames with fewer than three enemies, shows the "HURRY!" arrow (rendered
#   02DB76) after 0x100, and with none at all at 0x600 drops these (01EC8A "cmpi.w #$600, $633f6.l",
#   01EC96 "jsr $13354.l"), from height 0xF0 at 0x800 a frame, knocking flying (attack 07, damage 0xB10)
#
# kind 54 flying_drone: a flying machine of area 7, the building site (rendered 015886-0158F4: a round body under a
#   rotor, with a gun that points to the front, across or to the back; 0158FE resting, 01592C wrecked). The scene at
#   030716 (which puts Rocksteady in in area 1) starts seven of them far back, out of the area
#   (0307E8 "cmpi.b #$7, $60121.l", 0307F4 "jsr $133fe.l", 013448 "move.l #$13800, ($16,A6)"), resting, and later
#   releases them all (030860 "jsr $1349e.l"): each takes off on its own heading, then for 1800 frames weaves at a
#   turtle, firing bursts of four shots (kind 55), and then rises out of sight. Two bars, attack 07; destroyed, a medium
#   explosion (kind 57). While any is about, sound 38 every 32 to 128 frames (013A1A). 061312 counts them. No scenario
#   runs them.
# kind 55 drone_shot: the drone's shot (013A8C starts it: 013A92 "move.w #$5500, ($2,A6)"): a flash at the gun, then a
#   streak (rendered 015954-0159EA by the gun's aim) that drops to the ground at 0x400 a frame while it flies the aim's
#   way, then a flash on the ground (rendered 0159FC); it knocks turtles flying (attack 07) at the gun and on the
#   ground, not on its way
#
# kind 5C stockman: Baxter Stockman, the boss of area 3, the sewers (one spawn record, 05DF94), in his flying machine
#   (rendered 015C80: a seat under a canopy, two claws below; 015CD6 the claws opening), from which he drops Mousers
#   (kind 5E, which turn into Mousers, kind 4E: 0143A8 "move.l #$81004e80, ($0,A0)"). He waits until he is the only
#   enemy left, comes in to the boss music saying "Yippee-I-Ay!" (013D92 "jsr $30084.l"), then for 3600 frames weaves at
#   a turtle dropping Mousers, and then rises out of sight and is gone. 24 bars, one a hit (01828A); the last one: four
#   big explosions and the boss down (013F58 "move.b #$1, $61313.l").
# kind 5E falling_mouser: a Mouser Stockman drops (0142D6 starts it: 0142F6 "move.w #$5e00, ($2,A6)"; rendered 015D4E: a
#   Mouser seen from above), with its shadow: it falls 0x400 a frame to the floor and turns into a Mouser that thinks at
#   once (kind 4E, parameter 80); it cannot be hit (014336 "move.b #$81, ($0,A0)")
#
# Left unnamed: the kinds' data (the hit boxes' offsets 012C10, the soldiers' stops 012CFE, the helicopter's tables
# 013092, 0130F4, 0130FC, 013150, the drones' 013486, 01348E, 013496, 0138AC, 0138B4, 0139A2, 0139D6, 0139DA, 013A74,
# 013AE4, 013B72, Stockman's 013E30, 01416E, 014176, 014194, 0141B4, 01424A, 01426A, the state tables 0134CA, 013C46,
# 013F08, the animation tables 01584E and 015938): batch 4's records. The work RAM they use (06130A, 061311, 061312,
# 061318, 061319, 0633F6, 060080, 060083, 060094, 061207, 061313), cited by address. The +2F that kinds 35, 36, 44, 46
# and 5E only clear, Stockman's +31 that he only clears (013E5E "clr.b ($31,A0)"), and the car's byte +27 (the low byte
# of its scroll_y, which a field of its own cannot overlap): nothing reads them as their own.

# ---- the families' fields ----

# the address in the tile RAM of the layer's row it blanks next: the last row first
# (012A52 "move.l #$102f80, ($20,A0)"), one row up after each (012B9E "movea.l ($20,A0), A6",
# 012BBC "subi.w #$80, ($22,A0)")
scoped object parked_car 20 blank_row u32
# the layer's x scroll that keeps the car's picture with the object: moved by what the object moved on the screen

scoped object parked_car 24 scroll_x u16
# the layer's y scroll (012A4E "clr.w ($26,A0)", 012B94 "move.w ($26,A0), $6005e.l"); its low byte 4 less a frame as it
# drives off (012B0A "subi.b #$4, ($27,A0)")
scoped object parked_car 26 scroll_y u16
# its screen x the frame before (012A68 "move.w ($c,A0), ($2a,A0)", 012B74 "move.w ($2a,A0), D7",
# 012B78 "sub.w ($c,A0), D7", 012B7C "move.w ($c,A0), ($2a,A0)")
scoped object parked_car 2A last_screen_x u16
# frames before it drives off (012A96 "move.b #$a, ($30,A0)", 012AAA "subq.b #1, ($30,A0)"), frames of the drive

# routine 1: when the flash ends, the streak, not attacking (013B8A "tst.b ($33,A0)", 013BA0 "movea.l ($4,A6,D7.w), A6",
# 013BAE "bclr #$2, ($0,A0)")
013B8A  func  drone_shot_flash
# routine 2: on, kept in the area (013BB6 "jsr $182ee.l", 013BBC "jsr $1381e.l"); its attack hit, the burst, routine 4
# (013BC2 "btst #$3, ($0,A0)", 013BF6 "addq.b #2, ($4,A0)"); on the ground, attacking again, the flash there, routine 3
# (013BD6 "ori.b #$4, ($0,A0)", 013BE4 "movea.l $15950.l, A6")
013BB6  func  drone_shot_fly
# routines 3 and 4: gone when its animation ends (013C0C "tst.b ($33,A0)", 013C10 "beq $1832a")
013C0C  func  drone_shot_vanish

# -- kind 5C: Baxter Stockman --
# routine 0, his way in, by the state (013C3A "move.b ($5,A0), D7", 013C40 "movea.l (A6,D7.w), A6": the table at 013C46)
013C32  func  stockman_enter
# state 0: drawn, with his shadow (013C56 "ori.b #$80, ($0,A0)", 013C5C "ori.b #$80, ($1,A0)",
# 013C68 "move.l A6, ($3a,A0)"), his stats (013C6C "lea $1828a.l, A6"), flying (013C7E "movea.l $15c70.l, A6"), still,
# the boss not down (013C96 "clr.b $61313.l"), one enemy more (013CA4 "addq.b #1, $6120c.l")
013C56  func  stockman_start
# state 1: once he is the only enemy left (013CAC "cmpi.b #$1, $6120c.l"): the music fades (013CB8 "move.w #$fe, D7"),
# his palette (013CC8 "lea $c6c4.l, A6", 013CD2 "jsr $9242.l"), 100 frames (013CD8 "move.b #$64, ($30,A0)")
013CAC  func  stockman_wait_for_last_enemy
# state 2: with 060083 clear, after the 100 frames: sound 33, the boss music 64, sound BF (the speech of
# "Yippee-I-Ay!"), and he flies in, 0x300 a frame to the left and up 1 (013CE4 "tst.b $60083.l",
# 013CF6 "move.w #$33, D7", 013D00 "move.w #$64, D7", 013D10 "move.w #$bf, D7", 013D1A "move.w #$fd00, ($1a,A0)",
# 013D24 "move.w #$100, ($1e,A0)")
013CE4  func  stockman_wait
# state 3: until he is left of x 0x470 (013D3A "cmpi.l #$47000, ($12,A0)"): can be hit, heading left, turning, turn
# speed 0x20, 3600 frames (013D48 "ori.b #$2, ($0,A0)", 013D4E "move.w #$800, ($2c,A0)",
# 013D6A "move.w #$e10, ($24,A0)"), a target (013D70 "bsr $11dfa"), his drop's wait (013D7E "bsr $14218"), the balloon
# "Yippee-I-Ay!" by him (013D8E "addi.w #$20, D2", 013D92 "jsr $30084.l"), at height 0x2C
# (013D98 "move.w #$2c00, ($10,A0)"), routine 1
013D30  func  stockman_fly_in
# routine 1: while time_left lasts he bobs and weaves at his target (013DD4 "bsr $14120", 013DD8 "bsr $13fc8"); when the
# drop's wait is over, if the enemies are no more than 6-9 by the highest player in play (013DE4 "jsr $142aa.l",
# 013DEA "lea $13e30.l, A6", 013DF4 "cmp.b $6120c.l, D7"), with a free object of 12-23 a Mouser and his claws open,
# routine 2 (013E16 "bsr $142d6", 013E1A "movea.l $15c74.l, A6"), else a new wait; after time_left, up out of sight and
# gone, one enemy less (013DB8 "addq.b #1, ($10,A0)", 013DC6 "subq.b #1, $6120c.l")
013DA8  func  stockman_fly
# routine 2: bobs and weaves until his claws' animation ends (013E42 "tst.b ($33,A0)"), then routine 1, flying, a new
# wait (013E4A "subq.b #1, ($4,A0)", 013E4E "movea.l $15c70.l, A6", 013E5A "bsr $14218")
013E36  func  stockman_drop_mouser
# routine 3, which nothing sets: nothing (013E64 "rts")
013E64  func  stockman_idle
# routine 4, struck: with bars left, routine 5 for 30 frames, not hittable, hurt, sound 05 (013E78 "addq.b #1, ($4,A0)",
# 013E7C "move.b #$1e, ($30,A0)", 013E88 "movea.l $15c78.l, A6", 013E94 "move.b #$5, D7"); with none, routine 6, neither
# hittable nor attacking, defeated (013E9E "move.b #$6, ($4,A0)", 013EAC "andi.b #$f9, ($0,A0)",
# 013EB2 "movea.l $15c7c.l, A6")
013E66  func  stockman_struck
# routine 5: shakes and weaves (013EC4 "bsr $13f82", 013EC8 "bsr $13fc8"); after the 30 frames flying, routine 1,
# hittable (013ED4 "movea.l $15c70.l, A6", 013EE6 "move.b #$1, ($4,A0)", 013EEC "ori.b #$2, ($0,A0)")
013EC0  func  stockman_recover
# routine 6, defeated, by the state (013EFC "move.b ($5,A0), D7", 013F02 "movea.l (A6,D7.w), A6": the table at 013F08)
013EF4  func  stockman_defeated
# state 0: 12 frames, state 1, a big explosion from free objects 24-31 at its place by explosions, sound 11 or 13
# (013F18 "move.b #$c, ($30,A0)", 013F24 "lea $62780.l, A6", 013F38 "bne $10f46"); with none free, state 2
# (013F3C "move.b #$2, ($5,A0)")
013F14  func  stockman_defeated_explode
# state 1: after the 12 frames the next explosion, the boss down (013F54 "addq.b #1, ($2f,A0)",
# 013F58 "move.b #$1, $61313.l"); after 4, state 2 (013F60 "cmpi.b #$4, ($2f,A0)")
013F44  func  stockman_defeated_next_explosion
# state 2: gone when the defeat's animation ends, one enemy less (013F72 "tst.b ($33,A0)", 013F78 "subq.b #1, $6120c.l")
013F72  func  stockman_defeated_vanish
# his height by his depth from 014176/014194 plus a shake from 01416E by the frame count (013F8A "move.b $600a5.l, D7",
# 013F98 "lea $14176.l, A6", 013FB6 "lea $14194.l, A6", 013FC2 "move.w D7, ($10,A0)")
013F82  func  stockman_shake
# a target again every 64 frames; with one, the heading turns by turn_speed toward the target's side, swinging between
# directions 4 and C (014018 "cmpi.b #$c, ($2c,A0)", 01403C "cmpi.b #$4, ($2c,A0)"), 1 a frame in depth toward it
# (0140A0 "move.l #$100, D6", 0140B4 "add.l D6, ($16,A0)"), the move by the heading, kept in his part of the area
# (0140B8 "bsr $141f4", 0140BC "bsr $140c2")
013FC8  func  stockman_steer
# x kept within 0x3C0-0x470, y within 0x18-0x78 (0140C2 "cmpi.l #$47000, ($12,A0)", 0140EA "move.l #$3c000, ($12,A0)",
# 01410C "move.l #$7800, ($16,A0)", 014116 "move.l #$1800, ($16,A0)")
0140C2  func  stockman_keep_in_area
# his height by his depth plus the bob from 0141B4, 32 steps of 2 frames (014120 "lea $141b4.l, A6",
# 01412C "andi.w #$3e, D7", 014152 "lea $14194.l, A6", 01415E "move.w D7, ($10,A0)")
014120  func  stockman_bob
# x and y moved by his direction's step from 01426A (0141F4 "lea $1426a.l, A6", 014208 "add.l D6, ($12,A0)",
# 014212 "add.l D6, ($16,A0)")
0141F4  func  stockman_move
# the wait to his next drop: from 01424A by a random 0-7, less 8 times 060094, at least 1 (014218 "move.w #$8, D7",
# 01421C "jsr $48448.l", 014236 "sub.b D7, ($30,A0)", 014242 "move.b #$1, ($30,A0)")
014218  func  stockman_random_drop_time
# his floor 0x18 from y 0x60 back, else 0 (014252 "clr.b ($32,A0)", 014256 "cmpi.l #$6000, ($16,A0)",
# 014262 "move.b #$18, ($32,A0)")
014252  func  stockman_set_floor
# the number of the highest player in play, 0-3: how many of the players' bits are left as 0600A1 shifts down
# (0142AE "move.b $600a1.l, D5", 0142B4 "lsr.b #1, D5", 0142BA "addq.b #1, D7")
0142AA  func  highest_player_in_play
# not over y 0x38-0x67 (0142D6 "cmpi.l #$3800, ($16,A0)", 0142E2 "cmpi.l #$6800, ($16,A0)"): a Mouser (kind 5E) in the
# air with its shadow at his place, 2.5 pixels back and 8 lower, one enemy more (0142F6 "move.w #$5e00, ($2,A6)",
# 014302 "ori.b #$80, ($1,A6)", 01430E "move.l A5, ($3a,A6)", 014318 "addi.l #$280, ($16,A6)",
# 014320 "subi.w #$800, ($10,A6)", 01432A "addq.b #1, $6120c.l")
0142D6  func  stockman_start_mouser

# -- kind 5E: the Mouser Stockman drops --
# routine 0: drawn, not hittable (014336 "move.b #$81, ($0,A0)"), its stats (01433C "lea $18294.l, A6"), down at 0x400

014336  func  falling_mouser_start

# (014376 "cmpi.l #$4800, ($16,A0)", 01439C "move.w #$1800, ($10,A0)", 0143A2 "move.b #$18, ($32,A0)"), else on the

014366  func  falling_mouser_fall

0143BA  func  falling_mouser_idle

0143BC  func  falling_mouser_struck

# ---- comments ----
comment parked_car_start the car's spot shows a piece of wall; the car's picture will go into the layer
comment parked_car_draw the parked car is drawn into the layer, where the object is
comment parked_car_wait the car waits until it is well on the screen
comment parked_car_start_drive after a moment the car's three unseen hit boxes start
comment parked_car_drive_off the car drives off to the front right, its picture blanked row by row as it goes
comment parked_car_release_soldiers Foot soldiers leap out one after another, then the car's spot is gone
comment parked_car_follow_object the car's picture keeps with the object as the screen scrolls
comment parked_car_set_scroll the layer's scroll for the car's picture
comment parked_car_blank_row a row of the layer blanked, then the one above next time
comment parked_car_start_hit_box an unseen hit box along the car's body
comment parked_car_start_soldier a Foot soldier where the car stood
comment car_hit_box_start an unseen box that knocks flying the turtles in the car's way
comment car_hit_box_move the box goes with the car until it is off the screen
comment car_soldier_start a Foot soldier leaps out
comment car_soldier_leap the soldier leaps to his place, then is a gun soldier
comment helicopter_start a helicopter comes in the air, its sound on
comment helicopter_hover the helicopter hovers in front of the turtles until it fires
comment helicopter_fire_bullets the helicopter fires a burst of bullets
comment helicopter_drop_missiles the helicopter drops its missiles
comment helicopter_struck a hit makes the helicopter shake, or the last one destroys it in an explosion
comment helicopter_recover the helicopter gets over the hit
comment helicopter_destroyed the wreck stays behind until it leaves the screen
comment helicopter_fly the helicopter drifts in depth and keeps in front of the turtles; after a while it falls behind and is gone
comment helicopter_shake the hit helicopter shakes, carried with the ride
comment helicopter_bob the helicopter bobs in the air, carried with the ride
comment carry_with_ride in the skateboard ride the object moves on with the scroll
comment helicopter_random_wait how long until the helicopter fires, at random
comment helicopter_start_bullet a bullet from the front of the helicopter
comment helicopter_start_missile a missile from the helicopter
comment copter_bullet_start a bullet flies down at a slant, knocking turtles down
comment copter_bullet_fly the bullet bursts on what it hits, or sparks on the road
comment copter_bullet_vanish the bullet is gone when its spark or burst ends
comment copter_bullet_burst the bullet's burst goes on with the ride
comment falling_missile_start a missile drops from above
comment falling_missile_fall the missile drops until it hits the ground and explodes
comment falling_missile_explode the explosion ends and the missile is gone
comment rain_missiles_on_turtles missiles drop on the turtles that dawdle
comment start_falling_missile_at_turtle a missile high above a turtle
comment start_flying_drones the drones wait far back
comment start_flying_drone a drone with its own way to fly
comment release_flying_drones the drones come out, their sound on
comment flying_drone_enter the drone comes in, by the state of its way in
comment flying_drone_start the drone rests
comment flying_drone_wait the drone bobs until it is released, then takes off
comment flying_drone_take_off the drone flies out on its own way, rising
comment flying_drone_ready the drone picks a turtle and can be hit
comment flying_drone_fly the drone weaves at its turtle until it fires; after a while it rises out of sight
comment flying_drone_fire the drone fires a burst of shots
comment flying_drone_struck a hit makes the drone shake, or the last one wrecks it in an explosion
comment flying_drone_recover the drone gets over the hit
comment flying_drone_destroyed the wreck is gone when its animation ends
comment flying_drone_shake the hit drone shakes
comment flying_drone_steer the drone weaves toward its turtle
comment flying_drone_keep_in_area the drone or its shot is kept on the screen and within the area's depth
comment flying_drone_bob_low the resting drone bobs
comment flying_drone_bob the flying drone bobs
comment flying_drone_move the drone moves the way it flies
comment flying_drone_animate the drone faces its turtle and points its gun, the rotor turning
comment flying_drone_random_wait how long until the drone fires, at random
comment sound_flying_drones while drones are about their sound plays now and then
comment flying_drone_start_shot a shot from the drone's gun
comment drone_shot_start the shot flashes at the gun
comment drone_shot_flash after the flash the shot streaks down
comment drone_shot_fly the shot drops until it hits the ground and flashes there
comment drone_shot_vanish the shot is gone when its flash ends
comment stockman_enter Stockman comes in, by the state of his way in
comment stockman_start Stockman waits in his flying machine
comment stockman_wait_for_last_enemy once he is the last enemy the music fades
comment stockman_wait then the boss music, and he flies in
comment stockman_fly_in he flies in, picks a turtle and says "Yippee-I-Ay!"
comment stockman_fly he weaves at his turtle and now and then drops a Mouser; after a while he rises out of sight
comment stockman_drop_mouser his claws open as the Mouser drops
comment stockman_idle Unused: nothing
comment stockman_struck a hit makes him shake, or the last one defeats him
comment stockman_recover he gets over the hit
comment stockman_defeated the defeated Stockman, by the state of his defeat
comment stockman_defeated_explode an explosion on his machine
comment stockman_defeated_next_explosion the next explosion, until four; the boss is down
comment stockman_defeated_vanish he is gone when his defeat's animation ends
comment stockman_shake the hit machine shakes at its height
comment stockman_steer he weaves toward his turtle
comment stockman_keep_in_area he is kept within his part of the sewer
comment stockman_bob he bobs in the air at a height by how far back he is
comment stockman_move he moves the way he flies
comment stockman_random_drop_time how long until his next drop, at random
comment stockman_set_floor the floor under him, higher at the back
comment highest_player_in_play the number of the highest player in play
comment stockman_start_mouser a Mouser drops from his claws, unless he is over the middle of the sewer
comment falling_mouser_start a Mouser falls from Stockman's claws
comment falling_mouser_fall the Mouser falls to the floor and is a Mouser that goes after the turtles
comment falling_mouser_idle nothing
comment falling_mouser_struck Unused: the falling Mouser cannot be hit; it would be a Mouser on the floor

# ---- the pointers where these objects' code works them out ----
# the soldier parked_car_start_soldier starts, in A6 from its caller (012B4C "lea $623c0.l, A6", 012B58 "jsr $1830c.l"):
# its number (012CA6 "move.b ($30,A0), ($2f,A6)")
param parked_car_start_soldier a6 soldier object.car_soldier
# the drone start_flying_drone sets up, in A6 from its caller (013400 "lea $623c0.l, A6", 01340C "jsr $1830c.l"): its
# heading, turning, turn speed and number (01345A "move.b (A5,D0.w), ($2c,A6)", 01347C "move.b D0, ($2f,A6)")
param start_flying_drone a6 drone object.flying_drone
# the shot flying_drone_start_shot starts, in A6 from its caller (013624 "lea $62a00.l, A6", 013630 "jsr $1830c.l"): its
# aim (013AD8 "move.b ($2f,A0), ($2f,A6)")
param flying_drone_start_shot a6 shot object.drone_shot

# ######## batch 3 of the names: every function of the 68000 program ########
# ======== the boot (001000-00193D) ========
# The reset: the boot screen, the RAM and ROM check (no stack: the routines come back by a jump through a register),
# then the work RAM, the DIP switches and the main loop.
# the reset (vector 1): the chips' registers cleared (00108A "bsr $10bc", 00109A "bsr $10d2"), the RAM test (00106E "jmp
# $1492.l"), the cross hatch (001170-00118A), the work RAM zeroed (0011BE "movem.l D1-D6/A1-A2, -(A0)"), the queues
# (0011CC "lea $60400.l, A0", 0011FC "lea $60380.l, A0"), the DIP switches (00122C "move.b D0, $60030.l" on), IRQ5 on
# (00138A "ori.b #$20, $60058.l") and the main loop: the fire's tiles, then each job of the queue (0013C2 "jsr $1fdca.l",
# 0013B6 "lea $13de.l, A0", 0013C0 "jsr (A0)")
001000  func  reset
# the K051960's registers 1-3 to 0 (0010BE "move.b D7, $140001.l", 0010CA "move.b D7, $140002.l")
0010BC  func  clear_sprite_chip_registers
# the K052109's registers at 106900, 106B00, 106E00 and 106C00 to 0 (0010D4 "move.b D7, $106900.l", 0010E6 "move.b D7,
# $106c00.l")
0010D2  func  clear_tile_chip_registers
# the tile RAM: the fixed layer's 0x800 tiles (0010F4 "lea $100000.l, A0", 0010FA "move.w #$7ff, D7") and the two
# layers' 0x2000 (00110C "lea $102000.l, A0") to tile 0x10, the blank one; the layers' scroll registers to 0 (001120
# "move.b D0, $106402.l")
0010EE  func  clear_tile_ram
# D1-D6, A1 and A2 to 0, for the movem that zeroes the work RAM (0011BC "bsr $114c", 0011BE "movem.l D1-D6/A1-A2,
# -(A0)")
00114C  func  zero_boot_fill_registers
# the boot screen: one palette of 16 colours into the first 16 palettes (001712 "lea $191e.l, A1", 00171C "move.b
# (A1)+, ($1,A6)"), the rest black (001730 "lea $80400.l, A6"), the fixed layer blank, and "RAM ROM CHECK" (001776
# "move.w #$0, D3", 00177A "jmp $18e2.l")
001708  func  start_boot_screen
# the 4 hexadecimal digits of D4 written backwards at A4 (001788 "andi.w #$f, D6", 001794 "addi.w #$7, D6", 001798
# "move.b D6, -(A4)"): the program chips' sums when one is bad (00156A "move.w (A0)+, D4", 001572 "jmp $1782.l"); no
# scenario has a bad chip
001782  func  print_boot_hex
# a RAM tested by bytes: 0, 0x55, 0xAA and 0xFF written and read back at each even and odd byte (0017B2 "move.b D1,
# (A1)", 0017C0 "move.b D1, ($1,A1)", 0017EE "addi.b #$55, D1"), a bad one's bits from A2 into D7 (0017BC "or.b
# ($0,A2), D7"): the tile RAM, the work RAM and the sprite RAM (0014B4 "lea $100000.l, A0", 0014DA "lea $60000.l, A0",
# 0014FE "lea $140400.l, A0")
0017A6  func  test_ram
# the same for the palette RAM, whose bytes are every other one (001806 "move.b D1, ($1,A1)", 001818 "move.b D1,
# ($3,A1)"; 00149A "lea $80000.l, A0")
0017F6  func  test_palette_ram
# the check's results: the screen cleared, "RAM ROM CHECK" and the chips' "OK" (0018AE "move.w #$1, D3"), then a "BAD"
# for each bit of D7 (0018C8 "lsr.l #1, D6", 0018CE "lea $18d8.l, A4", 0018D4 "bra $18e2")
001890  func  show_boot_check
# the text D3 of the boot's table (0018E2 "lea $15ac.l, A6"): 16-bit offsets into the fixed layer (0018FA "adda.w
# (A5)+, A6"), its characters less 0x30 (00190C "subi.b #$30, D4"), '/' a new place, '?' the end; it comes back by
# A4 (00191C "jmp (A4)")
0018E2  func  print_boot_text

# ======== the test mode's mask ROM check (001942-001E97) ========
# the exceptions' handler (the vectors' other entries): it loops until the watchdog resets the board (00193E "nop",
# 001940 "bra $193e")
00193E  func  exception_hang
# test page 1, MASK ROM CHECK, by its steps (001942 "lea $1958.l, A6", 001948 "move.b $60007.l, D7")
001942  func  mask_rom_check_page
# its first step: the three layers cleared (job 0 with 7: 001972 "move.w #$7, D7", 001976 "jsr $4164.l")
00196C  func  mask_rom_check_clear
# "MASK ROM CHECK" (00198E "move.w #$8, D7", 001992 "jsr $22aa.l") and the test's palette (001998 "lea $a07e.l, A6")
001984  func  draw_mask_rom_check
# the sums at 063500 cleared and the job that works them out queued (0019BC "lea $63500.l, A0", 0019CE "move.w
# #$1100, D7")
0019AE  func  start_mask_rom_sums
# the six sums against the right ones (0019F8 "lea $1e34.l, A0", 001A0E "cmp.w (A6)+, D7"): "OK", or each chip's "OK"
# and a "BAD" for the wrong ones (001A22 "move.w #$9, D7", 001A32 "move.w #$a, D7", 001A40 "move.w #$b, D1"); then
# 0x80 frames (0019F0 "move.b #$80, $60004.l")
0019E0  func  show_mask_rom_check
# the next page when the 0x80 frames run out (001A64 "subq.b #1, $60004.l", 001A6E "addq.b #1, $60005.l"), the
# buttons as on every page (001A7A "bra $5da4")
001A5A  func  mask_rom_check_wait
# job 0x11: the mask ROMs summed into 063500, the sprites' (two halves) and the tiles' (001A84 "bsr $1a98", 001A88
# "bsr $1ada", 001A8C "bsr $1bd6")
001A7E  func  sum_mask_roms
# the sprites' ROM banks 0-3 read through the K051960 (001AA4 "move.b #$0, $63518.l", 001AAC "move.b #$3,
# $63519.l"), two sums kept (001AC8 "move.w D7, ($0,A0)", 001AD4 "move.w D7, ($2,A0)")
001A98  func  sum_sprite_roms_low

# ======== the frame (003B5C-00467B) ========
# Each player's coin slot has 8 bytes at 060300: +0 the coins left to count on the coin counter, +1 the coins towards a
# credit, +2 the counter's pulse, +5 the counter on, +6 the credits not given yet. Each player's record has 8 bytes at
# 060100: +0 the lives (BCD), +2 a value from the DIP switches (06009A), +4 the score (BCD), +6 the health, +7 the message
# under the box.
# every frame: the coin counters pulsed (003BC2 "bsr $3cb0"), board_control's bit 0 on while one is on (003BE0 "andi.b
# #$fe, D7", 003BEA "move.b D7, $a0001.l"), the coins just inserted counted (003C68 "bsr $3ce8") and the service
# switches' credits (003C94 "addi.w #$1, ($6,A0)")
003B5C  func  update_coins
# the coin and service switches pressed two frames ago and not in the two before (003BFE "move.b $60044.l, D0", 003C0C
# "and.b $60046.l, D0"), with the coin's sound: out of a game the sound stopped first (003C2E "move.b #$ff, D7",
# 003C36 "move.b #$86, D7")
003BFE  func  new_coin_presses
# the slot's coin counter: on for 30 frames of 60 for each coin still to count (003CD6 "subq.b #1, ($0,A0)", 003CE0
# "move.b #$3c, ($2,A0)", 003CC0 "cmpi.b #$1e, D0")
003CB0  func  pulse_coin_counter
# a coin in the slot: one more for the counter (003CE8 "addq.b #1, ($0,A0)"), and every 060303 coins 060304 credits
# (003CF4 "cmp.b $60303.l, D7", 003D06 "move.b $60304.l, D7", 003D0C "add.w ($6,A0), D7"): the coinage DIP switches
# (001274 "move.b (A0,D1.w), $60303.l")
003CE8  func  count_coin
# each slot's credits into its player's lives: 060090 lives a credit (the DIP switch: 0012B2 "move.b (A0,D0.w),
# $60090.l") added in BCD up to 999 (003D4E "abcd D5, D4", 003D58 "cmpi.w #$999, D4"), the player marked for his
# box (003D36 "bset D3, $600a2.l"); the players with lives into 0600B7 (003DA2 "move.b D6, $600b7.l")
003D1E  func  add_credit_lives
# the IRQ5: the registers kept at 060440 (003DC4 "movem.l D0-D7/A0-A6, $60440.l"), the IRQ lowered, frame_count (003DF8
# "addi.w #$1, $600a4.l"), the sound queue (003E06 "bsr $9202"), the inputs, the coins, the palettes (003E22 "jsr
# $9272.l"), and the game state's frame (003E38 "lea $3e60.l, A0", 003E42 "jsr (A0)"), back by rtr
003DC4  func  vblank_interrupt
# the inputs: each frame's coins and four players kept for four frames (003E70 "move.l $6003c.l, $60040.l", 003EC2
# "move.b D0, $60034.l"), the buttons pressed this frame (003ED2 "move.b D0, $60050.l"), and the layers' scroll copies
# into the K052109 (003F32 "move.b $6005f.l, $106018.l", 003F46 "move.b D7, $106400.l")
003E70  func  read_inputs
# game state 0, after the reset: its steps (003F7A "move.b $60003.l, D0", 003F82 "lea $3f8e.l, A0")
003F78  func  run_startup
# its first step: the K052109's and K051960's registers, the screen's flip, and with the test switch the test mode
# (003FCC "btst #$2, $60032.l", 003FFE "move.b #$3, $60001.l"); else the layers cleared (003FEA "bsr $4164") and the
# palettes (003FF8 "bsr $9242")
003F9A  func  startup_begin
# the next step when the jobs are done (004008 "tst.b $60082.l", 004012 "tst.b $60083.l")
004008  func  startup_wait
# the attract mode (game state 1: 004056 "addi.b #$1, $60001.l") after its frame, or at once with a coin (004036 "tst.b
# $60327.l", 004040 "move.b $60044.l, D0")
00402C  func  startup_end
# the screen's flip from the DIP switch (004086 "btst #$0, $60032.l") into the K052109 (0040A0 "move.b D7, $106d00.l")
# and the K051960 (0040AC "move.b D6, $140000.l")
004072  func  set_screen_flip
# game state 1, the attract mode: its step (0040BE "lea $4100.l, A0"), the players' starts (0040CA "bsr $419c"), and

# the 50 objects (062000-062F9F) to 0 (004604 "lea $62fa0.l, A0", 00460A "move.w #$7c, D0")
004604  func  clear_objects
# it works down from the objects' end (004604 "lea $62fa0.l, A0", 00460E "bra $453c", 004540 "movem.l D1-D7/A1,
# -(A0)"), which is where the
# players' slots start: the address points at no slot (none), whatever the pointers would make of it
local clear_objects 004604 a0 objects_end none

# ---- their one-line comments ----
comment reset the board's start: the RAM and ROM check, the work RAM, the DIP switches, then the main loop that runs the queued jobs
comment clear_sprite_chip_registers the sprite chip's registers cleared
comment clear_tile_chip_registers the tile chip's registers cleared
comment clear_tile_ram the three tile layers blank and their scroll at 0
comment zero_boot_fill_registers zeroes for the boot's clearing of the work RAM
comment start_boot_screen the boot's screen: a palette, the fixed layer blank and "RAM ROM CHECK"
comment print_boot_hex a number as four hexadecimal digits, for a bad program chip's sum
comment test_ram a RAM written and read back with four patterns, its bad chips marked
comment test_palette_ram the palette RAM written and read back with four patterns
comment show_boot_check the check's results: each chip OK or BAD
comment print_boot_text a text of the boot's screen
comment exception_hang an exception: the program stops until the watchdog resets the board
comment mask_rom_check_page the test mode's mask ROM check
comment mask_rom_check_clear the layers cleared for the mask ROM check
comment draw_mask_rom_check the mask ROM check's heading and palette
comment start_mask_rom_sums the mask ROMs' sums started
comment show_mask_rom_check each mask ROM's sum shown OK or BAD
comment mask_rom_check_wait the results shown a while, then the next page
comment sum_mask_roms the graphics ROMs summed through the video chips
comment sum_sprite_roms_low the sprite ROMs' first half summed
comment sum_sprite_roms_high the sprite ROMs' second half summed
comment sum_sprite_rom_banks the sprite ROMs' banks read through the sprite chip and summed
comment sum_tile_roms the tile ROMs read through the tile chip and summed
comment add_to_rom_sums four bytes of a graphics ROM added to its sums
comment clear_layers_job the tile layers chosen cleared
comment blank_tile the blank tile and its colour
comment clear_fixed_layer the fixed layer blank
comment clear_scroll_layer a scrolling layer blank
comment print_table_text a text of a table of texts into the fixed layer, or erased
comment set_text_colour the colour of the texts printed next
comment set_spare_text_colour a second texts' colour no text uses
comment add_point an enemy defeated: a point to the players' scores, the top score kept
comment add_point_to_score a point to a player's score and the top score kept
comment draw_scores the chosen players' scores drawn in their boxes
comment draw_score_player_1 the first player's score in his box
comment draw_score_player_2 the second player's score in his box
comment draw_score_player_3 the third player's score in his box
comment draw_score_player_4 the fourth player's score in his box
comment draw_top_score the top score after HIGH
comment draw_score_digits a score's four digits, without leading zeros
comment draw_blank_digit a blank in place of a leading zero
comment draw_digit a digit of a score
comment clear_scores the chosen scores to 0
comment print_plain_text a short text in the plain colour
comment draw_score_labels unused: the labels 1UP to 4UP and the top score
comment print_colour_text a text of the test mode's and the credits' table, in its own colour
comment draw_tile_picture unused: a picture of rows of tiles
comment draw_player_boxes the chosen players' boxes, and their scores
comment draw_player_box a player's box: its frame and label, its lives, its health bar
comment draw_box_frame_and_label a player's box frame and label in his colour
comment draw_lives a player's lives in his box
comment draw_health_bar a player's health bar in his box
comment player_box_colour a player's colour in the boxes
comment clear_lives_digits the lives' place in a box blank
comment clear_health_bar the health bar's place in a box blank
comment draw_box_frame a player's box frame
comment draw_box_label a player's box label 1UP to 4UP
comment print_stage_title unused: the stage's number and its name of the development
comment draw_player_message the message under a player's box
comment message_insert_coin INSERT COIN(S) in a player's box
comment message_press_start PRESS START blinking under a player's box
comment message_out_of_lives the knocked-out head of a player who lost his last life
comment message_clear the line under a player's box cleared
comment big_character_colour the colour of a big character
comment print_tall_text a text in tall characters: the rankings, CONTINUE, GAME OVER
comment draw_tall_character a tall character
comment erase_tall_character a tall character erased
comment tall_text_colour the colour of a text in tall characters
comment update_coins the coins: the coin counters, the coins inserted turned into credits
comment new_coin_presses the coin switches just pressed, with the coin's sound
comment pulse_coin_counter a coin slot's counter pulsed for each coin
comment count_coin a coin inserted, counted towards the slot's credits
comment add_credit_lives each slot's credits added to its player's lives
comment vblank_interrupt the frame's interrupt: the inputs, the coins, the sound, the palettes and the game state's frame
comment read_inputs the coins and the four players' inputs read, and the layers' scroll written
comment run_startup the steps after the reset
comment startup_begin the video chips set, the test mode when its switch is on, else the screen cleared
comment startup_wait the screen cleared
comment startup_end on to the attract mode
comment set_screen_flip the screen flipped by its DIP switch
comment run_attract the attract mode's frame, and a game when a player starts
comment run_game the game's frame by its scene
comment run_test_mode the test mode's frame
comment queue_job a job into the main loop's queue
comment queue_jobs a list of jobs into the main loop's queue
comment irq6_interrupt the second interrupt, which the board never raises
comment check_starts in the attract mode, the players with lives who press a button start the game
comment check_joins in a game, the players with lives who press a button join it
comment reset_player_1 the first player's score and health reset
comment reset_player_2 the second player's score and health reset
comment reset_player_3 the third player's score and health reset
comment reset_player_4 the fourth player's score and health reset
comment use_life_player_1 a life of the first player's used

# ======== the game's scenes (0061CE-006BE3) ========
# Game state 2, the game, runs the scene (060005) by the table at 00413A (00412E "lea $413a.l, A0"): 0 a new game, 1 the
# players' turtles, 2 an area's set-up, 3 its story and title screens, 4 the play, 5 the area's exit, 6 the ending, 7 the
# continue, 8 the ranking. Each scene runs its steps (060007, then 060009 and 06000B) by tables of its own, and most
# steps wait for the jobs they queue (060082) and the palette requests under way (060083). The demo runs scenes 1, 2, 3
# and 5-7 by a table of its own (attract_demo: 004CBC "lea $4cd2.l, A6"); 0600C2 is the scenes' frame counter.

# scene 0, a new game (one_player frames 761-762), by its step (0061CE "lea $61e8.l, A6"), then the players who join
# (0061E4 "bra $4206")
0061CE  func  scene_new_game
# when the jobs are done (0061F0 "tst.b $60082.l"): the play's work cleared (0061FA "bsr $44f8"), the four players' records
# (0061FE "bsr $4284", 00620A "bsr $42b4"), their four scores to 0 (00620E "move.w #$50f, D7", 006212 "bsr $2098"), the
# layers' scroll copies (006216 "clr.w $6005c.l", 006228 "clr.w $60062.l"), the difficulty from the DIP switches
# (00622E "move.b $60031.l, D0", 006234 "andi.b #$60, D0", 00623A "move.b D0, $60094.l"), the play time (006240 "clr.b
# $6111e.l"); then the next step
0061F0  func  new_game_reset
# the first area (006254 "clr.b $60120.l") and the next scene (00625A "addq.b #1, $60005.l")
006254  func  new_game_first_area

# choice: player N plays turtle N (the tall texts' "SELECT YOUR TURTLE", text 0xB of 00375C, is never printed)

# ======== the ending (006BE4-00781D) ========
# Scene 6, after the last area's exit (006BAE "cmpi.b #$9, $60121.l"). No scenario reaches it: it was seen with a scratch
# script (a one-player game, then 060005 poked to 6 and 060007, 060009 and 06000B to 0 at frame 1500): the Technodrome in
# a growing box (frames 1500-1530), reddening and exploding (1530-1840), its wreck (1840-2060), the epilogue typed
# (2060-3750) and darkened (3750-3930), the staff roll (3930-8120), GAME OVER (8120-8380), then the ranking.

# scene 6, the ending, by its step (006BE4 "lea $6bfa.l, A6")
006BE4  func  scene_ending
# step 0, by its substep (006C02 "lea $6c18.l, A6")
006C02  func  ending_setup
# the players taken out (006C2A "bsr $7174"), the layers cleared (006C34 "move.w #$7, D7"), the palettes 00A03E (006C3C
# "lea $a03e.l, A6", 006C46 "jsr $9242.l"), the palette effects, the objects and the sprites cleared
006C24  func  ending_clear
# when the jobs and the palettes are done: the box (006C74 "jsr $1864e.l") and the Technodrome's screen (006C7A "jsr
# $2ff44.l"), the sprites hidden, layer B's x 0x20 (006C8C "move.w #$20, $60060.l")
006C60  func  ending_show_technodrome
# the box grown (006C96 "jsr $18682.l") until it is open (006C9C "tst.b $63497.l"), then 0x28 frames and step 1 (006CA6
# "move.b #$28, $600c2.l", 006CAE "addq.b #1, $60007.l")
006C96  func  ending_open_box
# step 1, by its substep (006CBC "lea $6cde.l, A6"), the sprites drawn (006CD2 "jsr $46f08.l")
006CBC  func  ending_epilogue
# the 0x28 frames (006D06 "subq.b #1, $600c2.l"), then sound 22 (006D10 "move.w #$22, D7") and 0x78 frames
006D06  func  ending_pause
# the Technodrome reddened: the palette sets of the record at 0071DC copied over 0x78 frames (006D3A "lea $71dc.l, A6",
# 006D40 "jmp $71be.l"), then 0x96 frames (006D4C "move.b #$96, $600c2.l"): frames 1570-1690
006D30  func  ending_technodrome_glows
# the spent explosions removed (006D5C "bsr $7302"); every 8 frames a sound of the table at 006DFC (006D94 "lea $6dfc.l,
# A6") and an explosion (006DB2 "lea $623c0.l, A6", 006DCA "jsr $7286.l"), layer B shaken (006D82 "bsr $71fa"), the
# palette sets 0071E2 then 0071E8 (006DD0 "lea $71e2.l, A6", 006DE2 "lea $71e8.l, A6"); at 0x32 frames left sound 1A

comment scene_ending the ending after the last boss, by its steps

comment staff_roll_next_credit the next credit's title and name, or PRODUCED BY and the Konami logo after the last
comment staff_roll_type_name the credit's name typed in colours
comment staff_roll_hold_name the name held a moment, then its erasing started
comment staff_roll_erase_name the name erased, then the next credit
comment staff_roll_end the last pause of the staff roll
comment ending_game_over the GAME OVER after the ending, by its steps
comment ending_game_over_clear the screen and the objects cleared for GAME OVER

comment ending_game_over_skip a button ends GAME OVER early

comment clear_technodrome_top the top of the Technodrome taken off the picture

comment start_technodrome_explosion an explosion started at one of the Technodrome's places

comment start_staff_roll_portraits the four turtles' portraits put in for the staff roll
comment slide_staff_roll_portraits the portraits slide to the left round the screen
comment staff_roll_skip a button ends the staff roll after its first credit

comment start_credit_name the credit's name started in seven colour passes
comment start_credit_name_erase the credit's name recoloured and then blanked in eight passes

comment type_credit_name the seven passes of the name typed on
comment erase_credit_name the eight passes that take the name off typed on
comment type_credit_name_pass the next big character of a pass in its colour

# ======== the continue (00781E-007B0B) ========
# scene 7, when no player is left (play_frame 006996 "move.b #$7, $60005.l"; one_player frames 3073-4760), by its step
# (00781E "lea $783a.l, A6")
00781E  func  scene_continue
# step 0, by its substep (007846 "lea $7860.l, A6"), then the players who join (00785C "bra $4206")
007846  func  continue_setup
# the fixed layer cleared (job 0 with bit 0: 007868 "move.b #$1, $60082.l", 007870 "move.w #$1, D7")
007868  func  continue_clear_text
# when it is done: the boxes (00788A "move.w #$b7f, D7"), "CONTINUE" in tall characters (007892 "move.w #$f02, D7"), the
# count 20 (00789A "move.b #$14, $60006.l"), the sound stopped and the continue's music (0078B4 "move.b #$77, D7"):
# one_player frame 3075
007880  func  continue_start
# step 1, by its substep (0078CE "lea $78f8.l, A6"), the messages under the boxes (0078E4 "bsr $445e") and the boxes of

0078CE  func  continue_run

# (007906 "subq.b #1, $60008.l", 00791A "move.b #$48, $60008.l", 007922 "subq.b #1, $60006.l") drawn in big digits (00792E "bsr $27b2"; 19 at
# one_player frame 3080, 16 at 3300, 4 at 4200); a player who joins (00793E "move.b $600a6.l, D0", 007944 "or.b
# $600a1.l, D0") brings the play back with the area's music (0079DC "bsr $6a9c", 0079E0 "move.b #$4, $60005.l", 0079E8
# "move.b #$1, $60007.l"); from 16 down a player with no lives pressing attack or jump (00796C "andi.b #$30, D0", 007974
# "tst.w ($0,A5)", 007982 "andi.w #$7, D7") hurries it (00798A "move.b #$1, $60008.l"); at 0 GAME OVER (0079A2 "addq.b
# #1, $60007.l", 0079BA "move.w #$200, D7").
0078FC  func  continue_countdown

# ======== the ranking (007B0C-0088BF) ========
# The ranking in work RAM: 99 scores and the new one (BCD words 063500-0635C7: reset_ranking copies 0x32 longs) and the
# top ten's initials (4 bytes each from 0635C8, the new one at 0635F0); the players' places 0635F4-0635F7 (BCD, 0 none),
# the initial each enters 0635FA-0635FD (3 done), the players entering 0635FE, their buttons held 0635FF, and 0634A0
# set when a name was entered. 0635F8 is a frame counter, and before that the turtles left to draw in black.

# scene 8, after the continue's GAME OVER or the ending (one_player frames 4760-5310), by its step (007B0C "lea
# $7b22.l, A6")
007B0C  func  scene_ranking
# step 0, by its substep (007B42 "lea $7b58.l, A6")
007B42  func  ranking_setup
# the layers cleared (007B6E "move.b #$1, $60082.l", 007B76 "move.w #$7, D7")
007B64  func  ranking_clear_layers
# when they are: the objects and the sprites cleared, the palettes 00A02E (007B9C "lea $a02e.l, A6"), the palette effects
007B86  func  ranking_clear
# when the palettes are in: the places cleared (007BD0 "move.l D0, $635f4.l"), the tile banks 0 (007BDE "move.b D0,
# $106b00.l"), layer B's scroll (007BF0 "move.w #$0, $60062.l", 007BF8 "move.w #$60, $60060.l"), no name entered (007C00
# "clr.b $634a0.l"), step 1
007BBA  func  ranking_reset
# each player's score (+4 of his record: 007C40 "move.w ($4,A3), ($2,A6,D6.w)") into the ranking from its end up:
# swapped with each score it reaches (007C4E "cmp.w D0, D1", 007C54 "move.w ($2,A6,D6.w), (A6,D6.w)"), its place one up in
# BCD from 99 (007C66 "sbcd D1, D0"), the initials "AAA" moved with it in the top ten (007C34 "move.l #$41414100,
# ($28,A5)", 007C6C "cmpi.b #$11, D0", 007C92 "move.l D3, (A5,D2.w)"); then each place one down for each player put in
# later at or above it (007CC0 "cmp.b (A4,D6.w), D0", 007CCC "abcd D1, D0")
007C16  func  rank_players_scores
# for each player with a place (007CF2 "tst.b (A0,D7.w)"): his column's frame (007CFA "bsr $8150"), label (007CFE "bsr
# $81cc"), score (007D08 "jsr $1fb8.l"), place (007D10 "bsr $8212") and turtle's picture (007D20 "bsr $8b58"); the
# others' pictures kept for later in black (007CEA "move.b #$4f, $635f8.l", 007D1A "bclr D0, $635f8.l"): one_player
# frame 4780, "99TH" and Leonardo
007CE2  func  show_ranked_players
# with no player at all back to the attract mode (007D58 "cmpi.b #$4, D6", 007D60 "bra $7fec"); sound 71 (007D64
# "move.b #$71, D7"), the background (007D6E "bsr $804a"), the other turtles in black (007D72 "move.b $635f8.l, D7",
# 007D78 "bsr $8b58"); with a player in the top ten (007D4A "cmpi.b #$11, (A6,D7.w)") the name entry for those (007DB8
# "clr.b (A5,D7.w)", 007DBC "bset D7, D6", 007DCC "move.b D6, $635fe.l") for 0x5DC frames (007DE6 "move.w #$5dc,
# $635f8.l", 007DEE "addq.b #2, $60007.l"), else 0x1E0 frames to wait (007D7C "move.w #$1e0, $635f8.l")
007D32  func  ranking_choose_entry
# 0x1E0 frames or a player's attack or jump (007DF6 "subq.w #1, $635f8.l", 007E18 "andi.b #$30, D7"): the music faded
# and 60 frames more (007FB6 "move.w #$3c, $635f8.l", 007FBE "move.b #$7, $60007.l"): one_player frames 4770-5250
007DF6  func  ranking_wait
# the name entry, every 8 frames (007E3A "move.w $600a4.l, D7", 007E40 "andi.w #$7, D7"): the initials of the players
# entering drawn (007E54 "bsr $809c"); with the inputs of the last four frames (007E74 "move.l ($0,A6), D7", 007E84
# "move.l D7, $60020.l") the joystick turns the letter being entered back or on among 32 (007F08 "btst #$0, D6", 007F32
# "btst #$1, D6", 007F12 "andi.b #$1f, D4") with sound 1, a new attack or jump (007EC4 "btst D7, $635ff.l") takes the
# next initial with sound 1D (007ECE "addq.b #1, (A3,D7.w)", 007ED6 "move.b #$1d, D0"); when all are done (007F66
# "cmpi.b #$4, D0") or the time runs out (007E30 "subq.w #1, $635f8.l") a name was entered (007F78 "move.b #$1,
# $634a0.l"); no scenario enters one
007E30  func  enter_initials
# the 0x78 frames after the entry (007FA6 "subq.w #1, $635f8.l"), then leave_ranking (007FB2 "bra $7fd4"); 007FB6 on is
# where the wait and the entry's time out end: 60 frames and step 7
007FA6  func  ranking_entry_done
# the last 60 frames (007FC8 "subq.w #1, $635f8.l"), then leave_ranking: one_player frames 5250-5310
007FC8  func  ranking_end_wait
# with a name entered and no player with lives left (007FD4 "tst.b $600b7.l", 007FDE "tst.b $634a0.l") the attract
# mode's BEST 10 PLAYERS (00801A on: 008022 "move.b #$6, $60003.l"), else back_to_attract
007FD4  func  leave_ranking
# the attract mode from its first step, no game on (007FEC "move.b #$1, $60001.l", 007FF4 "clr.b $60003.l", 008012 "clr.b
# $600a3.l"); 00801A on, the same at step 6, BEST 10 PLAYERS (008022 "move.b #$6, $60003.l"): one_player frame 5310
007FEC  func  back_to_attract
# layer B's 30 rows of 40 tiles, each row one tile of the table at 00807E (00804A "lea $104f18.l, A6", 008050 "lea
# $807e.l, A5", 00806C "move.b D5, ($1,A6,D4.w)"): the background of the ranking and of BEST 10 PLAYERS, blue to green
00804A  func  draw_ranking_background
# a player's three initials (00809C "lea $840a.l, A6", 0080B0 "lea $635c8.l, A4") under his column, the one he is
# entering in colour 0xA0 (0080DA "cmpi.b #$0, (A3,D7.w)", 0080E4 "move.b #$a0, D0")
00809C  func  draw_initials
# a letter in tall characters of the font at 002884 in the colour given (00812E "lea $2884.l, A2", 00813A "move.b D0,
# ($0,A6)", 008148 "move.b ($1,A2,D5.w), ($81,A6)")
00812E  func  draw_initial
# a player's column's frame in his colour (008160 "bsr $82a8"): its top two rows from the tiles at 00841A (008168 "lea
# $841a.l, A5"), 0x18 rows of edges (008192 "moveq #$17, D5", 008198 "move.b #$1a, ($1,A6)", 0081A2 "move.b #$1d,
# ($11,A6)"), its bottom from 00842C (0081B6 "lea $842c.l, A5"): one_player frame 4780
008150  func  draw_ranking_frame
# the player's label "1UP"-"4UP" from the boxes' labels at 00332A (0081DA "lea $332a.l, A5") at the column's top (0081CC
# "lea $83ea.l, A6")
0081CC  func  draw_ranking_label
# the player's place (008212 "lea $83fa.l, A6"): its units and tens in tall characters (008258 "lea $2884.l, A5", 008282
# "andi.w #$f0, D5": the tens left out when 0) and "ST", "ND", "RD" or "TH" from the tiles at 008436 (008220 "lea
# $8436.l, A5", 008236 "bcs $823e", 00823A "move.b #$3, D5")
008212  func  draw_ranking_place
# the player's colour: text_colours by the player, odd (0082A8 "lea $1f1a.l, A5", 0082B2 "ori.b #$1, D6"), as
# player_box_colour does
0082A8  func  ranking_frame_colour
# at the reset (00121E "jsr $82b8.l"): the ranking's defaults from 0082E6, 0x32 longs of scores and 0xB of initials
# (0082BE "lea $82e6.l, A5", 0082CC "move.l #$31, D7", 0082D8 "move.l #$a, D7") into 063500, the top score 0x1000 (0082C4
# "move.w #$1000, $60076.l")
0082B8  func  reset_ranking

comment ranking_frame_colour the colour of a player's column
comment reset_ranking the ranking's default scores and initials, and the top score

# ======== the attract mode's steps (0088C0-0091A3) ========
# Game state 1 runs its step (060003) by the table at 004100 (0040BE "lea $4100.l, A0"): 0 its start, 1 the coin's
# screen, 2 the opening, 3 the title, 4 the demo, 5 a profile, 6 BEST 10 PLAYERS, 7 nothing, 8 the end; each step by
# its scene (060005) and scene step (060007). A coin (060327) sends the steps to the coin's screen (008B1E).

# step 0 (attract frame 9): straight on to step 1 (0088C0 "addq.b #1, $60003.l")
0088C0  func  attract_begin
# step 1, the coin's screen, by its scene (0088DA "lea $88f0.l, A6"): at the start a pass that goes on to the opening
# when no player has lives (attract frames 10-21); after a coin the boxes, PRESS START and the players' turtles
# (one_player frames 706-761)
0088DA  func  attract_coin_screen
# its scene 0, by its step (0088F8 "lea $890e.l, A6")
0088F8  func  coin_screen_setup
# 2 frames (00891E "move.b #$2, $60004.l")
00891E  func  coin_screen_pause
# when they are over (008938 "subq.b #1, $60004.l") the play's work cleared (008942 "jsr $44f8.l") and the layers
# (00894E "move.w #$7, D7")
00892E  func  coin_screen_clear
# the attract mode's palettes and palette effects (008968 "jsr $9a5c.l", 008974 "jsr $9aa8.l")
00895E  func  coin_screen_palettes
# when the palettes are in: the screen's flip (008996 "bsr $4072"), the layers' scroll reset (00899A "bsr $8c6a"); with
# a player's lives (00899E "move.w $60100.l, D0", 0089B0 "or.w $60118.l, D0"), free play (0089B6 "or.b $60091.l, D0") or
# 060320, sound 7E and scene 1 (0089C8 "move.w #$7e, D7", 0089D2 "addq.b #1, $60005.l"), else the opening (0089E0
# "addq.b #1, $60003.l")
008982  func  coin_screen_check_credits
# its scene 1, by its step (0089F4 "lea $8a0a.l, A6")
0089F4  func  coin_screen_show
# the four boxes with their frames, labels and lives (008A1E "move.w #$b5f, D7"): one_player frame 717
008A1E  func  coin_screen_boxes
# a frame (008A2E "addq.b #1, $60007.l")
008A2E  func  coin_screen_first_wait
# another (008A36 "addq.b #1, $60007.l")
008A36  func  coin_screen_second_wait
# the texts' colour, the Konami logo and "(C) KONAMI 1989" (008A3E "lea $8abe.l, A5", 008A44 "bsr $4184": game texts 7
# and 0xA); the slots' credits summed and the sum dropped (008A4A "add.b $60320.l, D0", 008A6A "or.b $60091.l, D0",
# 008A70 "nop"); the pictures of the players with lives (008A7C "tst.w ($0,A6)", 008A84 "bset D6, D7", 008A9A "bsr
# $8b58"), each his own turtle (008A90 "move.l #$10203, $600c4.l"); 10 into 060004 and sound 6F (008AB2 "move.w #$6f,
# D7"): one_player frame 720, Leonardo's picture
008A3E  func  coin_screen_draw
# the messages under the boxes (008AC6 "bsr $445e"); a new coin (008ACA "tst.b $60327.l") the boxes it changed (008B08
# "move.w #$b10, D7", 008B0C "or.b $600a2.l, D7") and the pictures again (008B16 "subq.b #1, $60007.l"); after a coin's
# sound sound 6F again (008AD8 "tst.b $60152.l"); 060004 counted down every 64 frames (008AFA "subq.b #1, $60004.l") to
# no effect (008B00 "bne $8b06", 008B04 "rts"): the screen stays until a player starts (one_player frames 721-761)
008AC6  func  coin_screen_wait
# where every attract step goes with a coin (attract_title 0049C0 "bra $8b1e"): the coin's screen from its start (008B36
# "move.b #$1, $60003.l", 008B1E "clr.b $60005.l"), a demo's players out (008B3E "clr.b $600a1.l", 008B44 "clr.b
# $600a6.l"): one_player frame 706
008B1E  func  go_to_coin_screen
# the four turtles' pictures (008B58 "moveq #$3, D6"), on into draw_turtle_pictures
008B58  func  draw_four_turtle_pictures
# the pictures D7's bits pick, from the last one D6 says (008B66 "btst D6, D7"), each two sprites into the K051960's
# sprite RAM (008B5A "lea $140400.l, A6"): their codes and colours from the turtle the player plays (008B60 "lea
# $600c4.l, A4", 008B6E "move.b (A4,D6.w), D5", 008B8C "move.b (A5,D5.w), (A6,D5.w)"), their places from the player's
# (008BBA "move.b ($4,A5,D5.w), ($4,A6,D5.w)"), from the table at 008BEA or with D7's bit 7 the one at 008C2A (008B82
# "lea $8c2a.l, A5"), and with bit 6 in colour 8, black (008BD2 "move.b #$8, ($3,A6)"): the ranking's other turtles
008B5A  func  draw_turtle_pictures
# the layers' scroll copies and their registers to 0 (008C72 "move.w D0, $6005c.l", 008C84 "move.w D3, $60062.l",
# 008CBA "move.b D0, $106400.l", 008CDC "move.b D3, $106019.l"), with 0600C8-0600CF and 060054-060057
008C6A  func  reset_layers_scroll
# step 2, the opening (attract frames 21-745): a coin plays 7E and goes to the coin's screen (008CE4 "tst.b $60327.l",
# 008CF8 "bra $8b1e"), else by its scene (008CFC "lea $8d12.l, A6")
008CE4  func  attract_opening

008D40  func  opening_setup_clear

008D66  func  opening_setup_palettes

008D8A  func  opening_setup_priority
# scene 1 with 0xB2 counts (008DC2 "addq.b #1, $60005.l", 008DCE "move.b #$b2, $600a8.l"), the sprites hidden,
# start_opening (008DDC "jsr $2f790.l"), the tile banks 0 (008DE2 "move.b #$0, $106b00.l"): attract frame 30
008DB8  func  opening_setup_start
# run_opening and the sprites (008E1E "jsr $2f8aa.l", 008E2A "jsr $46f92.l"); every 4 frames a count less (008E0A "andi.b
# #$3, D7", 008E12 "subi.b #$1, $600a8.l"), at 0 the title (008E32 "addq.b #1, $60003.l"): attract frames 30-745
008E04  func  opening_show
# step 6, BEST 10 PLAYERS (attract frames 2776-3040; after the ranking when a name was entered): a coin goes to the coin's
# screen (008E46 "tst.b $60327.l", 008E50 "bra $8b1e"), else by its scene (008E54 "lea $8e6a.l, A6")
008E46  func  attract_best_players
# its scene 0, by its step (008E72 "lea $8e88.l, A6")
008E72  func  best_players_setup
# the play's work cleared (008E9E "jsr $44f8.l") and the layers (008EAA "move.w #$7, D7")
008E94  func  best_players_clear

008EBA  func  best_players_palettes

# scroll (008F26 "move.w #$60, $60060.l"), the background (008F2E "bsr $804a"), the four turtles' pictures by the second
# table (008F34 "move.b #$8f, D7", 008F42 "bsr $8b5a"), the table (008F46 "bsr $902e"), scene 1 (008F4A "addq.b #1,

008EDE  func  best_players_draw

008F9C  func  best_players_pause
# the frames (008FB6 "subq.b #1, $60002.l"): the turtles' sprites in use 4 to the right each frame (008FC8 "btst #$7,
# ($0,A6)", 008FF0 "addi.w #$4, $635f8.l", 009000 "move.b $635f8.l, ($6,A6)"), then the next step (00901A "addq.b #1,
# $60003.l"): attract frames 2785-3040
008FB6  func  best_players_scroll
# the table: "BEST 10 PLAYERS" and the places in tall characters (00902E "lea $9190.l, A5", 009034 "bsr $4184": tall texts

00902E  func  draw_best_players

009042  func  draw_best_players_initials

0090B0  func  draw_best_players_character

0090C4  func  draw_best_players_scores
# step 7: nothing, straight on to step 8 (00919C "addq.b #1, $60003.l"): attract frame 3040
00919C  func  attract_spare_step

# ======== the layers' maps, the code (01E050-01FDC9) ========
# The areas' two tile layers, the K052109's layer A (tile RAM 102000) and layer B (104000), keep 64 bytes each at 063340
# (A) and 063380 (B): x and y (24.8), the speeds (+0C, +0E), the map, its blocks and width (+12, +16, +10), and the row
# and the column the scroll has brought into view (+30 and +34 set by queue_scroll_row: 01B526 "subq.b #1, $63334.l";
# +2C and +35 by auto_scroll: 01AD46 "subq.b #1, $63335.l"). The code works on a copy at 063300: load, draw, save.
# The floor map (061800, 0x40 cells a row) gets walls (FF, and FE or FD where a wall blocks one side: turtle_wall_contacts
# 01D774 "cmpi.b #-$2, D1" right, 01D7A0 "cmpi.b #-$3, D1" left) and floor (01).

# ---- the whole layers and the working copy ----
# the whole layer at A0 (8 rows of 16 blocks: 01E082 "moveq #$8, D7", 01E084 "moveq #$10, D6") from the working layer's
# map and blocks (01E074 "movea.l $63312.l, A2", 01E08C "movea.l $63316.l, A4"), and each block's floor cells into the
# floor map (01E07C "lea $61800.l, A1", 01E0AA "move.b ($1,A4), ($0,A1)"; the tiles from +11: 01E0C2 "move.b ($11,A4),
# ($1,A0)"): the street's and the building site's starts (01E3EC "bsr $1e050", 01E550 "bsr $1e050")
01E050  func  draw_whole_layer_and_floor
# the same without the floor map: each block's colour byte to the even addresses, its tile codes to the odd ones
# (01E15E "move.b ($0,A4), D1", 01E172 "move.b ($1,A4), ($1,A0)"); the other starts and the exits' new screens
01E11E  func  draw_whole_layer
# the 64 bytes of layer A into the working layer (01E708 "lea $63340.l, A3", 01E718 "lea $63300.l, A4", 01E720 "move.l
# (A3)+, (A4)+")
01E708  func  load_layer_a
# the same for layer B (01E712 "lea $63380.l, A3")
01E712  func  load_layer_b
# the working layer back into layer A's (01E728 "lea $63340.l, A4", 01E738 "lea $63300.l, A3")
01E728  func  save_layer_a
# the same for layer B (01E732 "lea $63380.l, A4")
01E732  func  save_layer_b
# the working layer's new row (13 blocks across at 063330's place: 01E9C2 "tst.b $63334.l", 01EA4C "moveq #$d, D7") and
# its new column (four tiles down a block, eight blocks: 01EAAA "tst.b $63335.l", 01EAB6 "move.l $6332c.l, D0", 01EB36
# "move.b (A3,D4.w), ($80,A0)") into the tile RAM at 0633CC (01E9F8 "add.l $633cc.l, D0")
01E9C2  func  draw_new_row_and_column
# the same with the blocks' floor cells into the floor map (01E740 "tst.b $63334.l", 01E780 "addi.l #$61800, D1",
# 01E7FA "move.b ($1,A3,D5.w), (A1)"): the street and the building site
01E740  func  draw_new_row_and_column_and_floor
# layer A's and layer B's x (01ECB6 "move.w D0, $6005c.l", 01ECC8 "move.w D0, $60060.l") and y (01ECD6 "move.b D0,
# $6005f.l", 01ECE4 "move.b D0, $60063.l") into the copies the VBLANK writes to the K052109's scroll registers
# (003F32 "move.b $6005f.l, $106018.l", 003F46 "move.b D7, $106400.l")
01ECAA  func  set_layers_scroll

# ---- the area's start ----
# the first step of an area's entry (019FC2 "jmp $1e1ba.l", 019FE6 "jsr $1e1ba.l", 01A156 "jsr $1e1ba.l"): the layers'
# variables cleared, the stage's record of area_layers into layer A and layer B (01E26E "move.l (A6,D0.w), $63340.l",
# 01E27E "move.l ($8,A6,D0.w), $63380.l"), the stage's start (01E2C8 "lea $1e2da.l, A6") and the scroll (01E2D4 "jmp
# $1ecaa.l")
01E1BA  func  start_area_layers
# stage 0: the floor map's top rows walls and the rest floor (01E308 "moveq #-$1, D0", 01E310 "cmpa.l #$61c7f, A0",
# 01E318 "move.l #$1010101, D0"), the first scroll segment (01E326 "jsr $1a704.l"), layer B drawn (01E330 "lea
# $104000.l, A0", 01E336 "bsr $1e11e")
01E302  func  start_layers_building
# stage 1: the floor map (01E34E "cmpa.l #$61cbf, A0") with a slope of left walls (cells FD from 061804: 01E364 "lea
# $61804.l, A0", 01E36E "moveq #-$3, D3"), April at the window (object 47: 01E386 "lea $62eb0.l, A0", 01E3B6 "lea
# $2d8ae.l, A6"), layer B drawn
01E340  func  start_layers_apartment
# stage 2: the floor map from the map's own blocks (01E3EC "bsr $1e050")
01E3DC  func  start_layers_street
# stage 3: walls, floor, a band of cells FC and floor again (01E404 "cmpa.l #$61aff, A0", 01E41A "move.l #$fcfcfcfc,
# D0", 01E420 "cmpa.l #$61dbf, A0"), both layers drawn (01E440 "lea $102000.l, A0", 01E452 "lea $104000.l, A0")
01E3F6  func  start_layers_sewer
# stage 4: the floor map (01E470 "cmpa.l #$61c7f, A0"), layer B
01E462  func  start_layers_car_street
# stage 5: the floor map (01E4AE "cmpa.l #$619ff, A0"), layer B
01E4A0  func  start_layers_highway
# stage 6: the floor map (01E4EC "cmpa.l #$61bbf, A0"), both layers (01E51E "lea $102000.l, A0")
01E4DE  func  start_layers_skateboard
# stage 7: both layers, layer B with its floor map (01E538 "lea $102000.l, A0", 01E550 "bsr $1e050")
01E52E  func  start_layers_building_site
# stage 9: one enemy counted from the start (Krang: 01E55A "move.b #$1, $6120c.l"), then stage 8's
01E55A  func  start_layers_final
# stage 8: the floor map (01E570 "cmpa.l #$61bff, A0"), layer B
01E562  func  start_layers_technodrome

# ---- every frame of play ----
# 0068E2 (00692C "jsr $1ebce.l"): the area's frame counters (01EBCE "addq.w #1, $61104.l"), the stage's layers (01EBF6
# "lea $1ecec.l, A6"), then the hurry sign: a count of the frames the layers stand still before the area's end (01EC02
# "move.b $6335b.l, D0", 01EC08 "or.b $6339b.l, D0", 01EC0E "or.b $633ec.l, D0") with fewer than 3 enemies (01EC18

# "lea $62f50.l, A0", 01EC4E "move.w #$158, ($c,A0)") with animation 02DB76, or 02DBAE in area 2's diagonal (01EC5A

01F8B8  func  drive_foot_car

# ---- April's own field ----
# which way her next step goes: 0 right, FF left (01EE70 "tst.b ($1a,A0)", 01EE8C "eori.b #$ff, ($1a,A0)"; cleared at
# her start: 01E3B2 "clr.b ($1a,A0)"); the C shows x_speed's high byte today
scoped object april 1A stepping_left u8
# April's object, loaded in these two (01E386 "lea $62eb0.l, A0", 01EE3A "lea $62eb0.l, A0")

local april_wait_for_rescue 01EE3A a0 april object.april

comment draw_whole_layer_and_floor a whole layer drawn from its map, and its floor cells into the floor map
comment draw_whole_layer a whole layer drawn from its map
comment load_layer_a layer A's variables into the working layer
comment load_layer_b layer B's variables into the working layer
comment save_layer_a the working layer back into layer A's variables
comment save_layer_b the working layer back into layer B's variables
comment draw_new_row_and_column the row and the column of a layer the scroll has brought into view
comment draw_new_row_and_column_and_floor the row and the column the scroll has brought into view, with their floor cells
comment set_layers_scroll the two layers' places into the scroll the frame shows
comment start_area_layers the area's layers from its record: the floor map, the first scroll segment and the layers drawn
comment start_layers_building the burning building's floor map and layer
comment start_layers_apartment April's apartment's floor map, its left wall, April at the window, and the layer
comment start_layers_street the street's layer and its floor map
comment start_layers_sewer the sewer's floor map and both layers
comment start_layers_car_street the street of the parked cars' floor map and layer
comment start_layers_highway the highway's floor map and layer
comment start_layers_skateboard the skateboard ride's floor map and both layers
comment start_layers_building_site the building site's layers and floor map
comment start_layers_final the last area starts with Krang counted as an enemy, then as the Technodrome
comment start_layers_technodrome the Technodrome's floor map and layer
comment update_area_layers every frame of play: the area's layers, and the HURRY! sign and the missiles when the players dawdle
comment update_layers_building the burning building's layer, the first cry of attack, and the wall at its end
comment update_layers_apartment the apartment's layer, the wall at its end, and April
comment april_wait_for_rescue April at the window cries for help and paces, kept at her place
comment update_layers_street the street's layers: the TV in the shop window shows April and Shredder, and a turtle's answer
comment draw_tv_picture a picture on the TV in the shop window
comment animate_tv_picture April talking on the TV
comment update_layers_sewer the sewer's two layers and the wall at its end
comment update_layers_car_street the street's layer, and April tied up at its end
comment update_layers_final the last area's layer
comment update_layers_building_site the building site's layers, and Splinter hanging tied at its end
comment update_layers_technodrome the Technodrome's layers by the step: to the lift, the ride, off the lift
comment technodrome_reach_lift the layers up to the lift, and the ride starts when every turtle is on it
comment technodrome_ride_lift the lift's ride: the scroll speeds up, loops the shaft, slows down and stops
comment technodrome_leave_lift the layers after the ride, the floor map set again and the wall at the end
comment fill_floor_map_rows rows of the floor map filled with one kind of cell
comment place_lift_edge the lift's striped edge kept at its place while it is near
comment update_layers_highway the highway's layer and the cars full of Foot soldiers
comment highway_first_car the first car full of Foot soldiers comes
comment highway_first_car_drive the first car drives past

comment highway_second_car_drive the second car drives past
comment highway_third_car the third car comes, in other colours
comment highway_third_car_drive the third car drives past
comment highway_cars_over no more cars
comment drive_foot_car the car's wheels turn and it drives across the screen
comment update_layers_skateboard the skateboard ride: the far background and the road scroll by themselves

comment skateboard_road_go_on the road goes on to new stretches
comment skateboard_road_to_second_loop the road on to its second stretch
comment skateboard_road_second_loop the road goes round its second stretch twenty times, then on
comment skateboard_road_to_third_loop the road on to its third stretch
