# ======== the object engine (046EEA-047D97, src/engine/sprites.c) ========
# the 128 sprites of the K051960's sprite RAM off, each with its own priority (046EEA "lea $140400.l, A0", 046EF2 "moveq
# #$4f, D1", 046EF4 "move.b D1, ($0,A0)", 046EFA "andi.b #$7f, D1": bit 7, shown, clear); the scenes' and the areas'
# starts (006846 "jsr $46eea.l")
046EEA  func  hide_all_sprites
# every frame: each object drawn into the draw list at 061400 by screen_y + depth + 0x38 (046F30 "move.w ($34,A0), D0",
# 046F38 "addi.w #$38, D0"), the objects of a row linked by draw_next (046F66 "move.w A0, ($3e,A2)"), the first row and
# the count (046F72 "move.w D5, $61204.l", 046F78 "move.b D6, $61206.l"); one out of the list (0x180 or more) or not
# drawn has its animation stepped on all the same (046F8C "bsr $471ae")
046F08  func  build_draw_list
# the attack check, every frame (006938 "jsr $47204.l"): the turtles' attacks against half the objects 4-39, every other
# one by frame_count (04720A "btst #$0, $600a5.l", 047318 "adda.w #$a0, A1"), those objects' attacks against the turtles
# (047342 "lea $62000.l, A1"), and two of the objects 4-11 (attack_check_objects: 04742E "lea $471f4.l, A0") against the
# objects 12-23 (047450 "lea $623c0.l, A1"); a hit when the attack box (the frame's, attack_depth not negative: 047232
# "tst.w ($8,A2)") meets the body box: routine 4 (0472CE "move.w #$400, ($4,A1)"), the hit, the damage off the health,
# and an enemy defeated by a turtle credited to its player (0472FE "move.w #$400, D7", 04730C "jsr $4164.l")
047204  func  check_attacks
# turtle_collide (01AB7E "jsr $47534.l"): the turtle against the objects 4-11 by the body boxes (047534 "lea $62140.l,
# A3", 04753A "moveq #$7, D7"): a side blocked into its contacts (047632 "or.b D1, ($1,A0)"), on top of one (0475F2
# "ori.b #$10, ($0,A0)", 047600 "move.b D0, ($32,A0)"), carried by one whose kind has bit 7 (04760E "tst.b ($2,A3)",
# 04761C "add.l D2, ($12,A0)")
047534  func  turtle_collide_with_objects
# the same for an enemy (enemy_think 048B56 "jsr $47662.l", enemy_react 048EE6 "jsr $47662.l"), keeping its contacts'
# top 3 bits (04766A "andi.b #$e0, ($1,A0)") and never carried
047662  func  enemy_collide_with_objects
# the area's start (006840 "jsr $4776e.l"): the players' slots at 062FA0 given their turtles (04776E "move.l #$62000,
# $62fa0.l") and cleared (04779C "movem.l D0-D2, $62fa4.l"), the first slot and turtle the nearest (0477BC "move.l
# #$62fa0, $61226.l", 0477C6 "move.l #$62000, $6122e.l"), a byte by the area into 06124C (0477D0 "lea $48830.l, A6")
04776E  func  start_player_slots
# the object loop, every frame (006932 "jsr $477ea.l"): the enemies' spawns (0477EA "jsr $47a32.l"), the players' slots
# (0477F6 "bsr $4783c"), and every object in use of 4-39 (0477FA "lea $62140.l, A0", 047832 "cmpa.l #$62c80, A0") runs
# its kind's routine (047812 "lea $53896.l, A6", 04781E "move.b ($4,A0), D0", 047828 "jsr (A6)") and gets its screen
# place (04782A "bsr $4789c")
0477EA  func  run_objects
# each slot whose turtle is in use (04784A "tst.b (A5)"): enemy_behind cleared (047850 "clr.b ($44,A5)") and counted;
# the slot with the fewest enemies so far into 061208 (047862 "move.l A6, $61208.l"), its grab_time counted down
# (047870 "subq.b #1, ($8,A6)"); the turtles in play into 061207, and the enemies about (06120C) shared among them, at
# least 1, into 06120D (04788E "divu.w D1, D0", 047894 "move.b D0, $6120d.l")
04783C  func  update_player_slots
# its screen place from its place in the area and the spawn scroll (0478A8 "move.l $61218.l, D1", 0478B4 "addi.w #$1a0,
# D0", 0478C0 "move.l $6121c.l, D3", 0478CC "addi.w #$100, D2"), and flags bit 5 when it is off the screen by the
# margins spawn_offsets has for its kind (0478D4 "bclr #$5, ($0,A0)", 0478DA "lea $53a5e.l, A4", 0478EE "cmpi.w #$1c0,
# D0")
04789C  func  update_object_screen_place
# A6 = the first free object (flags 0) of the group D0 picks, from object_groups and object_group_counts (047C02 "lea
# $47bde.l, A6", 047C12 "lea $47be2.l, A6", 047C1E "tst.b ($0,A6)"); Z set when there is one
047C02  func  take_free_object_in_group
# an object from the record in A5 (x, y and height from the object in A0, then its group, kind and parameter): in use,
# routine 0, its own colours, nothing a turtle throws (047C30 "move.b ($5,A5), D0", 047C3A "move.w #$100, ($0,A6)",
# 047C40 "move.w ($6,A5), ($2,A6)", 047C50 "clr.b ($4b,A6)"), placed by the record; the soldiers out of doors,
# elevators, windows and manholes, the Mousers out of their holes, Shredder's clones, what the tire and manhole
# soldiers throw
047C30  func  start_object_from_record

# ======== the palettes (src/video/palettes.c) ========
# The palette RAM's four banks (palette_bank_addresses; colour bases of runtime/src/video.c): 0 the fixed layer's, 1
# layer A's, 2 layer B's, 3 the sprites' (16 palettes: two halves of 8, 080400 and 080600)

# ---- the requests ----
# every frame (the VBLANK, 003DC4's calls): the next bank of a request under way (009272 "tst.b $60bf8.l", 00927C "tst.b
# $60bf9.l", 009286 "tst.b $60bfa.l"), or the sprites' second half (009290 "tst.b $60bfb.l", 0092A2 "lea $9334.l, A0"), or
# the next request of the queue by its kind (0092CC "andi.w #$3f00, D0", 0092EA "lea $9310.l, A0"); 060083 set while one
# is under way (009306 "move.b #$1, $60083.l"): the scenes wait for it
009272  func  run_palette_requests
comment run_palette_requests a bank of the palette requests a frame: the one under way, or the next one queued
# kinds 0 and 9, and the second halves of the kinds that have none (009310's and 009334's tables): nothing (009358 "rts")
009358  func  palette_request_none
# kinds 7 and 8 (00932C, 009330): nothing (0095D0 "rts")
0095D0  func  palette_request_spare
comment palette_request_none a palette request that does nothing
comment palette_request_spare a spare palette request kind that does nothing
# kind 1: the area's palette set (00935A "movea.l #$9f56, A6", 009390 "move.b $60121.l, D5": area_palettes by the
# stage), the next bank of the mask (009362 "btst D6, D7", 00937C "move.b D7, $60bf8.l"), the sprites' bank with its second
# half to come (0093CA "move.b #$1, $60bfb.l")
00935A  func  palette_request_area
# its sprites' second half (0093E2 "move.b #$3, D6", 0093E6 "move.b #$8, D7", 0093EA "bra $935a")
0093E2  func  palette_request_area_second_half
# kind 2: the palette set at A6 (a list a bank), kept for its banks to come (0093EE "move.l A6, $60bfc.l")
0093EE  func  palette_request_set
# the set's next bank (009412 "move.b D6, D4", 009416 "move.b D7, $60bf9.l", 00942A "movea.l (A6,D6.w), A6"; 009464
# "move.b $60bf9.l, D7" goes on with it)
0093F4  func  palette_request_set_next_bank
# its sprites' second half (009472 "movea.l $60bfc.l, A6", 00947C "move.b #$8, D7")
009472  func  palette_request_set_second_half
# kind 3: the palette list at A6 into the first bank of the mask only (009486 "btst D6, D7", 0094DC "bsr $95e6"), the
# sprites' second half to come (0094D4 "move.b #$3, $60bfb.l")
009484  func  palette_request_list
# its sprites' second half (0094E2 "movea.l $60bfc.l, A6", 0094F0 "bra $9484")
0094E2  func  palette_request_list_second_half
# kind 4: the 16 colours at A6 into one palette: the low nibble of the request's byte the palette, the high one the bank
# (0094F6 "andi.l #$f, D7", 0094FC "andi.b #$f0, D6", 00950C "lsl.w #6, D7", 009512 "move.b (A6)+, ($1,A5)")
0094F2  func  palette_request_palette
# kind 5: single colours (a palette, a colour, its two bytes) until a negative byte (009544 "move.b (A6)+, D6", 009546
# "bmi $9560", 009556 "move.b (A6)+, ($1,A4)")
009526  func  palette_request_colours
# kind 6: the banks of the mask black, a bank a frame (00957E "move.b D7, $60bfa.l", 0095BC "bsr $9602"), the sprites'
# second half to come (0095B4 "move.b #$6, $60bfb.l")
009562  func  palette_request_black
# its sprites' second half (0095CA "move.b #$8, D7", 0095CE "bra $9562")
0095CA  func  palette_request_black_second_half
# D7 + 1 palettes of 16 colours at A5 black (009602 "moveq #$f, D6", 009604 "clr.b ($1,A5)", 009608 "clr.b ($3,A5)")
009602  func  blacken_palettes
comment palette_request_area the area's palettes into the banks asked for, a bank a frame
comment palette_request_area_second_half the second half of the sprites' palettes of the area's set
comment palette_request_set a set of palettes into the banks asked for, a bank a frame
comment palette_request_set_next_bank the set's next bank
comment palette_request_set_second_half the second half of the sprites' palettes of the set
comment palette_request_list a list of palettes into one bank
comment palette_request_list_second_half the second half of the sprites' palettes of the list
comment palette_request_palette sixteen colours into one palette
comment palette_request_colours single colours into a bank's palettes
comment palette_request_black the banks asked for turned black, a bank a frame
comment palette_request_black_second_half the second half of the sprites' palettes turned black
comment blacken_palettes palettes turned black

# ---- the fade (copy_palette_sets' steps, by 063411) ----
# The palette RAM's parts in 063418's mask (5 of them: 00975C's table, 080000, 080800, 080A00, 080400, 080600) are read
# into a buffer at 063800, moved toward the target sets a step, and written back, a part a frame; a caller that goes on
# calling copy_palette_sets fades in (the opening: attract frames 40-250 go from dark to the city)
# step 0: the fade starts: its mask and step (00965C "move.w D6, $63418.l"), the target sets (009662 "move.l A5,
# $63414.l"), the buffer (009668 "lea $63800.l, A5")
009642  func  fade_palettes_start
# step 1: a part of the palette RAM into the buffer (0096A2 "move.b ($1,A6), (A5)+", 0096A6 "move.b ($3,A6), (A5)+"), the
# next step after the fifth (0096BE "cmpi.b #$5, $63412.l", 0096CA "addq.b #1, $63411.l")
009678  func  fade_palettes_read
# step 2: a part's colours in the buffer a step toward the target set's (009778 "move.b $63419.l, D0": the step, 009782
# "movea.l $63414.l, A5")
009770  func  fade_palettes_step
# a colour's red, green or blue (009872's masks: 1F, 3E0, 7C00) moved toward the target's by the step, no further
009838  func  fade_colour_part
# step 3: the buffer back into a part of the palette RAM (009714 "move.b (A5)+, ($1,A6)", 009718 "move.b (A5)+, ($3,A6)");
# after the fifth the fade's step is over (00973C "clr.l $63410.l")
0096EA  func  fade_palettes_write
# the part's palette RAM address (009744 "lea $975c.l, A6", 00974C "movea.l (A6,D7.w), A6"), 128 colours (009750 "move.w
# #$7f, D7"), the buffer's place (009754 "movea.l $6341a.l, A5")
009744  func  fade_palettes_part
comment fade_palettes_start a fade toward a set of palettes starts
comment fade_palettes_read a part of the palettes read into the fade's buffer
comment fade_palettes_step a part of the fade's colours a step toward the target palettes
comment fade_colour_part a colour's red, green or blue a step toward the target's
comment fade_palettes_write the fade's colours written back into a part of the palettes
comment fade_palettes_part where a part of the palettes is and how many colours it has

# ---- the palette effects (060480: 10-byte slots, the low 2 bits of the first byte the bank) ----
# an effect slot cleared (009A2C "clr.w ($0,A6)", 009A30 "clr.l ($2,A6)", 009A34 "clr.l ($6,A6)")
009A2C  func  clear_palette_effect
# every slot cleared (009A3E "lea $60480.l, A6", 009A46 "move.b #$40, D7": 65 of them, one more than step_palette_effects
# walks, so it also clears 060700-060709)
009A3A  func  clear_palette_effects
# unreached_0099B4's four by the effect's bank (0099D4 "andi.b #$3, D5", 0099C4 "lea $99f0.l, A5"): the effect stopped when
# D7 has its bank's bit (009A00 "btst #$0, D7" bank 0, the fixed layer's; 009A0E "btst #$1, D7" layer A's; 009A18 "btst
# #$2, D7" layer B's; 009A22 "btst #$3, D7" the sprites')
009A00  func  stop_fixed_layer_effect
009A0E  func  stop_layer_a_effect
009A18  func  stop_layer_b_effect
009A22  func  stop_sprite_effect
# the game step's palette set (009A5C "lea $9a7c.l, A6", 009A64 "move.b $60003.l, D7", 009A70 "move.w #$20f, D7": kind 2,
# all banks): the attract mode's screens
009A5C  func  request_game_step_palettes
# the game step's palette effects (009AA8 "lea $9ad2.l, A0", 009AB0 "move.b $60003.l, D7")
009AA8  func  start_game_step_palette_effects
